\section{\texorpdfstring{First- and Second-Order Differential Operators, Linearised 5D Einstein KK Cohomology, O'Neill Warped Submersions, and Equivariant Ricci Parity on $\mathcal{M}_{3,2}$ \catD}{First- and Second-Order Differential Operators, Linearised 5D Einstein KK Cohomology, O'Neill Warped Submersions, and Equivariant Ricci Parity on M\_\{3,2\}}}
\label{sec:c2_differential_operators}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{The Coordinate-Shift Operator ($\mathcal{L}_\xi \mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}}$), the $5\times 5$ Momentum-Independent Stueckelberg Jacobian, and Linearised 5D Einstein KK / Marsden--Weinstein Cohomology (\texttt{THM~\#08, COR~\#041--\#046})}{The Coordinate-Shift Operator, the 5x5 Stueckelberg Jacobian, and Linearised 5D Einstein KK / Marsden-Weinstein Cohomology}}

\begin{definition}[The Coordinate-Shift Operator $\mathcal{L}_\xi$ \catD]\label{def:c2_coord_shift}
Under an infinitesimal coordinate transformation $x^A \mapsto x^A + \xi^A(x)$ generated by a smooth vector field $\xi = \xi^A \partial_A$ on $\mathcal{M}_{3,2}$, the first-order variation of the projected metric tensor $\mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}} = [g_{AB}^{\mathrm{proj}}]$ is defined by the Coordinate-Shift (Lie derivative) Operator $\mathcal{L}_\xi$:
\begin{equation}\label{eq:c2_coord_shift_def}
\left(\mathcal{L}_\xi \mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}}\right)_{AB} \equiv \xi^C \partial_C g_{AB}^{\mathrm{proj}} + g_{CB}^{\mathrm{proj}} \partial_A \xi^C + g_{AC}^{\mathrm{proj}} \partial_B \xi^C.
\end{equation}
\end{definition}

\begin{theorem}[Block-Decomposition of $\mathcal{L}_\xi\mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}}$, Zero-Mode Translation Symmetry, Momentum-Independent $5\times 5$ Stueckelberg Jacobian $|\det\mathbf{M}_{\mathrm{St\ddot{u}ck}}^{(n)}| = 2\Phi_0^2(n/L_0)^5$, Linearised 5D Einstein KK Cohomology (Off-Shell $0$, On-Shell $5$ TT), and Zero-Mode $\mathrm{DoF} = 3$ --- \texttt{THM~\#08, COR~\#041--\#046} \catD]\label{thm:c2_coord_shift_nilpotent}
Evaluating $\mathcal{L}_\xi \mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}}$ on the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-projected metric $\mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}}$ ($g_{\mu 2}^{\mathrm{proj}} = g_{2\mu}^{\mathrm{proj}} \equiv 0$, $g_{22}^{\mathrm{proj}} = -\Phi^2$) of Theorem~\ref{thm:c1_rz2_projection} and constructing the linearised 5D Einstein operator on $\mathcal{M}_4 \times [0, \pi L_0]$ yields four exact identities:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
  \item \textbf{Exact Reduction of Pure Fiber Shifts ($\xi_{(2)} = \partial_2$) on the $n = 0$ Ground Subspace (\texttt{COR~\#041}):}
  For a uniform shift $\xi_{(2)}^A = \delta^A_2$ along $x^2$, $\left(\mathcal{L}_{\partial_2} \mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}}\right)_{AB} = \partial_2 g_{AB}^{\mathrm{proj}}$; hence on the $n = 0$ fiber ground-state subspace $\mathrm{Ker}(\partial_2)$ ($\partial_2 \mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}} = 0$), the Lie derivative vanishes identically: $\mathcal{L}_{\partial_2} \mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}} \equiv \mathbf{0}_5$ \catD.
  \item \textbf{Exact Block-Decomposition of General Non-Diagonal Coordinate Shifts ($\xi^A = (\xi^\mu, \xi^2)$, \texttt{COR~\#042--\#044}):}
  \begin{align}
  \left(\mathcal{L}_\xi \mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}}\right)_{\mu\nu} &= \xi^\alpha \partial_\alpha g_{\mu\nu} + \xi^2 \partial_2 g_{\mu\nu} + g_{\alpha\nu}\partial_\mu \xi^\alpha + g_{\mu\alpha}\partial_\nu \xi^\alpha \nonumber \\[2pt]
  &= \xi^2\partial_2 g_{\mu\nu} + {}^{(4)}\nabla_\mu\xi_\nu + {}^{(4)}\nabla_\nu\xi_\mu \qquad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#042})} \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c2_shift_munu} \\[3pt]
  \left(\mathcal{L}_\xi \mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}}\right)_{\mu 2} &= g_{\mu\alpha}\,\partial_2 \xi^\alpha - \Phi^2\,\partial_\mu \xi^2 \qquad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#043})} \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c2_shift_mu2} \\[3pt]
  \left(\mathcal{L}_\xi \mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}}\right)_{22} &= -2\Phi\left(\xi^\alpha\partial_\alpha\Phi + \xi^2\partial_2\Phi + \Phi\,\partial_2\xi^2\right) \qquad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#044})} \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c2_shift_22}
  \end{align}
  Preserving $g_{\mu 2}^{\mathrm{proj}} = 0$ under an $x^2$-dependent shift locks $\partial_2\xi^\alpha$ to $\partial_\mu\xi^2$ via the linear constraint $\mathcal{C}_\mu[\xi] \equiv g_{\mu\alpha}\partial_2\xi^\alpha - \Phi^2\partial_\mu\xi^2 = 0$ \catD.
  \item \textbf{Momentum-Independent $5\times 5$ Stueckelberg Gauge-Fixing Jacobian (\texttt{COR~\#045}):}
  Expanding perturbations around the flat orbifold background $\bm{\eta}_5 = \mathrm{diag}(-1, -\Phi_0^2, 1, 1, 1)$ in $\mathbb{Z}_2$-equivariant Fourier--Kaluza--Klein modes ($h_{\mu\nu}^{(n)}, h_{22}^{(n)} \propto \cos(q x^2)e^{i(\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{x} - \omega x^1)}$ and $h_{\mu 2}^{(n)} \propto \sin(q x^2)e^{i(\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{x} - \omega x^1)}$ with $q \equiv m_n = n/L_0 > 0$ for $n \ge 1$), the $5\times 5$ Stueckelberg block $\mathbf{M}_{\mathrm{St\ddot{u}ck}}^{(n)}$ of the linearised gauge operator $\mathbf{P}^{(n)}$ mapping the 5 contravariant diffeomorphism parameters $(\xi^{\mu(n)}, \xi^{2(n)})$ into the 5 fiber metric fluctuations $(h_{\mu 2}^{(n)}, h_{22}^{(n)})$ is upper-triangular with non-vanishing, 4-momentum-independent determinant:
  \begin{align}
  \mathbf{M}_{\mathrm{St\ddot{u}ck}}^{(n)} &= \begin{pmatrix}
  -q\,\eta_{\mu\nu} & -i\,k_\mu\Phi_0^2 \\
  \mathbf{0}_{1\times 4} & -2\,q\,\Phi_0^2
  \end{pmatrix} \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \Longrightarrow \quad \left|\det\mathbf{M}_{\mathrm{St\ddot{u}ck}}^{(n)}\right| &= 2\,\Phi_0^2\,q^5 = 2\,\Phi_0^2\left(\frac{n}{L_0}\right)^5 \neq 0, \qquad
  \frac{\partial}{\partial k_\mu}\det\mathbf{M}_{\mathrm{St\ddot{u}ck}}^{(n)} \equiv 0 \qquad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#045})} \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c2_stueckelberg_jacobian}
  \end{align}
  \item \textbf{Linearised 5D Einstein Operator Cohomology for $n \ge 1$ (Off-Shell $0$, On-Shell $5$ TT) and Zero-Mode $\mathrm{DoF} = 3$ (\texttt{COR~\#046}):}
  Let $\mathbf{E}^{(n)}: \mathbb{C}^{15} \to \mathbb{C}^{15}$ denote the linearised 5D Einstein operator $\delta G_{AB}^{(n)}$, $\mathbf{P}^{(n)}: \mathbb{C}^5 \to \mathbb{C}^{15}$ the diffeomorphism gauge map $(\mathcal{L}_\xi\bm{\eta}_5)_{AB}^{(n)}$, and $\mathbf{D}_v^{(n)}: \mathbb{C}^{15} \to \mathbb{C}^5$ the contracted Bianchi divergence map $\eta^{AC}\partial_C T_{AB}^{(n)}$ at level $n \ge 1$, with 5D dispersion polynomial $p^2 \equiv -\omega^2 + |\mathbf{k}|^2 - q^2/\Phi_0^2$. Then $\mathbf{E}^{(n)}$, $\mathbf{P}^{(n)}$, and $\mathbf{D}_v^{(n)}$ form an exact gauge complex satisfying:
  \begin{align}
  \mathbf{E}^{(n)}\,\mathbf{P}^{(n)} &\equiv \mathbf{0}_{15\times 5}, \qquad
  \mathbf{D}_v^{(n)}\,\mathbf{E}^{(n)} \equiv \mathbf{0}_{5\times 15}, \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \det\mathbf{E}_{U,\mu\nu}^{(n)} &= \frac{3}{1024}\left(\frac{q^2}{\Phi_0^2}\right)^5\left(p^2\right)^5, \qquad
  p^2 = 0 \iff \omega^2 = |\mathbf{k}|^2 - \frac{q^2}{\Phi_0^2} \;\;\catD, \nonumber \\[4pt]
  \dim\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{off\text{-}shell}}^{(n)} &\equiv \dim\ker\mathbf{E}^{(n)} - \mathrm{rank}\,\mathbf{P}^{(n)}\Big|_{p^2 \neq 0} = 5 - 5 = 0 \;\;\catD, \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \dim\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{on\text{-}shell}}^{(n)} &\equiv \dim\ker\mathbf{E}^{(n)} - \mathrm{rank}\,\mathbf{P}^{(n)}\Big|_{p^2 = 0} = 10 - 5 = 5 \;\;\textnormal{(5 TT states in $h_{\mu\nu}^{(n)}$)} \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c2_q_nilpotent_matrix}
  \end{align}
  Because $|\det\mathbf{M}_{\mathrm{St\ddot{u}ck}}^{(n)}| = 2\Phi_0^2 q^5 \neq 0$, the 5 fiber components $(h_{\mu 2}^{(n)}, h_{22}^{(n)})$ are pure Stueckelberg gauge modes eliminated algebraically in unitary gauge, leaving 0 off-shell states and 5 transverse-traceless ($\eta^{\mu\nu}h_{\mu\nu}^{(n)} = 0$, $k^\mu h_{\mu\nu}^{(n)} = 0$) states on the shell $p^2 = 0$. At the ground level $n = 0$ ($\mathbf{E}_0: \mathbb{C}^{11} \to \mathbb{C}^{11}$, $\mathbf{P}_0: \mathbb{C}^4 \to \mathbb{C}^{11}$, $\mathbf{E}_0\mathbf{P}_0 = \mathbf{0}_{11\times 4}$), on the null cone $\omega^2 = |\mathbf{k}|^2$ the physical cohomology has dimension $\dim\ker\mathbf{E}_0 - \mathrm{rank}\,\mathbf{P}_0 = 7 - 4 = 3$, matching the Dirac--Marsden--Weinstein symplectic reduction $(2 N_{\mathrm{even}} - 4 d_{\mathrm{macro}})/2 = (22 - 16)/2 = 2\text{ (graviton)} + 1\text{ (fiber scalar } \psi) = 3$ (\texttt{COR~\#046}) \catD.
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}

\begin{remark}[Identification with Recognized Operators]\label{rem:c2_step1_recognition}
$\mathcal{L}_\xi\mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}}$ is the \emph{Lie derivative} (with $\mathcal{L}_{\partial_2}\mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}} = 0$ on $\mathrm{Ker}(\partial_2)$ being \emph{Killing's equation} / the \emph{Kaluza cylinder projection}), $\mathbf{M}_{\mathrm{St\ddot{u}ck}}^{(n)}$ is the \emph{Stueckelberg gauge-fixing Jacobian}, and the gauge complex $\mathbb{C}^5 \xrightarrow{\mathbf{P}^{(n)}} \mathbb{C}^{15} \xrightarrow{\mathbf{E}^{(n)}} \mathbb{C}^{15} \xrightarrow{\mathbf{D}_v^{(n)}} \mathbb{C}^5$ ($\mathbf{E}^{(n)}\mathbf{P}^{(n)} = \mathbf{0}$, $\mathbf{D}_v^{(n)}\mathbf{E}^{(n)} = \mathbf{0}$) establishes that for every $n \ge 1$ the fiber-shift sector $(h_{\mu 2}^{(n)}, h_{22}^{(n)})$ is pure gauge, leaving 0 off-shell modes and 5 transverse-traceless modes in $h_{\mu\nu}^{(n)}$ on $p^2 = 0$, while at $n = 0$ the physical configuration space has dimension $7 - 4 = 3$.
\end{remark}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{The Parallel-Transport Operator ($\Gamma^C_{AB}$), O'Neill Warped Submersion Tensors ($\mathcal{A}_E F \equiv 0$, $\mathcal{T}_U U = +\nabla^{\mathcal{H}}\psi$), and Painlev\'e--Gullstrand Connection (\texttt{THM~\#09, COR~\#047--\#049} \& \texttt{THM~\#10, COR~\#053})}{The Parallel-Transport Operator, O'Neill Warped Submersion Tensors, and Painleve-Gullstrand Connection}}

\begin{definition}[The Parallel-Transport Operator $\Gamma^C_{AB}$, Covariant Derivative $\nabla_A$, and O'Neill Submersion Tensors $(\mathcal{A}, \mathcal{T})$ \catD]\label{def:c2_parallel_transport}
On $\mathcal{M}_{3,2}$, the unique torsion-free, metric-compatible Parallel-Transport (Levi-Civita) Operator $\Gamma^C_{AB} = \Gamma^C_{BA}$ and covariant derivative $\nabla_A$ are defined by:
\begin{align}
\Gamma^C_{AB} &\equiv \frac{1}{2}\,g^{CD}_{\mathrm{proj}}\!\left(\partial_A g_{DB}^{\mathrm{proj}} + \partial_B g_{AD}^{\mathrm{proj}} - \partial_D g_{AB}^{\mathrm{proj}}\right), \nonumber \\[3pt]
\nabla_A V^C &\equiv \partial_A V^C + \Gamma^C_{AB}V^B, \qquad
\nabla_A W_B \equiv \partial_A W_B - \Gamma^C_{AB}W_C. \label{eq:c2_christoffel_def}
\end{align}
For the warped bundle projection $\pi: \mathcal{M}_{3,2} \to \mathcal{M}_4$ with vertical fiber subbundle $\mathcal{V} \equiv \ker(d\pi) = \mathrm{span}\{\partial_2\}$ and orthogonal horizontal subbundle $\mathcal{H} \equiv \mathcal{V}^\perp = \mathrm{span}\{\partial_\mu\}$, O'Neill's fundamental submersion tensors \cite{ONeill1983} for arbitrary vector fields $E, F \in \Gamma(T\mathcal{M}_{3,2})$ are:
\begin{align}
\mathcal{A}_E F &\equiv \mathcal{H}\nabla_{\mathcal{H}E}\left(\mathcal{V}F\right) + \mathcal{V}\nabla_{\mathcal{H}E}\left(\mathcal{H}F\right), \nonumber \\[3pt]
\mathcal{T}_E F &\equiv \mathcal{H}\nabla_{\mathcal{V}E}\left(\mathcal{V}F\right) + \mathcal{V}\nabla_{\mathcal{V}E}\left(\mathcal{H}F\right). \label{eq:c2_oneill_tensors_def}
\end{align}
\end{definition}

\begin{theorem}[Exact Block-Decomposition of $\Gamma^C_{AB}$, O'Neill Umbilic Fiber Identities, and the 12 Painlev\'e--Gullstrand Connection Components --- \texttt{THM~\#09, COR~\#047--\#049} \& \texttt{THM~\#10, COR~\#053} \catD]\label{thm:c2_parallel_transport_blocks}
For any $x^2$-independent $\mathbb{Z}_2$-projected metric $\mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}}$ ($\partial_2 \mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}} = 0$, $g_{\mu 2}^{\mathrm{proj}} \equiv 0$, $g_{22}^{\mathrm{proj}} = -\Phi^2(x^\alpha)$, $g^{22}_{\mathrm{proj}} = -\Phi^{-2}(x^\alpha)$, $\psi \equiv \ln(\Phi/\Phi_\infty)$), the $5 \times 15 = 75$ independent components of $\Gamma^C_{AB}$ and O'Neill's submersion tensors satisfy:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
  \item \textbf{Exact Block-Decomposition of $\Gamma^C_{AB}$ on $\mathcal{M}_{3,2}$ (\texttt{COR~\#047--\#049}):}
  \begin{align}
  \Gamma^\lambda_{\mu\nu} &= \frac{1}{2}\,g^{\lambda\sigma}\!\left(\partial_\mu g_{\sigma\nu} + \partial_\nu g_{\mu\sigma} - \partial_\sigma g_{\mu\nu}\right) \equiv {}^{(4)}\Gamma^\lambda_{\mu\nu} \qquad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#047})} \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c2_gamma_macro} \\[3pt]
  \Gamma^2_{\mu\nu} &\equiv 0, \qquad
  \Gamma^\lambda_{2\mu} = \Gamma^\lambda_{\mu 2} \equiv 0, \qquad
  \Gamma^2_{22} \equiv 0 \qquad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#047})} \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c2_gamma_zeros} \\[3pt]
  \Gamma^2_{2\mu} &= \Gamma^2_{\mu 2} = \frac{1}{\Phi}\,\partial_\mu\Phi = \partial_\mu \ln\Phi = \partial_\mu\psi \qquad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#048})} \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c2_gamma_22mu} \\[3pt]
  \Gamma^\mu_{22} &= +\Phi\,g^{\mu\nu}\,\partial_\nu\Phi = \frac{1}{2}\,g^{\mu\nu}\,\partial_\nu\!\left(\Phi^2\right) = \Phi^2\,g^{\mu\nu}\partial_\nu\psi \qquad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#048})} \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c2_gamma_mu22} \\[3pt]
  \Gamma^B_{\mu B} &= {}^{(4)}\Gamma^\alpha_{\mu\alpha} + \Gamma^2_{\mu 2} = \partial_\mu\ln\!\left(\Phi\sqrt{-g_4}\right), \qquad
  \Gamma^B_{2B} \equiv 0 \qquad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#049})} \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c2_gamma_trace}
  \end{align}
  \item \textbf{O'Neill Integrability Tensor $\mathcal{A} \equiv 0$ and Totally Umbilic Fiber Extrinsic Curvature $\mathcal{T}_U U = +\nabla^{\mathcal{H}}\psi$ (\texttt{COR~\#047--\#048}):}
  Because $g_{\mu 2}^{\mathrm{proj}} \equiv 0$ implies $\Gamma^2_{\mu\nu} \equiv 0$ and $\Gamma^\lambda_{2\mu} \equiv 0$ in Eq.~\eqref{eq:c2_gamma_zeros}, the horizontal distribution $\mathcal{H}$ is Frobenius-integrable and O'Neill's integrability obstruction tensor vanishes identically ($\mathcal{A}_E F \equiv 0$). For the unit vertical tangent vector $U \equiv \Phi^{-1}\partial_2 \in \Gamma(\mathcal{V})$ ($g_{\mathrm{proj}}(U, U) = -1$) and any horizontal vector $X = X^\mu\partial_\mu \in \Gamma(\mathcal{H})$, O'Neill's second fundamental form tensor evaluates to:
  \begin{align}
  \mathcal{A}_E F &\equiv 0, \qquad
  \mathcal{T}_U X = \left(X^\mu\partial_\mu\psi\right) U, \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \mathcal{T}_U U &= \Phi^{-2}\,\Gamma^\mu_{22}\,\partial_\mu = g^{\mu\nu}\!\left(\partial_\nu\psi\right)\partial_\mu = +\nabla^{\mathcal{H}}\psi \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c2_oneill_umbilic}
  \end{align}
  \item \textbf{Explicit 12 Non-Vanishing Connection Components on Spherical Painlev\'e--Gullstrand Flow Charts (\texttt{COR~\#053}):}
  On the equatorial ($\theta = \pi/2$) spherical Painlev\'e--Gullstrand chart $ds_5^2 = -(1 - r_s/r)(dx^1)^2 + 2\sqrt{r_s/r}\,dx^1 dr + dr^2 + r^2 d\phi^2 - \Phi(r)^2(dx^2)^2$ of Theorem~\ref{thm:c1_det_inverse_metric}(ii), the inverse $(1,r)$ block is $g^{11} = -1$, $g^{1r} = +\sqrt{r_s/r}$, $g^{rr} = 1 - r_s/r$, and the 12 non-vanishing Christoffel symbols are:
  \begin{align}
  \Gamma^1_{11} &= +\frac{r_s}{2r^2}\sqrt{\frac{r_s}{r}}, &
  \Gamma^1_{1r} &= +\frac{r_s}{2r^2}, &
  \Gamma^1_{rr} &= +\frac{1}{2r}\sqrt{\frac{r_s}{r}}, &
  \Gamma^1_{\phi\phi} &= -r\sqrt{\frac{r_s}{r}}, \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \Gamma^1_{22} &= +\sqrt{\frac{r_s}{r}}\,\Phi\Phi', &
  \Gamma^2_{2r} &= \frac{\Phi'}{\Phi} = \psi', &
  \Gamma^r_{11} &= +\frac{r_s(r - r_s)}{2r^3}, &
  \Gamma^r_{1r} &= -\frac{r_s}{2r^2}\sqrt{\frac{r_s}{r}}, \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \Gamma^r_{rr} &= -\frac{r_s}{2r^2}, &
  \Gamma^r_{\phi\phi} &= -(r - r_s), &
  \Gamma^r_{22} &= +\left(1 - \frac{r_s}{r}\right)\Phi\Phi', &
  \Gamma^\phi_{r\phi} &= \frac{1}{r} \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c2_pg_12_christoffels}
  \end{align}
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Evaluating Eq.~\eqref{eq:c2_christoffel_def} with $g_{\mu 2}^{\mathrm{proj}} = g^{\mu 2}_{\mathrm{proj}} = 0$, $g_{22}^{\mathrm{proj}} = -\Phi^2$, $g^{22}_{\mathrm{proj}} = -\Phi^{-2}$, and $\partial_2 g_{AB}^{\mathrm{proj}} = 0$ directly yields Eqs.~\eqref{eq:c2_gamma_macro}--\eqref{eq:c2_gamma_trace}. Substituting Eqs.~\eqref{eq:c2_gamma_zeros}--\eqref{eq:c2_gamma_mu22} into Eq.~\eqref{eq:c2_oneill_tensors_def} with $U = \Phi^{-1}\partial_2$ gives $\nabla_U U = \Phi^{-1}\partial_2(\Phi^{-1})\partial_2 + \Phi^{-2}\Gamma^\mu_{22}\partial_\mu = g^{\mu\nu}(\partial_\nu\psi)\partial_\mu \in \Gamma(\mathcal{H})$, proving Eq.~\eqref{eq:c2_oneill_umbilic}. Evaluating Eq.~\eqref{eq:c2_christoffel_def} on the Painlev\'e--Gullstrand metric yields Eq.~\eqref{eq:c2_pg_12_christoffels}.
\end{proof}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Equivariant Parity Invariance of the Ricci Operator ($\mathrm{Ric}(\mathcal{R}^* g) = \mathcal{R}^*\mathrm{Ric}(g)$), the Total Bending Operator ($R^{(5)}$), and $G^2_2 \equiv -\frac{1}{2}R^{(4)}$ (\texttt{THM~\#09, COR~\#050--\#052})}{Equivariant Parity Invariance of the Ricci Operator, the Total Bending Operator, and G\^2\_2 = -(1/2)R\^(4)}}

\begin{definition}[The Curvature Tensor $R^C_{\;DAB}$, Bending Tensor $R_{DB}^{(5)}$, and Total Bending Scalar $R^{(5)}$ \catD]\label{def:c2_total_bending}
The failure of parallel transport to commute around an infinitesimal closed loop $[\nabla_A, \nabla_B]V^C = R^C_{\;DAB}V^D$ defines the rank-4 Curvature Tensor $R^C_{\;DAB}$, the contracted rank-2 Bending (Ricci) Tensor $R_{DB}^{(5)} \equiv R^A_{\;DAB}$, the Einstein Tensor $G_{AB}^{(5)} \equiv R_{AB}^{(5)} - \frac{1}{2}g_{AB}^{\mathrm{proj}}R^{(5)}$, and the Total Bending Scalar $R^{(5)} \equiv g^{DB}_{\mathrm{proj}}R_{DB}^{(5)}$:
\begin{align}
R^C_{\;DAB} &\equiv \partial_A \Gamma^C_{DB} - \partial_B \Gamma^C_{DA} + \Gamma^C_{AE}\Gamma^E_{DB} - \Gamma^C_{BE}\Gamma^E_{DA}, \label{eq:c2_riemann_def} \\[3pt]
R_{DB}^{(5)} &\equiv \partial_A \Gamma^A_{DB} - \partial_D \Gamma^A_{AB} + \Gamma^A_{AE}\Gamma^E_{DB} - \Gamma^A_{DE}\Gamma^E_{AB}, \label{eq:c2_ricci_tensor_def} \\[3pt]
R^{(5)} &\equiv g^{\mu\nu}R_{\mu\nu}^{(5)} - \frac{1}{\Phi^2}\,R_{22}^{(5)}. \label{eq:c2_ricci_scalar_def}
\end{align}
\end{definition}

\begin{theorem}[All-Orders Equivariant Parity Invariance of the Ricci and Einstein Operators, Exact Block-Decomposition of $R_{AB}^{(5)}$, and the Gauss--Codazzi Identities $R^{(5)} = R^{(4)} - \frac{2}{\Phi}\Delta_4\Phi$ ($c_{\mathrm{GC}}=-2$) and $G^2_2 \equiv -\frac{1}{2}R^{(4)}$ --- \texttt{THM~\#09, COR~\#050--\#052} \catD]\label{thm:c2_total_bending_reduction}
On the orbifold bundle $\mathcal{M}_{3,2}$ equipped with the isometric involution $\mathcal{P}_{t_2}: (x^\mu, x^2) \mapsto (x^\mu, -x^2)$ ($\mathbf{R}_{\mathbb{Z}_2} = \mathrm{diag}(+1,-1,+1,+1,+1)$), the Riemann, Ricci, and Einstein curvature operators satisfy five exact identities:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
  \item \textbf{Exact All-Orders Equivariant Parity Invariance of the Non-Linear Ricci and Einstein Operators ($R_{\mu 2}^{(5)}(g) \equiv 0$ and $G_{\mu 2}^{(5)}(g) \equiv 0$ on $\Gamma^\infty_+(\mathrm{Sym}^2 T^*\mathcal{M}_{3,2})$, \texttt{COR~\#050--\#051}):}
  By diffeomorphism naturality of the Levi-Civita connection and Ricci curvature operator, $\mathrm{Ric}(\mathcal{P}_{t_2}^* g) = \mathcal{P}_{t_2}^*\mathrm{Ric}(g)$ for every smooth metric $g$ (including metrics with arbitrary $x^2$-dependent even fluctuations $g_{AB}(x^\mu, -x^2) = \epsilon_A\epsilon_B g_{AB}(x^\mu, x^2)$). Therefore, for every $\mathbb{Z}_2$-even metric $g \in \Gamma^\infty_+(\mathrm{Sym}^2 T^*\mathcal{M}_{3,2})$ ($\mathcal{P}_{t_2}^* g = g$, so $g_{\mu 2}(x^\mu, x^2)$ is odd in $x^2$ and vanishes identically on the zero-mode section), all 256 horizontal components $R^{(5)}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta} = R^{(4)}_{\mu\nu\alpha\beta}$, all 64 odd components $R^{(5)}_{\mu\nu\alpha 2} \equiv 0$, and all 16 tidal components $R^{(5)}_{\mu 2\nu 2} = \Phi\,{}^{(4)}\nabla_\nu\partial_\mu\Phi$ (\texttt{COR~\#050}) satisfy $\mathcal{P}_{t_2}^*\mathrm{Ric}(g) = \mathrm{Ric}(g)$ and $\mathcal{P}_{t_2}^*\mathbf{G}^{(5)}(g) = \mathbf{G}^{(5)}(g)$, which in matrix form reads:
  \begin{align}
  \mathbf{R}_{\mathbb{Z}_2}\,\mathrm{Ric}(\mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}})\,\mathbf{R}_{\mathbb{Z}_2} &= \mathrm{Ric}(\mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}}) \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \Longrightarrow \quad R_{\mu 2}^{(5)}(\mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}}) &\equiv 0, \qquad
  G_{\mu 2}^{(5)}(\mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}}) \equiv 0 \qquad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#051})} \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c2_equivariant_ricci_parity}
  \end{align}
  Thus the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-even subbundle $\Gamma^\infty_+(\mathrm{Sym}^2 T^*\mathcal{M}_{3,2})$ is an exact invariant manifold of the non-linear Einstein differential operator at all dynamical orders.
  \item \textbf{Macroscopic and Fiber Ricci Tensor Blocks ($R_{\mu\nu}^{(5)}$ and $R_{22}^{(5)}$, \texttt{COR~\#051}):}
  For any $x^2$-independent metric ($\partial_2 \mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}} = 0$), substituting the Parallel-Transport blocks of Theorem~\ref{thm:c2_parallel_transport_blocks} into Definition~\ref{def:c2_total_bending} yields:
  \begin{align}
  R_{\mu\nu}^{(5)} &= R_{\mu\nu}^{(4)} - \frac{1}{\Phi}\,{}^{(4)}\nabla_\mu\!\left(\partial_\nu\Phi\right) = R_{\mu\nu}^{(4)} - {}^{(4)}\nabla_\mu\partial_\nu\psi - \left(\partial_\mu\psi\right)\!\left(\partial_\nu\psi\right) \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c2_ricci_munu} \\[4pt]
  R_{22}^{(5)} &= \Phi\,\Delta_4\Phi = \Phi^2\left[\Delta_4\psi + g^{\mu\nu}\!\left(\partial_\mu\psi\right)\!\left(\partial_\nu\psi\right)\right], \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \Delta_4\Phi &\equiv \frac{1}{\sqrt{-g_4}}\,\partial_\mu\!\left(\sqrt{-g_4}\,g^{\mu\nu}\partial_\nu\Phi\right) \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c2_ricci_22}
  \end{align}
  \item \textbf{Gauss--Codazzi Curvature Scalar Reduction ($R^{(5)} = R^{(4)} - \frac{2}{\Phi}\Delta_4\Phi$, $c_{\mathrm{GC}} = -2$, \texttt{COR~\#052}):}
  \begin{align}
  R^{(5)} &= R^{(4)} + \frac{c_{\mathrm{GC}}}{\Phi}\,\Delta_4\Phi = R^{(4)} - \frac{2}{\Phi}\,\Delta_4\Phi = R^{(4)} - 2\,\Delta_4\psi - 2\,g^{\mu\nu}\!\left(\partial_\mu\psi\right)\!\left(\partial_\nu\psi\right) \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c2_r5_reduction} \\[3pt]
  R^{(5)}\sqrt{|g_5|} &= \Phi\,R^{(4)}\sqrt{-g_4} - 2\,\partial_\mu\!\left(\sqrt{-g_4}\,g^{\mu\nu}\partial_\nu\Phi\right) \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c2_r5_volume_density}
  \end{align}
  \item \textbf{Exact Mixed $(2,2)$ Einstein Tensor Reduction Identity ($G^2_2 \equiv -\frac{1}{2}R^{(4)}$, \texttt{COR~\#052}):}
  Raising the first index of $R_{22}^{(5)}$ with $g^{22}_{\mathrm{proj}} = -\Phi^{-2}$ gives $R^2_2 = -\frac{1}{\Phi}\Delta_4\Phi$. Substituting Eq.~\eqref{eq:c2_r5_reduction} into $G^2_2 \equiv R^2_2 - \frac{1}{2}R^{(5)}$ cancels $-\frac{1}{\Phi}\Delta_4\Phi$ against $-\frac{1}{2}\big(-\frac{2}{\Phi}\Delta_4\Phi\big) = +\frac{1}{\Phi}\Delta_4\Phi$ identically, leaving:
  \begin{equation}\label{eq:c2_g22_mixed_identity}
  G^2_2 \equiv R^2_2 - \frac{1}{2}\,R^{(5)} = -\frac{1}{\Phi}\,\Delta_4\Phi - \frac{1}{2}\left(R^{(4)} - \frac{2}{\Phi}\,\Delta_4\Phi\right) \equiv -\frac{1}{2}\,R^{(4)} \;\;\catD,
  \end{equation}
  independent of $\Phi(x^\mu)$ and its derivatives.
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
For any diffeomorphism $\varphi: \mathcal{M}_{3,2} \to \mathcal{M}_{3,2}$, naturality of the Riemann and Ricci tensors states $\mathrm{Ric}(\varphi^* g) = \varphi^*\mathrm{Ric}(g)$. Taking $\varphi = \mathcal{P}_{t_2}$ on $\mathcal{P}_{t_2}^*\mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}} = \mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}}$ gives $(\mathcal{P}_{t_2}^*\mathrm{Ric}(\mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}}))_{\mu 2} = \epsilon_\mu\epsilon_2 R_{\mu 2}^{(5)} = -R_{\mu 2}^{(5)} = +R_{\mu 2}^{(5)}$, forcing $R_{\mu 2}^{(5)} \equiv 0$ and $G_{\mu 2}^{(5)} \equiv 0$, proving Eq.~\eqref{eq:c2_equivariant_ricci_parity}. Expanding $R_{\mu\nu}^{(5)} = R_{\mu\nu}^{(4)} - \partial_\mu\Gamma^2_{2\nu} + {}^{(4)}\Gamma^\alpha_{\mu\nu}\Gamma^2_{2\alpha} - \Gamma^2_{\mu 2}\Gamma^2_{2\nu}$ with $\Gamma^2_{2\nu} = \Phi^{-1}\partial_\nu\Phi$ cancels $+\Phi^{-2}(\partial_\mu\Phi)(\partial_\nu\Phi)$ against $-\Gamma^2_{\mu 2}\Gamma^2_{2\nu}$, proving Eq.~\eqref{eq:c2_ricci_munu}. Similarly, $R_{22}^{(5)} = \partial_\alpha\Gamma^\alpha_{22} + {}^{(4)}\Gamma^\beta_{\alpha\beta}\Gamma^\alpha_{22} - \Gamma^2_{2\alpha}\Gamma^\alpha_{22} = \Phi\Delta_4\Phi$. Contracting with $g^{\mu\nu}$ and $g^{22}_{\mathrm{proj}} = -\Phi^{-2}$ adds $-\frac{1}{\Phi}\Delta_4\Phi$ and $-\frac{1}{\Phi}\Delta_4\Phi$ to give $R^{(5)} = R^{(4)} - \frac{2}{\Phi}\Delta_4\Phi = R^{(4)} + 2 R^2_2$, from which $G^2_2 = R^2_2 - \frac{1}{2}(R^{(4)} + 2 R^2_2) \equiv -\frac{1}{2}R^{(4)}$ follows identically.
\end{proof}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{The Divergence-of-Gradient Wave Operator ($\Delta_5$), the Indicial Equation $\alpha^2 + 2\alpha + \gamma_n^2 = 0$, Liouville Normal Form ($\beta_-+\beta_+=1$), and Weyl Limit-Circle Square-Integrability (\texttt{THM~\#10, COR~\#054--\#055})}{The Divergence-of-Gradient Wave Operator, the Indicial Equation, Liouville Normal Form, and Weyl Limit-Circle Square-Integrability}}

\begin{theorem}[Exact Block-Split of $\Delta_5$, Standing-Wave Dispersion $\omega^2 = |\mathbf{k}|^2 - q^2/\Phi_0^2$, Zero-Mode Luminality, Throat Indicial Equation $\alpha^2 + 2\alpha + \gamma_n^2 = 0$, and Weyl Limit-Circle Square-Integrability --- \texttt{THM~\#10, COR~\#054--\#055} \catD]\label{thm:c2_wave_operator_properties}\label{thm:c2_laplace_reduction}
The five-dimensional Divergence-of-Gradient Wave Operator $\Delta_5\Psi \equiv \frac{1}{\sqrt{|g_5|}}\partial_A\big(\sqrt{|g_5|}\,g^{AB}_{\mathrm{proj}}\partial_B\Psi\big)$ on $\mathcal{M}_{3,2}$ satisfies four exact second-order identities:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
  \item \textbf{Exact Macroscopic-Plus-Fiber Decomposition of $\Delta_5$ (\texttt{COR~\#054}):}
  \begin{equation}\label{eq:c2_delta5_split}
  \Delta_5\Psi = \Delta_4\Psi + \frac{1}{\Phi}\,g^{\mu\nu}\!\left(\partial_\mu\Phi\right)\!\left(\partial_\nu\Psi\right) - \frac{1}{\Phi^2}\,\frac{\partial^2\Psi}{\partial(x^2)^2} \;\;\catD.
  \end{equation}
  \item \textbf{Discrete Standing-Wave Spectrum and Spectral Gap on $\mathcal{S}_{t_2} = S^1_{t_2}/\mathbb{Z}_2 = [0, \pi L_0]$:}
  With Neumann mirror boundary conditions ($\partial_2\Psi|_{0,\pi L_0} = 0$), $-\partial_2^2\cos(n x^2/L_0) = q^2\cos(n x^2/L_0)$ where $q \equiv k_{2,n} = n/L_0$ ($n \in \mathbb{N}_0$), with strictly positive wavenumber gap $k_{\mathrm{gap}} \equiv (L_0\Phi_0)^{-1} > 0$ for $n=1$.
  \item \textbf{Plane-Wave Dispersion $\omega^2 = |\mathbf{k}|^2 - q^2/\Phi_0^2$ and Exact Unit Null-Cone Speed of the $n = 0$ Ground State (\texttt{COR~\#055}):}
  On a uniform flat background ($\Phi = \Phi_0$, $g_{\mu\nu} = \eta_{\mu\nu}$), applying $\Delta_5$ to $\Psi = \cos(q x^2)e^{i(\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{x} - \omega x^1)}$ yields the dispersion relation $\omega^2 = |\mathbf{k}|^2 - q^2/\Phi_0^2$, matching identically the spin-2 Kaluza--Klein dispersion relation of Theorem~\ref{thm:c2_coord_shift_nilpotent}(iv). At $n = 0$ ($q = 0$), $\omega_0(\mathbf{k}) = |\mathbf{k}|$ and $v_{\mathrm{phase}} = v_{\mathrm{group}} = 1$ ($c_{\mathrm{GW}} = c$) \catD.
  \item \textbf{Throat Indicial Equation $\alpha^2 + 2\alpha + \gamma_n^2 = 0$, Liouville Normal Form $\beta(\beta - 1) = \frac{3}{4} - \gamma_n^2$ ($\beta_- + \beta_+ = 1$), and Weyl Limit-Circle Square-Integrability for $n \ge 1$ (\texttt{COR~\#055}):}
  On a static spherical throat chart $ds_5^2 = -(dx^1)^2 - \Phi(r)^2(dx^2)^2 + dr^2 + r^2 d\Omega_2^2$ with linear throat profile $\Phi(r) = (\Phi_\infty/r_0)\,r$ ($\sqrt{|g_5|} = \Phi(r)\,r^2\sin\theta \propto r^3\sin\theta$), applying $\Delta_5$ in Eq.~\eqref{eq:c2_delta5_split} to a static $s$-wave mode $\Psi_n(r, x^2) = r^\alpha\cos(q x^2)$ ($q = n/L_0$, $n \ge 1$) combines the radial operator $r^{-3}\partial_r(r^3\partial_r r^\alpha) = \alpha(\alpha + 2)r^{\alpha - 2}$ with the negative-signature temporal fiber term $-\Phi(r)^{-2}\partial_2^2\Psi_n = +(\gamma_n^2/r^2)\Psi_n$ ($\gamma_n \equiv q r_0/\Phi_\infty = n r_0/(L_0\Phi_\infty) > 0$), yielding the exact Frobenius indicial equation and its Liouville normal form $\Psi_n(r, x^2) = r^{-s_{\mathrm{shift}}}u_n(r)\cos(q x^2)$ ($s_{\mathrm{shift}} = \frac{3}{2}$, $u_n(r) \sim r^\beta$ with $\alpha = \beta - \frac{3}{2}$):
  \begin{align}
  \frac{\Delta_5\Psi_n}{r^{\alpha - 2}\cos(q x^2)} &= \alpha^2 + 2\alpha + \gamma_n^2 = 0, \qquad
  \gamma_n \equiv \frac{q\,r_0}{\Phi_\infty} = \frac{n\,r_0}{L_0\,\Phi_\infty} > 0 \;\;\catD, \nonumber \\[4pt]
  \beta(\beta - 1) &= W_{\mathrm{normal}} \equiv \frac{3}{4} - \gamma_n^2 \quad \Longleftrightarrow \quad
  \beta_\pm = \frac{1}{2} \pm \sqrt{1 - \gamma_n^2}, \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \beta_- + \beta_+ &\equiv 1, \qquad
  \beta_-\beta_+ = \gamma_n^2 - \frac{3}{4} \qquad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#055})} \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c2_weyl_barrier}
  \end{align}
  Square-integrability of $u_n(r) \sim r^\beta$ on $L^2((0, \delta), dr)$ (equivalently $\Psi_n \in L^2((0, \delta), r^3 dr)$ with the 5D radial measure $\sqrt{|g_5|} \propto r^3$) requires $2\operatorname{Re}(\beta) + 1 > 0$. On the real branch $0 < \gamma_n \le 1$, the smaller root $\beta_- = \frac{1}{2} - \sqrt{1 - \gamma_n^2}$ satisfies the exact algebraic identity $(2\beta_- + 1)\big(1 + \sqrt{1 - \gamma_n^2}\big) \equiv 2\gamma_n^2 > 0$, whence $2\beta_- + 1 > 0$; on the oscillatory branch $\gamma_n = \sqrt{1 + h^2} > 1$ ($h > 0$), $\beta_\pm = \frac{1}{2} \pm i h$ has $2\operatorname{Re}(\beta_\pm) + 1 = 2 > 0$. Therefore, both linearly independent Frobenius solutions are square-integrable as $r \to 0^+$ for every $n \ge 1$, placing $r = 0$ in Hermann Weyl's \emph{limit-circle} case on $L^2((0, \delta), dr)$ (where self-adjointness is specified by the reflective core boundary condition at the throat, Theorem~\ref{thm:c4_kretschmann_ghy_identities}) \catD.
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{The Antisymmetric Curl Operator ($d$) and Exact Four-Dimensional Scale Invariance ($\Omega_c^4 \Omega_c^{-2} \Omega_c^{-2} \equiv 1$, \texttt{THM~\#11, COR~\#056--\#057})}{The Antisymmetric Curl Operator and Exact Four-Dimensional Scale Invariance}}

\begin{theorem}[Connection Independence, Nilpotency $d^2 \equiv 0$, and Exact 4D Scale Invariance $\Omega_c^4 \Omega_c^{-2} \Omega_c^{-2} \equiv 1$ --- \texttt{THM~\#11, COR~\#056--\#057} \catD]\label{thm:c2_curl_invariance}\label{thm:c2_conformal_maxwell}
For any smooth covector field $A = A_\mu(x^\alpha)\,dx^\mu$ ($\mu \in \{1,3,4,5\}$) on $\mathcal{M}_4$, the Antisymmetric Curl $F_{\mu\nu} \equiv (dA)_{\mu\nu} = \partial_\mu A_\nu - \partial_\nu A_\mu$ and its quadratic density $\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{curl}}[A, g_{\mu\nu}] \equiv -\frac{1}{4}\sqrt{-g_4}\,g^{\mu\alpha}g^{\nu\beta}F_{\mu\nu}F_{\alpha\beta}$ satisfy three exact identities:
\begin{align}
\nabla_\mu A_\nu - \nabla_\nu A_\mu &= \partial_\mu A_\nu - \partial_\nu A_\mu = F_{\mu\nu} \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c2_curl_connection_indep} \\[3pt]
(d^2 A)_{\lambda\mu\nu} &\equiv \partial_\lambda F_{\mu\nu} + \partial_\mu F_{\nu\lambda} + \partial_\nu F_{\lambda\mu} \equiv 0 \qquad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#056})} \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c2_bianchi_nilpotent} \\[3pt]
\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{curl}}\!\left[A, \Omega_c^2 g_{\mu\nu}\right] &= \Omega_c^{d_{\mathrm{macro}} - 4}\,\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{curl}}\!\left[A, g_{\mu\nu}\right]\Big|_{d_{\mathrm{macro}} = 4} \nonumber \\[3pt]
&= \underbrace{\Omega_c^4\,\Omega_c^{-2}\,\Omega_c^{-2}}_{\equiv\, 1}\,\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{curl}}\!\left[A, g_{\mu\nu}\right] \equiv \mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{curl}}\!\left[A, g_{\mu\nu}\right] \qquad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#057})} \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c2_weyl_invariance}
\end{align}
so that under $g_{\mu\nu} = e^{-c\psi}\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}$ across dimensions $d_{\mathrm{macro}} \in \{2,\dots,8\}$, the Weyl exponent $\frac{c}{2}(4 - d_{\mathrm{macro}})$ vanishes and $\frac{\delta\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{curl}}}{\delta\psi} \equiv 0$ identically if and only if $d_{\mathrm{macro}} = D - 1 = 4 \iff D = 5$.
\end{theorem}

\begin{remark}[Identification of Section~2 Differential Operators with Recognized Mathematical Structures]\label{rem:c2_master_recognition}
The differential operators deduced in Section~2 correspond directly to foundational theorems of differential geometry:
\begin{enumerate}[label=\arabic*.]
  \item \textbf{Levi-Civita Connection and O'Neill's Warped Submersion Theory (Theorem~\ref{thm:c2_parallel_transport_blocks}):} Equation~\eqref{eq:c2_gamma_22mu} proves that the logarithmic gradient $\partial_\mu\psi \equiv \partial_\mu\ln\Phi = \Gamma^2_{2\mu}$ is literally the $(2,2,\mu)$ Christoffel connection component of the 5D metric. In O'Neill's geometry of semi-Riemannian submersions \cite{ONeill1983}, Eq.~\eqref{eq:c2_oneill_umbilic} proves that the horizontal distribution $\mathcal{H}$ has zero integrability tensor ($\mathcal{A}_E F \equiv 0$) and that the fibers $\mathcal{S}_{t_2}$ are totally umbilic submanifolds with normal curvature vector $\mathcal{T}_U U = +\nabla^{\mathcal{H}}\psi$.
  \item \textbf{Equivariant Ricci Invariance and Gauss--Codazzi Reductions (Theorem~\ref{thm:c2_total_bending_reduction}):} Equation~\eqref{eq:c2_equivariant_ricci_parity} proves via diffeomorphism naturality ($\mathrm{Ric}(\mathcal{P}_{t_2}^* g) = \mathcal{P}_{t_2}^*\mathrm{Ric}(g)$) that $R_{\mu 2}^{(5)} \equiv 0$ and $G_{\mu 2}^{(5)} \equiv 0$ hold identically on the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-even metric bundle at all non-linear orders. Equations~\eqref{eq:c2_r5_reduction} and~\eqref{eq:c2_g22_mixed_identity} establish the exact Gauss--Codazzi curvature identities $R^{(5)} = R^{(4)} - \frac{2}{\Phi}\Delta_4\Phi$ ($c_{\mathrm{GC}} = -2$) and $G^2_2 \equiv -\frac{1}{2}R^{(4)}$.
  \item \textbf{Laplace--Beltrami Wave Operator and Throat Indicial Equation (Theorem~\ref{thm:c2_wave_operator_properties}):} $\Delta_5$ is the five-dimensional d'Alembertian $\Box_5$, whose $n = 0$ mode propagates at unit null-cone speed $v_{\mathrm{phase}} = v_{\mathrm{group}} = 1$, and whose $n \ge 1$ standing modes on a linear throat $\Phi = (\Phi_\infty/r_0)r$ satisfy the exact indicial equation $\alpha^2 + 2\alpha + \gamma_n^2 = 0$ and Liouville normal form $\beta(\beta - 1) = \frac{3}{4} - \gamma_n^2$ ($\beta_- + \beta_+ = 1$), with both solutions square-integrable at $r \to 0$ (Weyl limit-circle case) in Eq.~\eqref{eq:c2_weyl_barrier}.
  \item \textbf{De Rham Exterior Derivative and 4D Conformal Invariance (Theorem~\ref{thm:c2_curl_invariance}):} The Antisymmetric Curl $d$ is Cartan's exterior derivative ($d^2 \equiv 0$), and $\Omega_c^{d_{\mathrm{macro}}-4} \equiv 1$ in Eq.~\eqref{eq:c2_weyl_invariance} proves that the quadratic 2-form density $\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{curl}}$ is conformally invariant uniquely in macroscopic dimension $d_{\mathrm{macro}} = 4$.
\end{enumerate}
\end{remark}

\section{\texorpdfstring{The Five-Dimensional Total Bending Integral, Constructive Curved Weyl Rescaling ($\kappa_\psi = -3/2$), 5D Geodesic First Integrals, and the Khronon-Coupled Master Action $\mathcal{I}_{3,2}$ \catD}{The Five-Dimensional Total Bending Integral, Constructive Curved Weyl Rescaling, 5D Geodesic First Integrals, and the Khronon-Coupled Master Action}}
\label{sec:c3_master_action}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{The 5D Total Bending Integral ($\mathcal{I}_5$) and Stationary Jordan-Frame Field Equations ($\delta\mathcal{I}_5 = 0$, \texttt{THM~\#12, COR~\#058})}{The 5D Total Bending Integral and Stationary Jordan-Frame Field Equations}}

\begin{theorem}[Exact Integration Over $\mathcal{S}_{t_2}$, Boundary Divergence Cancellation, and Stationary Jordan-Frame Equations --- \texttt{THM~\#12, COR~\#058} \catD]\label{thm:c3_i5_reduction}\label{thm:c3_stationary_field_equations}
On $\mathcal{M}_{3,2} = \mathcal{M}_4 \times_{\Phi} \mathcal{S}_{t_2}$ ($\mathcal{S}_{t_2} = [0, \pi L_0]$), integrating $R^{(5)}\sqrt{|g_5|}$ of Eq.~\eqref{eq:c2_r5_volume_density} defines the dimensionless Total Bending Integral:
\begin{align}
\mathcal{I}_5\!\left[\mathbf{g}_{\mathrm{proj}}\right] &\equiv \frac{1}{\pi L_0}\int_{\mathcal{M}_4} d^4x \int_{0}^{\pi L_0} dx^2\,R^{(5)}\sqrt{|g_5|} \nonumber \\[3pt]
&= \int_{\mathcal{M}_4} d^4x\,\sqrt{-g_4}\,\Phi\,R^{(4)} - 2\oint_{\partial\mathcal{M}_4} d^3\Sigma\,\sqrt{|h_3|}\,n_\mu g^{\mu\nu}\partial_\nu\Phi \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c3_i5_4d_split}
\end{align}
whose interior bulk functional $\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{bulk}}[g_{\mu\nu}, \Phi] \equiv \int_{\mathcal{M}_4} d^4x\sqrt{-g_4}\,\Phi R^{(4)}$ has first variation:
\begin{align}
\delta\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{bulk}} &= \int_{\mathcal{M}_4} d^4x\,\sqrt{-g_4}\,\Bigl\{\left[\Phi\,G_{\mu\nu}^{(4)} - \left({}^{(4)}\nabla_\mu\partial_\nu\Phi - g_{\mu\nu}\,\Delta_4\Phi\right)\right]\delta g^{\mu\nu} \nonumber \\[3pt]
&\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad + R^{(4)}\,\delta\Phi\Bigr\} \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c3_delta_ibulk}
\end{align}
Consequently, requiring $\delta\mathcal{I}_5 = 0$ (or equivalently $G_{AB}^{(5)} \equiv R_{AB}^{(5)} - \frac{1}{2}g_{AB}^{\mathrm{proj}}R^{(5)} = 0$) yields the induced-matter macroscopic equation, off-diagonal identity, fiber equation, and trace wave equation (\texttt{COR~\#058}):
\begin{align}
G_{\mu\nu}^{(4)} &= T_{\mu\nu}^{(\Phi)} \equiv \frac{1}{\Phi}\left({}^{(4)}\nabla_\mu\partial_\nu\Phi - g_{\mu\nu}\,\Delta_4\Phi\right), \qquad
G_{\mu 2}^{(5)} \equiv 0 \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c3_macro_field_eq} \\[4pt]
G_{22}^{(5)} &= \frac{1}{2}\,\Phi^2\,R^{(4)} = 0 \implies R^{(4)} = 0, \qquad
\Delta_4\Phi = \frac{1}{3}\,\Phi\,R^{(4)} = 0 \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c3_trace_phi_wave}
\end{align}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Applying the Palatini identity $\sqrt{-g_4}\,g^{\mu\nu}\delta R_{\mu\nu}^{(4)} = \sqrt{-g_4}\big({}^{(4)}\nabla_\mu{}^{(4)}\nabla_\nu - g_{\mu\nu}\Delta_4\big)\delta g^{\mu\nu}$ and integrating twice by parts onto $\Phi$ yields Eq.~\eqref{eq:c3_delta_ibulk}. Contracting Eq.~\eqref{eq:c3_macro_field_eq} with $g^{\mu\nu}$ ($g^{\mu\nu}G_{\mu\nu}^{(4)} = -R^{(4)}$, $g^{\mu\nu}g_{\mu\nu} = 4$) gives $-R^{(4)} = \frac{1-4}{\Phi}\Delta_4\Phi = -\frac{3}{\Phi}\Delta_4\Phi$, proving Eq.~\eqref{eq:c3_trace_phi_wave}.
\end{proof}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{The Canonical Metric Rescaling ($\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu} = e^{\psi}g_{\mu\nu}$) and Constructive Origin of the $\kappa_\psi = -\frac{3}{2}$ Kinetic Term (\texttt{THM~\#12, COR~\#059--\#060})}{The Canonical Metric Rescaling and Constructive Origin of the -(3/2)(nabla psi)\^2 Kinetic Term}}

\begin{definition}[Dimensionless Logarithmic Fiber Scale $\psi$ and Rescaled 4D Metric $\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}$ \catD]\label{def:c3_rescaled_metric}
Relative to a constant asymptotic reference scale $\Phi_\infty > 0$, define the dimensionless fiber ratio $\hat{\Phi}(x)$ and the dimensionless logarithmic fiber field $\psi(x)$ by:
\begin{align}
\hat{\Phi}(x) &\equiv \frac{\Phi(x)}{\Phi_\infty} = e^{\psi(x)}, \qquad
\partial_\mu\psi = \frac{\partial_\mu\Phi}{\Phi} = \Gamma^2_{2\mu}, \nonumber \\[3pt]
\psi(x) &\equiv \ln\!\left(\frac{\Phi(x)}{\Phi_\infty}\right) = -\frac{1}{2}\ln\!\left(-g^{22}_{\mathrm{proj}}\,\Phi_\infty^2\right), \label{eq:c3_psi_def}
\end{align}
where $\partial_\mu\psi = \Gamma^2_{2\mu}$ is the exact fiber Parallel-Transport component of Theorem~\ref{thm:c2_parallel_transport_blocks} and $\frac{\partial\psi}{\partial g^{22}_{\mathrm{proj}}} = -\frac{1}{2g^{22}_{\mathrm{proj}}}$ holds identically (\texttt{COR~\#058}). The rescaled four-dimensional macroscopic metric $\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}$ and its inverse $\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}$ are defined by:
\begin{align}
\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}(x) &\equiv \hat{\Phi}(x)^{c_{\mathrm{Weyl}}}\,g_{\mu\nu}(x) = e^{\psi(x)}\,g_{\mu\nu}(x) \qquad \left(c_{\mathrm{Weyl}} = 1\right), \nonumber \\[3pt]
\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}(x) &= \hat{\Phi}(x)^{-1}\,g^{\mu\nu}(x) = e^{-\psi(x)}\,g^{\mu\nu}(x). \label{eq:c3_gtilde_def}
\end{align}
\end{definition}

\begin{theorem}[Constructive Non-Diagonal Curved Weyl Transformation Under $\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu} = e^{\psi}g_{\mu\nu}$ and Emergence of $(c_{\mathrm{Weyl}}, c_{\mathrm{div}}, \kappa_\psi) = (1, 1, -3/2)$ --- \texttt{THM~\#12, COR~\#059--\#060} \catD]\label{thm:c3_einstein_frame_reduction}\label{thm:c3_weyl_reduction}
Under the metric rescaling $g_{\mu\nu} = e^{-c_{\mathrm{Weyl}}\psi}\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}$ on the curved non-diagonal four-dimensional macroscopic submanifold $\mathcal{M}_4$, requiring the multiplier of $\tilde{R}^{(4)}$ in the reduced Einstein--Hilbert density $(\Phi/\Phi_\infty)\sqrt{-g_4}\,R^{(4)}[g] = e^{(1 - \frac{d_{\mathrm{macro}}-2}{2}c_{\mathrm{Weyl}})\psi}\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4}\,\tilde{R}^{(4)} + \dots$ to be independent of $\psi$ uniquely selects $c_{\mathrm{Weyl}} = 1$, and the volume measure, parallel-transport operator, curvature scalar, and reduced bulk integral satisfy four exact identities (\texttt{COR~\#059--\#060}):
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
  \item \textbf{Exact Volume-Measure Rescaling ($\sqrt{-g_4} = e^{-2\psi}\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4}$):}
  Because $\mathcal{M}_4$ has dimension $d_{\mathrm{macro}} = 4$, the determinant $g_4 = \det(e^{-\psi}\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}) = e^{-4\psi}\tilde{g}_4$ scales with weight $-4$, so the volume measure and its product with $\hat{\Phi} = e^\psi$ transform as:
  \begin{equation}\label{eq:c3_volume_rescaling}
  \sqrt{-g_4} = e^{-2\psi}\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4} \quad \Longrightarrow \quad
  \hat{\Phi}\,\sqrt{-g_4} = e^{-\psi}\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4} \;\;\catD.
  \end{equation}

  \item \textbf{Exact Shift of All 64 Non-Diagonal 4D Parallel-Transport Components (${}^{(4)}\Gamma^\lambda_{\mu\nu}$, \texttt{COR~\#059}):}
  Substituting $g_{\mu\nu} = e^{-\psi}\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}$ and $g^{\lambda\sigma} = e^{\psi}\tilde{g}^{\lambda\sigma}$ into Eq.~\eqref{eq:c2_gamma_macro} relates ${}^{(4)}\Gamma^\lambda_{\mu\nu}$ to the rescaled parallel-transport operator $\tilde{\Gamma}^\lambda_{\mu\nu}$ via the fiber connection gradient $\partial_\mu\psi = \Gamma^2_{2\mu}$ (with contracted trace shift $\Delta\Gamma^\alpha_{\mu\alpha} = -2\partial_\mu\psi$):
  \begin{equation}\label{eq:c3_gamma_rescaling}
  {}^{(4)}\Gamma^\lambda_{\mu\nu} = \tilde{\Gamma}^\lambda_{\mu\nu} - \frac{1}{2}\left(\delta^\lambda_\mu\,\partial_\nu\psi + \delta^\lambda_\nu\,\partial_\mu\psi - \tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}\,\tilde{g}^{\lambda\sigma}\partial_\sigma\psi\right) \;\;\catD.
  \end{equation}

  \item \textbf{Exact Transformation of the 4D Curvature Scalar ($R^{(4)}$) and Origin of $\kappa_\psi = -\frac{3}{2}$ (\texttt{COR~\#059}):}
  Substituting Eq.~\eqref{eq:c3_gamma_rescaling} into $R^{(4)} = g^{\mu\nu}R_{\mu\nu}^{(4)} = e^{\psi}\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}R_{\mu\nu}^{(4)}$ yields the exact four-dimensional curvature transformation:
  \begin{align}
  R^{(4)} &= e^{\psi}\left[\tilde{R}^{(4)} + 3\,\tilde{\Delta}_4\psi - \frac{3}{2}\,\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}\!\left(\partial_\mu\psi\right)\!\left(\partial_\nu\psi\right)\right] \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c3_r4_rescaling} \\[4pt]
  \kappa_\psi \equiv -\frac{(d_{\mathrm{macro}} - 1)(d_{\mathrm{macro}} - 2)}{4}\Bigg|_{d_{\mathrm{macro}}=4} &= -\frac{(4 - 1)(4 - 2)}{4} = -\frac{3 \times 2}{4} = -\frac{3}{2} \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c3_three_halves_origin}
  \end{align}
  where $\tilde{\Delta}_4\psi \equiv \frac{1}{\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4}}\partial_\mu\!\left(\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4}\,\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}\partial_\nu\psi\right)$.

  \item \textbf{The Canonical Rescaled Bending Integral ($\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{bulk}}[\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}, \psi]$) and Decoupled First-Order Field Equations (\texttt{COR~\#059--\#060}):}
  Multiplying Eq.~\eqref{eq:c3_r4_rescaling} by $\Phi\sqrt{-g_4} = \Phi_\infty e^{-\psi}\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4}$ from Eq.~\eqref{eq:c3_volume_rescaling} cancels $e^{-\psi}e^{+\psi} = 1$ identically (while in $\Phi_\infty^{-1}\sqrt{|g_5|}R^{(5)}$, $+3\tilde{\Delta}_4\psi$ combines with $-2\tilde{\Delta}_4\psi$ from $c_{\mathrm{GC}}=-2$ to yield $c_{\mathrm{div}} = 3 - 2 = +1$), transforming the bulk functional into:
  \begin{equation}\label{eq:c3_canonical_action}
  \mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{bulk}}\!\left[\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}, \psi\right] = \Phi_\infty\int_{\mathcal{M}_4} d^4x\,\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4}\left[\tilde{R}^{(4)} - \frac{3}{2}\,\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}\!\left(\partial_\mu\psi\right)\!\left(\partial_\nu\psi\right) + 3\,\tilde{\Delta}_4\psi\right] \;\;\catD.
  \end{equation}
  Because $3\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4}\,\tilde{\Delta}_4\psi = 3\partial_\mu\!\left(\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4}\,\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}\partial_\nu\psi\right)$ is an exact boundary divergence with zero interior variation, requiring $\delta\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{bulk}} = 0$ with respect to $\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}$ and $\psi$ yields the completely decoupled first-order stationary equations:
  \begin{align}
  \tilde{G}_{\mu\nu}^{(4)} &= \tilde{T}_{\mu\nu}^{(\psi)} \equiv \frac{3}{2}\left[\left(\partial_\mu\psi\right)\!\left(\partial_\nu\psi\right) - \frac{1}{2}\,\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}\,\tilde{g}^{\alpha\beta}\!\left(\partial_\alpha\psi\right)\!\left(\partial_\beta\psi\right)\right] \;\;\catD, \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \tilde{\Delta}_4\psi &= 0 \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c3_canonical_field_eqs}
  \end{align}
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Let $\Delta\Gamma^\lambda_{\mu\nu} \equiv {}^{(4)}\Gamma^\lambda_{\mu\nu} - \tilde{\Gamma}^\lambda_{\mu\nu} = -\frac{1}{2}(\delta^\lambda_\mu\partial_\nu\psi + \delta^\lambda_\nu\partial_\mu\psi - \tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}\tilde{\nabla}^\lambda\psi)$ with trace $\Delta\Gamma^\alpha_{\mu\alpha} = -2\partial_\mu\psi$. Contracting $R_{\mu\nu}^{(4)} - \tilde{R}_{\mu\nu}^{(4)} = \tilde{\nabla}_\alpha\Delta\Gamma^\alpha_{\mu\nu} - \tilde{\nabla}_\mu\Delta\Gamma^\alpha_{\nu\alpha} + \Delta\Gamma^\alpha_{\alpha\beta}\Delta\Gamma^\beta_{\mu\nu} - \Delta\Gamma^\alpha_{\mu\beta}\Delta\Gamma^\beta_{\nu\alpha}$ with $g^{\mu\nu} = e^\psi\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}$ yields $+3\tilde{\Delta}_4\psi$ from the linear derivative terms and $(-2 + \frac{1}{2})(\tilde{\nabla}\psi)^2 = -\frac{3}{2}(\tilde{\nabla}\psi)^2$ from the quadratic connection terms, proving Eqs.~\eqref{eq:c3_r4_rescaling}--\eqref{eq:c3_canonical_field_eqs}.
\end{proof}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{The 5D Geodesic Operator ($\delta\int ds_5 = 0$), Conserved Invariant $P_2$, Null-Projection Causal Threshold ($\Phi = |P_2|/\epsilon_5$), Radial Force $a_\Omega(r) \ge 0$, Routhian Slopes $(+1/2, +3/2)$, and Rescaled Bianchi Closure (\texttt{THM~\#13, COR~\#061--\#063})}{The 5D Geodesic Operator, Conserved Invariant P\_2, Null-Projection Causal Threshold, Radial Force, Routhian Slopes, and Rescaled Bianchi Closure}}

\begin{theorem}[Exact Block-Decomposition of the 5D Geodesic Equation, Conserved Fiber Invariant $P_2$, Null-Projection Causal Threshold $\Phi = |P_2|/\epsilon_5$, Null Geodesic Corollary, Radial First Integral, Routhian Slopes $(+1/2, +3/2)$, and Bianchi Closure --- \texttt{THM~\#13, COR~\#061--\#063} \& \texttt{COR~\#060} \catD]\label{thm:c3_geodesic_decomposition}\label{thm:c3_5d_geodesic_splitting}\label{thm:c3_rescaled_geodesic_bianchi}\label{thm:c3_universal_coupling}
Along any five-dimensional geodesic $x^A(\lambda)$ satisfying $\frac{d^2 x^A}{d\lambda^2} + \Gamma^A_{BC}\frac{dx^B}{d\lambda}\frac{dx^C}{d\lambda} = 0$ with constant affine norm $g_{AB}^{\mathrm{proj}}U^A U^B = -\epsilon_5^2$ ($\epsilon_5^2 > 0$ for timelike trajectories, $\epsilon_5^2 = 0$ for null trajectories) on an $x^2$-independent background:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
  \item \textbf{Conserved Secondary-Time Velocity Component ($P_2$) and Macroscopic Geodesic Equation (\texttt{COR~\#061}):}
  \begin{align}
  \frac{1}{\Phi^2}\frac{d}{d\lambda}\!\left(\Phi^2\frac{dx^2}{d\lambda}\right) &= 0 \quad \Longrightarrow \quad
  P_2 \equiv g_{22}^{\mathrm{proj}}\frac{dx^2}{d\lambda} = -\Phi(x)^2\frac{dx^2}{d\lambda} = \mathrm{const} \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c3_p2_conserved} \\[4pt]
  \frac{d^2 x^\mu}{d\lambda^2} + {}^{(4)}\Gamma^\mu_{\alpha\beta}\,\frac{dx^\alpha}{d\lambda}\,\frac{dx^\beta}{d\lambda} &= -\Gamma^\mu_{22}\left(\frac{dx^2}{d\lambda}\right)^2 = -\frac{P_2^2}{\Phi(x)^3}\,g^{\mu\nu}\partial_\nu\Phi \nonumber \\[3pt]
  &= \frac{1}{2}\,P_2^2\,g^{\mu\nu}\partial_\nu\!\left(\frac{1}{\Phi(x)^2}\right) \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c3_macro_geodesic_affine}
  \end{align}

  \item \textbf{Null-Projection Causal Threshold ($\Phi(x) \ge \Phi_{\mathrm{null}} \equiv |P_2|/\epsilon_5$, \texttt{COR~\#062}), Null Geodesic Corollary ($\epsilon_5 = 0 \implies P_2 = 0$), and Static Radial First Integral ($a_\Omega(r) \ge 0$):}
  Substituting $dx^2/d\lambda = -P_2/\Phi(x)^2$ into the geodesic first integral $g_{AB}^{\mathrm{proj}}\dot{x}^A\dot{x}^B = -\epsilon_5^2$ determines the on-shell norm of the projected 4-velocity $\dot{x}^\mu$ on $\mathcal{M}_4$; requiring this projected 4-velocity to be causal ($-g_{\mu\nu}\dot{x}^\mu\dot{x}^\nu \ge 0$, becoming null at equality) defines the null-projection causal threshold $\Phi_{\mathrm{null}} \equiv |P_2|/\epsilon_5$:
  \begin{align}
  \left(\frac{ds_4}{d\lambda}\right)^2 &\equiv -g_{\mu\nu}\frac{dx^\mu}{d\lambda}\frac{dx^\nu}{d\lambda} = \epsilon_5^2 - \frac{P_2^2}{\Phi(x)^2} \ge 0 \quad \Longleftrightarrow \quad \Phi(x) \ge \Phi_{\mathrm{null}} \equiv \frac{|P_2|}{\epsilon_5} \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c3_4d_velocity_norm} \\[4pt]
  u^\alpha\,{}^{(4)}\nabla_\alpha u^\mu &\equiv \frac{d^2 x^\mu}{ds_4^2} + {}^{(4)}\Gamma^\mu_{\alpha\beta}\,u^\alpha u^\beta = -\frac{P_2^2}{\Phi^2\left(\epsilon_5^2\Phi^2 - P_2^2\right)}\,h^{\mu\nu}\,\partial_\nu\ln\Phi \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c3_proper_time_geodesic}
  \end{align}
  where $u^\mu = dx^\mu/ds_4$ and $h^{\mu\nu} = g^{\mu\nu} + u^\mu u^\nu$. In particular, for any 5D null geodesic ($\epsilon_5 = 0$) whose 4D projection on $\mathcal{M}_4$ is causal ($-g_{\mu\nu}U^\mu U^\nu \ge 0$), Eq.~\eqref{eq:c3_4d_velocity_norm} gives $-P_2^2/\Phi(x)^2 \ge 0 \Longleftrightarrow P_2 \equiv 0$ identically \catD. Furthermore, on any static spherically symmetric metric $ds_5^2 = -A(r)(dx^1)^2 - \Phi(r)^2(dx^2)^2 + B(r)dr^2 + r^2 d\Omega_2^2$ with Killing invariants $\mathcal{E} = A(r)U^1$, $P_2 = -\Phi(r)^2 U^2$, and $L = r^2 U^\phi$, the radial first integral and outward gradient force are:
  \begin{align}
  A(r)B(r)\left(\frac{dr}{d\lambda}\right)^2 + A(r)\left(\epsilon_5^2 + \frac{L^2}{r^2} - \frac{P_2^2}{\Phi(r)^2}\right) &= \mathcal{E}^2 \;\;\catD, \nonumber \\[4pt]
  a_\Omega(r) \equiv -\frac{1}{A(r)B(r)}\frac{d}{dr}\!\left(-\frac{P_2^2\,A(r)}{2\,\Phi(r)^2}\right) &= \frac{P_2^2\left[\frac{1}{2}A'(r) - A(r)\psi'(r)\right]}{A(r)B(r)\Phi(r)^2} \ge 0 \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c3_radial_first_integral_force}
  \end{align}
  which is strictly non-negative whenever $A > 0, B > 0, A' > 0, \psi' \le 0$.

  \item \textbf{Transformation to the Rescaled Metric $\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu} = e^\psi g_{\mu\nu}$ and Asymptotic Routhian Slopes $(+1/2, +3/2)$ (\texttt{COR~\#063}):}
  With the Effective Geometric Routhian Line-Weight $\mu_{\mathrm{geom}}(\psi) \equiv e^{-\psi/2}\sqrt{m_0^2 + P_2^2(-g^{22}_{\mathrm{proj}}(\psi))} = e^{-\psi/2}\sqrt{m_0^2 + P_2^2\Phi_\infty^{-2}e^{-2\psi}}$ (extracted directly from the inverse fiber metric $-g^{22}_{\mathrm{proj}}(\psi) = \Phi_\infty^{-2}e^{-2\psi}$ and the Weyl line-element factor $\sqrt{g_{\theta\theta}/\tilde{g}_{\theta\theta}} = e^{-\psi/2}$ of Theorem~\ref{thm:c3_einstein_frame_reduction}), Eq.~\eqref{eq:c3_proper_time_geodesic} transforms via Eq.~\eqref{eq:c3_gamma_rescaling} into:
  \begin{align}
  \tilde{u}^\alpha\tilde{\nabla}_\alpha\tilde{u}^\mu &= -\frac{d\ln\mu_{\mathrm{geom}}(\psi)}{d\psi}\,\tilde{h}^{\mu\nu}\partial_\nu\psi = \left(\frac{1}{2} + \frac{P_2^2}{m_0^2\Phi_\infty^2 e^{2\psi} + P_2^2}\right)\tilde{h}^{\mu\nu}\partial_\nu\psi \;\;\catD, \nonumber \\[3pt]
  -\frac{d\ln\mu_{\mathrm{geom}}(\psi)}{d\psi}\Bigg|_{P_2=0} &= +\frac{1}{2}, \qquad
  \lim_{|P_2|\to\infty}\left(-\frac{d\ln\mu_{\mathrm{geom}}(\psi)}{d\psi}\right) = +\frac{3}{2} \qquad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#063})} \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c3_rescaled_geodesic_full}
  \end{align}
  where $\tilde{h}^{\mu\nu} = \tilde{g}^{\mu\nu} + \tilde{u}^\mu\tilde{u}^\nu$. For $P_2 = 0$, $-\frac{d\ln\mu_{\mathrm{geom}}}{d\psi} = +\frac{1}{2}$ and $\tilde{u}^\alpha\tilde{\nabla}_\alpha\tilde{u}^\mu = +\frac{1}{2}\tilde{h}^{\mu\nu}\partial_\nu\psi$ \catD.

  \item \textbf{Exact Vacuum Bianchi Identity ($\tilde{\nabla}^\mu\tilde{G}_{\mu\nu}^{(4)} \equiv 0 \Longleftrightarrow \tilde{\Delta}_4\psi = 0$, \texttt{COR~\#060}):}
  Taking the rescaled covariant divergence $\tilde{\nabla}^\mu$ of $\tilde{T}_{\mu\nu}^{(\psi)}$ in Eq.~\eqref{eq:c3_canonical_field_eqs} yields:
  \begin{equation}\label{eq:c3_bianchi_scalar_div}
  \tilde{\nabla}^\mu\tilde{T}_{\mu\nu}^{(\psi)} = \frac{3}{2}\left(\tilde{\Delta}_4\psi\right)\partial_\nu\psi + \frac{3}{2}\,\tilde{g}^{\alpha\beta}\!\left(\partial_\alpha\psi\right)\!\left(\tilde{\nabla}_\beta\partial_\nu\psi - \tilde{\nabla}_\nu\partial_\beta\psi\right) = \frac{3}{2}\left(\tilde{\Delta}_4\psi\right)\partial_\nu\psi \;\;\catD,
  \end{equation}
  locking the trajectory equation~\eqref{eq:c3_rescaled_geodesic_full} and scalar wave equation together via $\tilde{\nabla}^\mu\tilde{G}_{\mu\nu}^{(4)} \equiv 0$.
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}

\subsection{The Khronon-Coupled Master Action ($\mathcal{I}_{3,2}$), Full-Rank 6D 2-Jet Space $J^2(\mathbb{R}^2)$, Hilbert Stress Weights $(-2,-2)$, Stationary Foliation Uniqueness, Bivariate Noether Identity, Universal Ratio $\alpha_0^2 = \frac{1}{3} = -c_{\mathrm{DW}}^{-1}$, and Human-Units Dictionary (\texttt{THM~\#14 \& \#20, COR~\#065 \& \#080--\#082})}

By Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_one} (\texttt{AXIOM\_1}), the bundle $\mathcal{M}_{3,2} = (\mathbb{R}_{t_1} \times_\Phi \mathcal{S}_{t_2}) \times \Sigma_3$ is equipped with the canonical projection onto its primary temporal line $\Theta^{(1)}: \mathcal{M}_{3,2} \to \mathbb{R}_{t_1}$ (with $\partial_2\Theta^{(1)} = 0$ and $\partial_\mu\Theta^{(1)} \neq 0$). As we prove in Section~\ref{sec:c5_symmetry_reductions} (Theorem~\ref{thm:c5_static_radial_odes}(b)) from Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_three} (\texttt{AXIOM\_3}), Bekenstein's elliptic identity and the Janis--Newman--Winicour singularity theorem on $\mathcal{M}_{3,2}$ establish that any potential $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi)$ independent of the primary temporal foliation norm forces either $\psi(r) \equiv 0$ or a horizon curvature singularity $\mathcal{K}_5(r_h) \to \infty$. Consequently, horizon regularity ($|\mathcal{K}_5(r_h)| < \infty$ with $\psi(r) \not\equiv 0$) deductively requires $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}$ to depend on the unique first-order diffeomorphism invariant of $\Theta^{(1)}$.

\begin{definition}[{The Primary Temporal Foliation Norm $\mathcal{A}$ and Dimensionless Master Action $\mathcal{I}_{3,2}$ \catD\ / \catH}]\label{def:c3_master_action}
Let $\Theta^{(1)}: \mathcal{M}_4 \to \mathbb{R}_{t_1}$ be the canonical primary temporal coordinate projection of $\mathcal{M}_{3,2}$, and define its unique first-order diffeomorphism-invariant inverse norm $\mathcal{A}$ relative to the 5D metric $g^{AB}_{\mathrm{proj}}$ ($g^{\mu\nu} = e^\psi\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}$) by \catD:
\begin{equation}\label{eq:c3_khronon_norm_def}
\mathcal{A} \equiv \left(-g^{\mu\nu}\,\partial_\mu\Theta^{(1)}\,\partial_\nu\Theta^{(1)}\right)^{-1}
= \left(-e^{\psi}\,\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}\,\partial_\mu\Theta^{(1)}\,\partial_\nu\Theta^{(1)}\right)^{-1} \;\;\catD,
\end{equation}
which satisfies the exact chain-rule identities $\frac{\partial\mathcal{A}}{\partial g^{11}} = \mathcal{A}^2$ (in adapted coordinates $\Theta^{(1)} = x^1$) and $\left.\frac{\partial\mathcal{A}}{\partial\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}}\right|_\psi = e^\psi\mathcal{A}^2\,\partial_\mu\Theta^{(1)}\partial_\nu\Theta^{(1)}$ across all domains ($\mathcal{A} > 0$ exterior, $\mathcal{A} = 0$ Killing horizon, $\mathcal{A} < 0$ interior) \catD. Measuring all coordinate intervals in units of the fundamental fiber half-period ($L_0 \equiv 1$), the dimensionless Master Action $\mathcal{I}_{3,2}[\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}, \psi, \Theta^{(1)}, A_\mu, x^A(\lambda)]$ on $\mathcal{M}_{3,2}$ (combining the pure vacuum spectral field action \catD\ with the probe trajectory action $-2\int\mu_{\mathrm{geom}}(\psi)\,d\tilde{s}_4$ \catD\ / $\catH_3$) is defined by:
\begin{align}
\mathcal{I}_{3,2}[\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}, \psi, \Theta^{(1)}, A_\mu, x^A] &\equiv \int_{\mathcal{M}_4} d^4x\,\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4}\left[\tilde{R}^{(4)} - \frac{3}{2}\,\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}(\partial_\mu\psi)(\partial_\nu\psi) - 2\,\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A}) - \frac{1}{4}\,F_{\mu\nu}F^{\mu\nu}\right] \nonumber \\[3pt]
&\quad - 2\int \mu_{\mathrm{geom}}(\psi)\,d\tilde{s}_4 \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c3_master_action_i32}
\end{align}
where $\psi = \ln(\Phi/\Phi_\infty)$, $F_{\mu\nu} = \partial_\mu A_\nu - \partial_\nu A_\mu$, and $\mu_{\mathrm{geom}}(\psi) = e^{-\psi/2}\sqrt{m_0^2 + P_2^2\Phi_\infty^{-2}e^{-2\psi}}$. (In Section~\ref{sec:c6_operator_pyramid}, Theorem~\ref{thm:c6_spectral_action_emergence}, we prove under Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_two} that all bulk field terms of Eq.~\eqref{eq:c3_master_action_i32} emerge automatically from the spectral trace of the 5D Dirac--Kostant operator $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}$ \catD.)
\end{definition}

\begin{theorem}[{Complete Stationary System of the Master Action $\delta\mathcal{I}_{3,2} = 0$, Full-Rank 6D 2-Jet Space $J^2(\mathbb{R}^2)$, Hilbert Stress Weights $(c_V, c_{\mathrm{kh}}) = (-2, -2)$, Stationary Foliation Uniqueness $h'(r) \equiv 0$, Bivariate Noether Identity for General $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi,\mathcal{A})$, and Quasi-Static Yukawa Coupling $\alpha_0^2 = \frac{1}{3} = -c_{\mathrm{DW}}^{-1}$ --- \texttt{THM~\#14 \& \#20, COR~\#065 \& \#080--\#082} \catD}]\label{thm:c3_master_action_equations}
Requiring the Master Action $\mathcal{I}_{3,2}$ to be stationary ($\delta\mathcal{I}_{3,2} = 0$) with respect to arbitrary smooth interior variations of all geometric degrees of freedom ($\delta\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}$, $\delta\psi$, $\delta\Theta^{(1)}$, $\delta A_\nu$, $\delta x^\mu$) yields a closed, self-consistent, parameter-free system of differential equations:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
  \item \textbf{Full-Rank 6D Bivariate 2-Jet Space $J^2(\mathbb{R}^2)$, Macroscopic Tensor Field Equation ($\delta\mathcal{I}_{3,2}/\delta\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu} = 0$), Hilbert Stress Weights $(c_V, c_{\mathrm{kh}}) = (-2, -2)$, and Stationary Foliation Uniqueness ($h'(r) \equiv 0$, \texttt{COR~\#080--\#082}):}
  On any bivariate $(t_1, r)$ metric $\widetilde{ds}_4^2 = -N(t_1, r)^2 dt_1^2 + B(t_1, r)^2 dr^2 + r^2 d\Omega_2^2$, the 6 second-order jet coordinates $(\partial_{t_1}^2 N, \partial_{t_1}\partial_r N, \partial_r^2 N, \partial_{t_1}^2 B, \partial_{t_1}\partial_r B, \partial_r^2 B)$ span a full-rank 6-dimensional Jacobian ($\mathrm{rank}\,\mathbf{J}_{\mathrm{jet6}} = 6$, $\tilde{\nabla}_\mu\tilde{G}^{(4)\mu}_{\;\nu} \equiv \mathbf{0}_4$, \texttt{COR~\#080}), and varying Eq.~\eqref{eq:c3_master_action_i32} with respect to $\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}$ yields:
  \begin{equation}\label{eq:c3_master_tensor_eq}
    \begin{aligned}
    &\tilde{G}_{\mu\nu}^{(4)} + \mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})\,\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu} - 2\mathcal{A}^2\,\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})}{\partial\mathcal{A}}\,e^\psi\,\partial_\mu\Theta^{(1)}\,\partial_\nu\Theta^{(1)} \\
    &\qquad = \frac{3}{2}\left[(\partial_\mu\psi)(\partial_\nu\psi) - \frac{1}{2}\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}(\tilde{\nabla}\psi)^2\right] \\
    &\qquad\quad + \frac{1}{2}\left(F_{\mu\alpha}{F_\nu}^\alpha - \frac{1}{4}\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}F_{\alpha\beta}F^{\alpha\beta}\right) + \tilde{T}_{\mu\nu}^{(\mathrm{traj})} \quad \catD.
    \end{aligned}
  \end{equation}
  In adapted coordinates $\Theta^{(1)} = x^1$ on a diagonal metric ($\mathcal{A} = (-e^\psi\tilde{g}^{11})^{-1} = -1/g^{11}$), raising one index with $\tilde{g}^{\mu 1}$ gives the mixed potential stress-energy components with exact Hilbert weights $(c_V, c_{\mathrm{kh}}) = (-2, -2)$ (\texttt{COR~\#081}):
  \begin{align}
  \tilde{T}^1_{\;1(\mathcal{V})} &= \frac{1}{2}\,c_V\,\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A}) + c_{\mathrm{kh}}\,\mathcal{A}\,\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})}{\partial\mathcal{A}}
  = -\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}} - 2\,\mathcal{A}\,\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\mathcal{A}}, \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \tilde{T}^i_{\;j(\mathcal{V})} &= -\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}\,\delta^i_{\;j} \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c3_hilbert_stress_weights}
  \end{align}
  Furthermore, on any static diagonal metric ($g_{11} = -A(r)$, $g_{rr} = B(r)$, $G^1_{\;r} \equiv 0$) with a general stationary foliation ansatz $\Theta^{(1)}(x^1, r) = x^1 + h(r)$, the covariant off-diagonal khronon stress $T_{1r} = c_{\mathrm{kh,cov}}\,\mathcal{A}(r)^2\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\mathcal{A}}\,e^\psi\,h'(r)$ ($c_{\mathrm{kh,cov}} = +2$) raised with $\tilde{g}^{11}$ gives $G^1_{\;r} = T^1_{\;r} = -\frac{2}{N(r)^2}\mathcal{A}(r)^2\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\mathcal{A}}\,e^\psi\,h'(r) = 0$, which uniquely forces $h'(r) \equiv 0$ ($\Theta^{(1)} = x^1$, $\mathcal{A}(r) = A(r)$) wherever $\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\mathcal{A}} \neq 0$ (\texttt{COR~\#082}) \catD.
  \item \textbf{The Bitemporal Scalar Wave Equation ($\delta\mathcal{I}_{3,2}/\delta\psi = 0$) and Quasi-Static Yukawa Coupling $\alpha_0^2 = \frac{1}{3} = -c_{\mathrm{DW}}^{-1}$ (\texttt{COR~\#065}):}
  Varying Eq.~\eqref{eq:c3_master_action_i32} with respect to $\psi$ (using $\frac{\delta\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{curl}}}{\delta\psi} \equiv 0$ from Theorem~\ref{thm:c2_curl_invariance} and $\tilde{T}_{(\mathrm{traj})} = \tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}\tilde{T}_{\mu\nu}^{(\mathrm{traj})} = -\mu_{\mathrm{geom}}n_{\mathrm{traj}}$ for $\tilde{u}_\mu\tilde{u}^\mu = -1$, and noting that $\left.\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})}{\partial\psi}\right|_{\tilde{g}} = \left.\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\psi}\right|_{\mathcal{A}} - \mathcal{A}\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\mathcal{A}}$) yields:
  \begin{equation}\label{eq:c3_master_scalar_eq}
  \begin{aligned}
  \tilde{\Delta}_4\psi - \frac{2}{3}\left(\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\psi} - \mathcal{A}\,\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\mathcal{A}}\right)
  &= -\frac{2}{3}\,\frac{d\ln\mu_{\mathrm{geom}}(\psi)}{d\psi}\,\tilde{T}_{(\mathrm{traj})} \\[3pt]
  &\xrightarrow{P_2 = 0}\;\; +\frac{1}{3}\,\tilde{T}_{(\mathrm{traj})} = -\frac{1}{3}\,\tilde{\rho}_{\mathrm{traj}} \;\;\catD.
  \end{aligned}
  \end{equation}
  In the quasi-static linear response regime around a stationary point ($\mathcal{V}'(0) = 0$, $\mathcal{V}''(0) = -\kappa_\psi m_{\mathrm{eff}}^2$), solving the linearised Poisson equation $\delta\tilde{G}^0_{\;0} = -\rho_m$ together with the linearised Euler--Lagrange equation of Eq.~\eqref{eq:c3_master_scalar_eq} and applying the first-order Weyl map $g_{\mu\nu} = e^{-c_{\mathrm{Weyl}}\psi}\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}$ of Theorem~\ref{thm:c3_einstein_frame_reduction} yields $\mu_{\mathrm{eff}}(a, k) \equiv \Psi_J(k)/\Psi_N(k) = 1 + \alpha_0^2\,\frac{k^2}{k^2 + a^2 m_{\mathrm{eff}}^2}$, where the scalar-to-tensor coupling squared $\alpha_0^2(D) = \frac{c_{\mathrm{Weyl}}^2}{-2\kappa_\psi(D)} = \frac{2}{(D-2)(D-3)}\Big|_{D=5} = \frac{1/2}{3/2} = \frac{1}{3}$ equals minus the inverse trace weight $-c_{\mathrm{DW}}^{-1}(\lambda, D) = -\frac{\lambda}{1 - \lambda D}\Big|_{\lambda=1/2,\,D=5} = \frac{1}{D-2}\Big|_{D=5} = \frac{1}{3}$ of the $15\times 15$ trace-inversion operator $\mathbf{G}_{1/2}$ deduced in Theorem~\ref{thm:c1_metric_15_components}(iii) ($\mu_{\mathrm{UV}} = 1 + \frac{1}{3} = \frac{4}{3}$, \texttt{COR~\#024 \& COR~\#065}) \catD.
  \item \textbf{The Khronon Conservation Equation ($\delta\mathcal{I}_{3,2}/\delta\Theta^{(1)} = 0$) and Bivariate $(t_1, r)$ Diffeomorphism Noether Identity for General $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})$ (\texttt{COR~\#081}):}
  Varying Eq.~\eqref{eq:c3_master_action_i32} with respect to $\Theta^{(1)}$ yields:
  \begin{equation}\label{eq:c3_khronon_conservation_eq}
  \mathcal{E}_\Theta \equiv \tilde{\nabla}_\mu\!\left[\mathcal{A}^2\,\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})}{\partial\mathcal{A}}\,e^\psi\,\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}\,\partial_\nu\Theta^{(1)}\right] = 0 \;\;\catD.
  \end{equation}
  For any general differentiable potential $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A}) \equiv V_{\mathrm{gen}}(e^\psi, \mathcal{A})$, diffeomorphism invariance of $\int d^4x\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4}\,\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})$ and torsion-freeness $\tilde{\nabla}_\mu\partial_\nu\Theta^{(1)} = \tilde{\nabla}_\nu\partial_\mu\Theta^{(1)}$ imply that the covariant divergence of the mixed scalar-plus-potential stress tensor on any bivariate $(t_1, r)$-dependent background satisfies the exact off-shell Noether identity $\tilde{\nabla}_\mu\!\left[\tilde{T}^\mu_{\;\nu(\psi)} + \tilde{T}^\mu_{\;\nu(\mathcal{V})}\right] \equiv \frac{3}{2}\,\mathcal{E}_\psi\,\partial_\nu\psi + 2\,\mathcal{E}_\Theta\,\partial_\nu\Theta^{(1)}$ (where $\mathcal{E}_\psi \equiv \tilde{\Delta}_4\psi - \frac{2}{3}(\partial_\psi\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}} - \mathcal{A}\partial_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}})$, \texttt{COR~\#081}); hence Eq.~\eqref{eq:c3_khronon_conservation_eq} is implied automatically by $\tilde{\nabla}_\mu\tilde{G}^{(4)\mu}_{\;\nu} \equiv 0$ \catD.
  \item \textbf{The Antisymmetric Curl and Rescaled Trajectory Equations ($\delta\mathcal{I}_{3,2}/\delta A_\nu = 0$, $\delta\mathcal{I}_{3,2}/\delta x^\mu = 0$):}
  \begin{align}
  \tilde{\nabla}_\mu F^{\mu\nu} &= \frac{1}{\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4}}\,\partial_\mu\!\left(\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4}\,\tilde{g}^{\mu\alpha}\tilde{g}^{\nu\beta}F_{\alpha\beta}\right) = 0, \qquad
  \partial_\lambda F_{\mu\nu} + \partial_\mu F_{\nu\lambda} + \partial_\nu F_{\lambda\mu} \equiv 0 \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c3_master_maxwell_eq} \\[4pt]
  \tilde{u}^\alpha\tilde{\nabla}_\alpha\tilde{u}^\mu &= -\frac{d\ln\mu_{\mathrm{geom}}(\psi)}{d\psi}\left(\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu} + \tilde{u}^\mu\tilde{u}^\nu\right)\partial_\nu\psi \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c3_master_trajectory_eq}
  \end{align}
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Using $\left.\delta\mathcal{A}\right|_\psi = \mathcal{A}^2 e^\psi\partial_\mu\Theta^{(1)}\partial_\nu\Theta^{(1)}\delta\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu} + 2\mathcal{A}^2 e^\psi\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}\partial_\mu\Theta^{(1)}\partial_\nu\delta\Theta^{(1)}$ inside $\delta\!\left[-2\int d^4x\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4}\,\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})\right]$ in Eq.~\eqref{eq:c3_master_action_i32} yields the khronon stress tensor in Eqs.~\eqref{eq:c3_master_tensor_eq}--\eqref{eq:c3_hilbert_stress_weights} with $(c_V, c_{\mathrm{kh}}) = (-2, -2)$ and, upon integrating $\partial_\nu\delta\Theta^{(1)}$ by parts, the Euler--Lagrange equation~\eqref{eq:c3_khronon_conservation_eq}. For $\Theta^{(1)} = x^1 + h(r)$, $\partial_1\Theta^{(1)} = 1$ and $\partial_r\Theta^{(1)} = h'(r)$, so $G^1_{\;r} = T^1_{\;r} = -(2/N^2)\mathcal{A}^2(\partial_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}})e^\psi h'(r) = 0 \implies h'(r) \equiv 0$. Under an infinitesimal coordinate shift $\delta x^\nu = \epsilon^\nu$, $\delta_{\mathrm{diff}}\psi = \epsilon^\nu\partial_\nu\psi$ and $\delta_{\mathrm{diff}}\Theta^{(1)} = \epsilon^\nu\partial_\nu\Theta^{(1)}$, yielding the bivariate Noether identity $\tilde{\nabla}_\mu(\tilde{T}^\mu_{\;\nu(\psi)} + \tilde{T}^\mu_{\;\nu(\mathcal{V})}) = \frac{3}{2}\mathcal{E}_\psi\partial_\nu\psi + 2\mathcal{E}_\Theta\partial_\nu\Theta^{(1)}$ for any differentiable function $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})$.
\end{proof}

\begin{remark}[{Identification of Section~3 Variational Structures and the Human-Units Translation Dictionary $(c, G_4, \hbar)$}]\label{rem:c3_variational_recognition}\label{rem:c3_human_units_dictionary}
The variational structures deduced in Section~3 correspond to five foundational principles of differential geometry and variational mechanics:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item \textbf{Kaluza--Klein Reduction and Induced-Matter Stress Tensor $T_{\mu\nu}^{(\Phi)}$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:c3_i5_reduction}):} $\mathcal{I}_5[g_{\mathrm{proj}}]$ is the five-dimensional vacuum Einstein--Hilbert action, $\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{bulk}} = \int d^4x\sqrt{-g_4}\,\Phi R^{(4)}$ is the pure Jordan-frame action ($\omega_{\mathrm{BD}} = 0$), and $G_{\mu\nu}^{(4)} = T_{\mu\nu}^{(\Phi)} = \frac{1}{\Phi}({}^{(4)}\nabla_\mu\partial_\nu\Phi - g_{\mu\nu}\Delta_4\Phi)$ proves that an empty 5D geometry ($G_{AB}^{(5)} = 0$) induces an effective 4D stress tensor whenever $\Phi(x)$ varies, with trace factor $g^{\mu\nu}g_{\mu\nu} - 1 = 4 - 1 = 3$ ($\Delta_4\Phi = \frac{1}{3}\Phi R^{(4)}$).
  \item \textbf{Weyl Transform to Einstein Frame and Ghost-Free Positivity of $-\frac{3}{2}(\tilde{\nabla}\psi)^2$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:c3_einstein_frame_reduction}):} The rescaling $\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu} = e^\psi g_{\mu\nu}$ maps the 5D Jordan frame into the 4D Einstein frame, generating $-\frac{3}{2}(\tilde{\nabla}\psi)^2$ ($\kappa_\psi = -\frac{3}{2}$) automatically from the Jacobian $e^{-2\psi}$ and connection shift ${}^{(4)}\Gamma^\lambda_{\mu\nu}$, with strictly positive temporal kinetic energy $\tilde{T}_{11}^{(\psi)} = \frac{3}{4}((\partial_1\psi)^2 + (\nabla_3\psi)^2) \ge 0$ (and canonical Klein--Gordon field $\varphi \equiv \sqrt{3}\,\psi$).
  \item \textbf{Noether Fiber Invariant $P_2$, Null Geodesic Corollary $P_2 = 0$, and Null-Projection Causal Threshold (Theorem~\ref{thm:c3_geodesic_decomposition}):} Conservation of $P_2 = g_{AB}^{\mathrm{proj}}\xi_{(2)}^A U^B = -\Phi^2 U^2$ follows from Killing's equation $\mathcal{L}_{\partial_2}g_{\mathrm{proj}} = 0$; causal 4D projection of null geodesics ($\epsilon_5 = 0$) forces $P_2 = 0$; and on shell $(ds_4/d\lambda)^2 = \epsilon_5^2 - P_2^2/\Phi^2 \ge 0$ establishes that the projected 4-velocity on $\mathcal{M}_4$ is causal if and only if $\Phi \ge \Phi_{\mathrm{null}} = |P_2|/\epsilon_5$ (becoming null at equality).
  \item \textbf{Einstein--Infeld--Hoffmann Bianchi Closure, Bivariate Khronon Noether Identity, Stationary Foliation Uniqueness, and Quasi-Static Yukawa Coupling $\alpha_0^2 = \frac{1}{3} = -c_{\mathrm{DW}}^{-1}$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:c3_master_action_equations}):} The contracted second Bianchi identity $\tilde{\nabla}^\mu\tilde{G}_{\mu\nu}^{(4)} \equiv 0$ locks the trajectory equation, scalar wave equation, and khronon equation together for any differentiable potential $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})$, while $G^1_{\;r} = T^1_{\;r} = 0$ uniquely selects $\Theta^{(1)} = x^1$ and fixes the quasi-static scalar-matter coupling squared to $\alpha_0^2 = \frac{c_{\mathrm{Weyl}}^2}{-2\kappa_\psi} = \frac{1/2}{3/2} = \frac{1}{3} = -c_{\mathrm{DW}}^{-1}\big|_{\lambda=1/2,\,D=5}$ \catD.
  \item \textbf{The Human-Units Translation Dictionary ($c, G_4, \hbar$, Bridge Hypothesis $\catH_2$):} Multiplying $t_1, t_2$ by $c$ ($x^1 = c t_1$, $x^2 = c t_2$), multiplying $k_{2,n}$ by $\hbar/c$ ($m_{\mathrm{KK}} = \frac{\hbar}{c L_0\Phi_\infty}$), and multiplying the dimensionless Master Action $\mathcal{I}_{3,2}$ by the constant conversion factor $\frac{c^4 L_0^2}{16\pi G_4}$ ($G_4 \equiv \frac{G_5}{\pi L_0\Phi_\infty}$) expresses the action in human energy-time units ($\mathrm{J\cdot s}$) $\catH_2$:
  \begin{align}
  S_{\mathrm{CBG}} \equiv \frac{c^4 L_0^2}{16\pi G_4}\,\mathcal{I}_{3,2} &= \int_{\mathcal{M}_4} d^4x\,\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4}\left[\frac{c^4}{16\pi G_4}\left(\tilde{R}^{(4)} - \frac{3}{2}(\tilde{\nabla}\psi)^2 - 2\Lambda_{\mathrm{eff}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})\right) - \frac{1}{4\mu_0}\,F_{\mu\nu}F^{\mu\nu}\right] \nonumber \\[3pt]
  &\quad - \int m_{\mathrm{eff}}(\psi)\,c^2\,d\tilde{\tau} \;\;\catH, \label{eq:c3_human_units_action}
  \end{align}
  where $\Lambda_{\mathrm{eff}}(\psi, \mathcal{A}) \equiv L_0^{-2}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})$ and $m_{\mathrm{eff}}(\psi) \equiv \frac{c^2 L_0}{8\pi G_4}\,\mu_{\mathrm{geom}}(\psi)$.
\end{enumerate}
\end{remark}
