\section{\texorpdfstring{Exact Lie Symmetry Reductions, Boundary-Value Regularity Theorems, and ODE Dynamical Systems of the Master Action $\mathcal{I}_{3,2}$ \catD}{Exact Lie Symmetry Reductions, Boundary-Value Regularity Theorems, and ODE Dynamical Systems of the Master Action I\_\{3,2\}}}
\label{sec:c5_symmetry_reductions}

\subsection{Spatially Homogeneous Reductions ($\mathrm{SO}(4)$, $\mathrm{E}(3)$, $\mathrm{SO}(3,1)$, and Bianchi-I), Nullspace Raychaudhuri Ratio $4\kappa_\psi = -6$, Reparametrization Noether Closure, 2D Minisuperspace Laplace--Beltrami Ordering ($p \equiv 1$), and the $k = +1$ Turning Point (\texttt{THM~\#21, COR~\#083--\#085})}

\begin{theorem}[{Autonomous Homogeneous ODEs With Khronon Coupling, Nullspace Raychaudhuri Ratio $4\kappa_\psi = -6$, Reparametrization Noether Identity, Constraint Preservation, 2D Minisuperspace Laplace--Beltrami Ordering $p \equiv 1$, Exact Bianchi-I Shear First Integral, and Nonsingular Turning Point --- \texttt{THM~\#21, COR~\#083--\#085} \catD}]\label{thm:c5_homogeneous_odes}\label{thm:c5_homogeneous_odes_and_bounce}
Under a transitive spatial isometry group $\mathcal{G}_{\mathrm{hom}}$ on $\Sigma_3$ ($\partial_i \equiv 0$, $\psi = \psi(t_1)$, $\Theta^{(1)} = \Theta(t_1)$, lapse $N(t_1)$, $\mathcal{A} = N^2/\dot{\Theta}^2$, $H \equiv \dot{a}/(Na)$) with spatial curvature $R_{ij}(\gamma) = 2k\gamma_{ij}$ ($k \in \{+1, 0, -1\}$), Bianchi-I traceless shear $\sigma^i_{\;j}(t_1) = \dot{\beta}_i(t_1)\delta^i_{\;j}$ ($\sum_{i=1}^3\dot{\beta}_i = 0$, $\sigma^2 \equiv \frac{1}{2}\sigma^i_{\;j}\sigma^j_{\;i}$), and homogeneous trajectory fluid $\tilde{T}^\mu_{\;\nu(\mathrm{traj})} = \mathrm{diag}(-\tilde{\rho}, \tilde{p}, \tilde{p}, \tilde{p})$, the Master Action field equations~\eqref{eq:c3_master_tensor_eq}--\eqref{eq:c3_master_scalar_eq} satisfy:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
  \item \textbf{Autonomous Homogeneous Evolution System ($N = 1, \Theta^{(1)} = t_1$) and Nullspace Raychaudhuri Ratio $4\kappa_\psi = -6$ (\texttt{COR~\#083}):}
 \begin{align}
    &\quad 3\left(H^2 + \frac{k}{a^2}\right) - \sigma^2 = \frac{3}{4}\dot{\psi}^2 + \mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A}) + 2\mathcal{A}\,\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})}{\partial\mathcal{A}} + \tilde{\rho} \quad \catD, \label{eq:c5_friedmann1} \\
    &\quad 2\dot{H} + 3H^2 + \frac{k}{a^2} + \sigma^2 = -\frac{3}{4}\dot{\psi}^2 + \mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A}) - \tilde{p} \quad \catD, \label{eq:c5_friedmann2} \\
    &\quad \ddot{\psi} + 3H\dot{\psi} + \frac{2}{3}\left(\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\psi} - \mathcal{A}\,\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\mathcal{A}}\right) = \frac{1}{3}(\tilde{\rho} - 3\tilde{p}) \quad \catD, \label{eq:c5_klein_gordon} \\
    &\quad \dot{H} = \frac{k}{a^2} - \frac{3}{4}\dot{\psi}^2 - \sigma^2 - \mathcal{A}\,\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\mathcal{A}} - \frac{1}{2}(\tilde{\rho} + \tilde{p}) \quad \catD, \label{eq:c5_hdot} \\
    &\quad \frac{\ddot{a}}{a} = -\frac{1}{2}\dot{\psi}^2 - \frac{2}{3}\sigma^2 + \frac{1}{3}\left(\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}} - \mathcal{A}\,\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\mathcal{A}}\right) - \frac{1}{6}(\tilde{\rho} + 3\tilde{p}) \quad \catD. \label{eq:c5_addot_identity}
  \end{align}
  In particular, projecting the curved-$k$ vacuum FLRW Einstein components $(-G^{(4)0}_{\;0},\; -G^{(4)1}_{\;1}) = \left(3\frac{\dot{a}^2 + k}{a^2},\; \frac{2a\ddot{a} + \dot{a}^2 + k}{a^2}\right)$ onto the left nullspace vector $\mathbf{n}_{\mathrm{Ray}} = (-1/3,\; 1)^T$ eliminates $\frac{\dot{a}^2 + k}{a^2}$ identically for all $k \in \{-1, 0, +1\}$, giving $\frac{\ddot{a}}{a} = -\frac{1}{6}(\rho_\psi + 3p_\psi) = \frac{\kappa_\psi}{3}\dot{\psi}^2 + \frac{1}{3}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}} = -\frac{1}{2}\dot{\psi}^2 + \frac{1}{3}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}$ in Eq.~\eqref{eq:c5_addot_identity} (where $\rho_\psi = -\frac{1}{2}\kappa_\psi\dot{\psi}^2 + \mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}$ and $p_\psi = -\frac{1}{2}\kappa_\psi\dot{\psi}^2 - \mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}$ follow from the Hilbert variation of Eq.~\eqref{eq:c3_master_action_i32}, with direct ratio $\frac{-1/2}{1/3} = -\frac{3}{2} = \kappa_\psi$), or equivalently in the convention $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{Lag}}(\psi) \equiv 2\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi)$ and $\dot{\hat{\psi}}^2 \equiv \frac{1}{2}\dot{\psi}^2$ ($\rho_\psi = (-\kappa_\psi)\dot{\hat{\psi}}^2 + \frac{1}{2}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{Lag}}$, $p_\psi = (-\kappa_\psi)\dot{\hat{\psi}}^2 - \frac{1}{2}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{Lag}}$), $\frac{\ddot{a}}{a} = c_{\mathrm{kin}}\dot{\hat{\psi}}^2 + c_{\mathrm{pot}}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{Lag}} = -\dot{\hat{\psi}}^2 + \frac{1}{6}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{Lag}}$ with kinetic-to-potential ratio $\frac{c_{\mathrm{kin}}}{c_{\mathrm{pot}}} = \frac{-1}{1/6} = -6 = 4\kappa_\psi$ (\texttt{COR~\#083}) \catD.
  \item \textbf{Deduced Minisuperspace Lagrangians, Off-Shell Time-Reparametrization Noether Identity, and On-Shell Constraint Preservation (\texttt{COR~\#085}):}
  Evaluating the 5D Jordan-frame action density $\sqrt{|g_5|}[R^{(5)} - 2V(\psi, \mathcal{A})]$ and the 4D Einstein-frame action density $\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4}[\tilde{R}^{(4)} + \kappa_\psi\tilde{g}^{\mu\nu}\partial_\mu\psi\partial_\nu\psi - 2\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi)]$ on curved-$k$ FLRW metrics with lapse $N(t_1)$ and removing the boundary total time derivative $\frac{d}{dt_1}\sum_q (\partial L/\partial\ddot{q})\dot{q}$ deduces the first-order minisuperspace Lagrangians $L_{\mathrm{mini}}(N, a, \psi, \Theta) = a e^\psi[-6\dot{a}^2/N - 6a\dot{a}\dot{\psi}/N + 6kN - 2a^2 N V(\psi, N^2/\dot{\Theta}^2)]$ and $L_{\mathrm{mini}}^{(E)}(N, \tilde{a}, \psi) = \frac{\tilde{a}^3}{N}(-3\frac{\dot{\tilde{a}}^2}{\tilde{a}^2} - \frac{1}{2}\kappa_\psi\dot{\psi}^2) + 3kN\tilde{a} - N\tilde{a}^3\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi)$. Their Euler--Lagrange expressions $(\mathcal{E}_N, \mathcal{E}_a, \mathcal{E}_\psi, \mathcal{E}_\Theta)$ satisfy the exact off-shell reparametrization identity, and for the lapse constraint $\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{Fried}} \equiv 3(H^2 + k/a^2 + H\dot{\psi}) - (\rho_r + V(\psi))$ at $N = 1, \sigma = 0$ coupled to a conformally coupled radiation test fluid $\rho_r(t_1)$ satisfying the continuity equation $\dot{\rho}_r + (4H + \dot{\psi})\rho_r = 0$, the constraint satisfies the exact on-shell evolution identity (\texttt{COR~\#085}):
  \begin{align}
  \dot{\Theta}\mathcal{E}_\Theta - N\dot{\mathcal{E}}_N + \dot{a}\mathcal{E}_a + \dot{\psi}\mathcal{E}_\psi &\equiv 0 \quad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#085})} \;\catD, \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \frac{d}{dt_1}\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{Fried}}(t_1) &\equiv -\left(3H + \dot{\psi}\right)\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{Fried}}(t_1) \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c5_minisuperspace_noether}
  \end{align}
  \item \textbf{Vanishing of the Yamabe Conformal Coupling in Dimension $d_{\mathrm{mini}} = d_t = 2$ and Unique Laplace--Beltrami Factor-Ordering Exponent $p \equiv 1$ (\texttt{COR~\#085}):}
  On the two-dimensional minisuperspace $\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{mini}} = \{(\tilde{a}, \psi) \in \mathbb{R}_+ \times \mathbb{R}\}$ ($\dim\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{mini}} = d_{\mathrm{mini}} = d_t = 2$), the reduced DeWitt kinetic metric $G_{AB}^{(\mathrm{mini})} = \mathrm{diag}(-6\tilde{a},\; \frac{3}{2}\tilde{a}^3)$ of $L_{\mathrm{mini}}^{(E)}$ has Lorentzian signature $(-,+)$, determinant $|\det G_{AB}^{(\mathrm{mini})}| = 9\tilde{a}^4$, volume measure $\sqrt{|\det G_{AB}^{(\mathrm{mini})}|} = 3\tilde{a}^2$, and inverse components $G^{\tilde{a}\tilde{a}} = -\frac{1}{6\tilde{a}}$ and $G^{\psi\psi} = +\frac{2}{3\tilde{a}^3}$, so that $\sqrt{|\det G_{AB}^{(\mathrm{mini})}|}\,|G^{\tilde{a}\tilde{a}}| = \frac{1}{2}\,\tilde{a}^{2-1} \propto \tilde{a}^1$. Because the Yamabe conformal curvature coupling coefficient vanishes identically in dimension two:
  \begin{equation}\label{eq:c5_yamabe_2d_zero}
  \xi_{\mathrm{Yamabe}}(d_{\mathrm{mini}}) \equiv \frac{d_{\mathrm{mini}} - 2}{4(d_{\mathrm{mini}} - 1)}\Bigg|_{d_{\mathrm{mini}} = d_t = 2} = 0 \;\;\catD,
  \end{equation}
  coordinate-reparametrization invariance on $\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{mini}}$ and self-adjointness on $L^2(\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{mini}}, \sqrt{|\det G_{AB}^{(\mathrm{mini})}|}\,d\tilde{a}\,d\psi)$ uniquely select the covariant Laplace--Beltrami wave operator, fixing the scale-factor ordering exponent to $p = 2 - 1 \equiv 1$ identically (\texttt{COR~\#085}):
  \begin{align}
  \widehat{\Delta}_{\mathrm{LB}}\Psi_{\mathrm{Univ}}(\tilde{a}, \psi) &\equiv \frac{1}{\sqrt{|\det G^{(\mathrm{mini})}|}}\,\partial_A\!\left(\sqrt{|\det G^{(\mathrm{mini})}|}\,G^{(\mathrm{mini})AB}\,\partial_B\Psi_{\mathrm{Univ}}\right) \nonumber \\[4pt]
  &= -\frac{1}{6\tilde{a}^2}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial\tilde{a}}\!\left(\tilde{a}^p\,\frac{\partial\Psi_{\mathrm{Univ}}}{\partial\tilde{a}}\right) - \frac{4}{\tilde{a}}\,\frac{\partial^2\Psi_{\mathrm{Univ}}}{\partial\psi^2}\right]_{p=1} \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c5_wdw_laplace_beltrami}
  \end{align}
  \item \textbf{Exact Bianchi-I Shear First Integral ($a^{-6}$, \texttt{COR~\#084}) and $k = +1$ Nonsingular Turning Point:}
  On the Bianchi-I background ($k = 0$), the traceless spatial Ricci difference $R^{(4)1}_{\;1} - R^{(4)2}_{\;2} = (\ddot{\beta}_1 - \ddot{\beta}_2) + 3H(\dot{\beta}_1 - \dot{\beta}_2) = 0$ ($c_H = 3$) integrates in both conformal frames ($a_{\mathrm{un}} = e^{-\psi/2}a$, $dt_{1,\mathrm{un}} = e^{-\psi/2}dt_1$, $\hat{\Phi} = e^\psi$) to:
  \begin{align}
  \frac{d}{dt_1}\!\left(a(t_1)^3\,\sigma^i_{\;j}(t_1)\right) = 0 &\;\;\Longleftrightarrow\;\; \frac{d}{dt_{1,\mathrm{un}}}\!\left(a_{\mathrm{un}}^3\,\hat{\Phi}\,\sigma^i_{\;j,\mathrm{un}}\right) = 0, \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \sigma^2(t_1) &= \frac{\Sigma_0^2}{a(t_1)^{2c_H}} = \frac{\Sigma_0^2}{a(t_1)^6} \quad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#084})} \;\catD. \label{eq:c5_bianchi_shear_integral}
  \end{align}
  For $k = +1$ and $\sigma^2 = 0$, defining $\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{tot}} \equiv \frac{3}{4}\dot{\psi}^2 + \mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}} + 2\mathcal{A}\partial_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}} + \tilde{\rho}$ and $\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{tot}} \equiv \frac{3}{4}\dot{\psi}^2 - \mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}} + \tilde{p}$, any trajectory reaching $a_* > 0$ with $a_*^2\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{tot}} = 3$ and $\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{tot}} + 3\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{tot}} < 0$ attains a smooth nonsingular bounce $\dot{a}(t_*) = 0$ with:
  \begin{equation}\label{eq:c5_nonsingular_bounce}
  \ddot{a}(t_*) = -\frac{a_*}{6}\left(\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{tot}} + 3\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{tot}}\right) > 0, \qquad \left|\mathcal{K}_5(t_*)\right| < \infty \;\;\catD.
  \end{equation}
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Fr\'echet Linearization Around Homogeneous Solutions, Unification $\alpha_0^2 = \frac{1}{3} = -c_{\mathrm{DW}}^{-1}$, Jordan Slip $\eta_J \in [1/2, 1]$, and Sturm--Liouville Perturbation Operator (\texttt{THM~\#14, COR~\#064--\#066})}{Frechet Linearization Around Homogeneous Solutions, Unification alpha\_0\^2 = 1/3, Jordan Slip, and Sturm-Liouville Perturbation Operator}}

\begin{theorem}[{Zero Linear Anisotropic Slip $\Phi_N = \Psi_N$, Screened Yukawa Kernel $\mu_{\mathrm{eff}}(a,k)$ ($\alpha_0^2 = \frac{1}{3} = -c_{\mathrm{DW}}^{-1}\big|_{\lambda=1/2}$), Scale-Dependent Jordan Slip $\eta_J(a,k) \in [1/2, 1]$, and Sturm--Liouville ODE --- \texttt{THM~\#14, COR~\#064--\#066} \catD}]\label{thm:c5_sturm_liouville_d1_thm}\label{thm:c5_sturm_liouville_perturbation}
Linearizing Eqs.~\eqref{eq:c3_master_tensor_eq}--\eqref{eq:c3_master_scalar_eq} and~\eqref{eq:c3_rescaled_geodesic_full} around a spatially flat ($k=0, \sigma^2=0, \bar{p}=0, \partial_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}=0$) background in longitudinal gauge $\widetilde{ds}_4^2 = -(1 + 2\Psi_N)dt_1^2 + a(t_1)^2(1 - 2\Phi_N)\delta_{ij}dx^i dx^j$ (with Jordan-frame potentials $\Psi_{\mathrm{dyn}} = \Psi_J = \Phi_N + \frac{1}{2}\delta\psi$ and $\Phi_J = \Phi_N - \frac{1}{2}\delta\psi$ under convention $\delta\psi \mapsto -\delta\psi$) for spatial Laplace eigenmodes $e^{i\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{x}}$ ($\delta(a, \mathbf{k}) \equiv D_1(a; k)\delta_{\mathrm{init}}(\mathbf{k})$) in the quasi-static regime yields (\texttt{COR~\#064--\#066}):
\begin{align}
\delta\tilde{G}^{(4)}_{xy} &= \partial_x\partial_y\!\left(\Phi_N - \Psi_N\right) = 0 \quad \Longrightarrow \quad
\Phi_N(t_1, \mathbf{k}) = \Psi_N(t_1, \mathbf{k}) \quad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#064})} \;\catD, \label{eq:c5_no_slip_identity} \\[4pt]
\delta\tilde{G}^{(4)0}_{\;0}\big|_{\Psi_N=\Phi_N} &= -\frac{2}{a^2}\,\nabla_3^2\Phi_N + 6\,H\left(\dot{\Phi}_N + H\,\Phi_N\right) \quad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#064})} \;\catD, \nonumber \\[4pt]
\left(\frac{k^2}{a^2} + m_\psi^2\right)\delta\psi(t_1, \mathbf{k}) &= +\frac{1}{3}\,\bar{\rho}(t_1)\,\delta(t_1, \mathbf{k}) \quad \left(\alpha_0^2 = \frac{1}{3} \equiv -c_{\mathrm{DW}}^{-1}\Big|_{\lambda=1/2}, \;\; \textnormal{\texttt{COR~\#065}}\right) \;\catD, \label{eq:c5_helmholtz_deltapsi} \\[4pt]
-\frac{k^2}{a^2}\,\Psi_{\mathrm{dyn}}(t_1, \mathbf{k}) &= \frac{1}{2}\,\mu_{\mathrm{eff}}(a, k)\,\bar{\rho}(t_1)\,\delta(t_1, \mathbf{k}) \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c5_effective_poisson_eq} \\[4pt]
\mu_{\mathrm{eff}}(a, k) &\equiv 1 + \frac{1}{3}\,\frac{k^2}{k^2 + a^2 m_\psi^2} = \frac{4k^2 + 3a^2 m_\psi^2}{3\left(k^2 + a^2 m_\psi^2\right)} \in \left[1, \frac{4}{3}\right) \quad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#065})} \;\catD, \label{eq:c5_effective_poisson_kernel} \\[4pt]
\eta_J(a, k) &\equiv \frac{\Phi_J(t_1, \mathbf{k})}{\Psi_J(t_1, \mathbf{k})} = \frac{2k^2 + 3a^2 m_\psi^2}{4k^2 + 3a^2 m_\psi^2} \in \left(\frac{1}{2}, 1\right] \quad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#066})} \;\catD, \label{eq:c5_jordan_slip_kernel} \\[4pt]
\frac{d^2 D_1}{d(\ln a)^2} &+ \left(2 + \frac{d\ln H}{d\ln a} - \frac{1}{2}\,\frac{d\bar{\psi}}{d\ln a}\right)\frac{d D_1}{d\ln a} - \frac{3}{2}\,\Omega_m(a)\,\mu_{\mathrm{eff}}(a, k)\,D_1(a; k) = 0 \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c5_sturm_liouville_d1}
\end{align}
\end{theorem}

\subsection{Injection of Axiom~5.3 (\texttt{AXIOM\_3}), Static Spherically Symmetric Isometry Reduction ($\mathbb{R}_{t_1} \times \mathrm{SO}(3)$), Bekenstein Star-Shaped Domain $(-\infty, \frac{1}{2}\ln 3)$, Horizon Theorem, Normal-Form 5D Radial ODEs, Frobenius Horizon Regularity ($\mathcal{C}_{B^2} + 1/b_{-1} \equiv 0$), and Pole-Free Stiff-Limit Reduction (\texttt{THM~\#22, COR~\#086--\#087})}

\begin{axiom}[{Killing Horizon Curvature Regularity and Equilibrium-Root Coupling --- \texttt{AXIOM\_3} \catA}]\label{ax:axiom_three}
Any static five-dimensional geometry on $\mathcal{M}_{3,2}$ supporting a non-constant temporal fiber profile $\psi(r) \neq \mathrm{const}$ across a Killing horizon $r = r_h > 0$ must maintain a bounded five-dimensional Kretschmann scalar $|\mathcal{K}_5(r_h)| < \infty$, with the primary temporal foliation norm $\mathcal{A}$ entering the spectral potential $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})$ through its equilibrium root $\psi_t(\mathcal{A})$ \catA.
\end{axiom}

\begin{theorem}[{Einstein-Frame and Jordan-Frame Coupled Radial ODE Systems, Semi-Infinite Bekenstein Star-Shaped Domain $(-\infty, \frac{1}{2}\ln 3)$ \& Barrier Height $\frac{1}{54}$, Bekenstein--JNW Horizon Theorem Under Axiom~5.3, Radial 5-Divergence Identity $\nabla_A\mathcal{T}^A_{\;r} \equiv 0$, Frobenius Horizon Regularity $\mathcal{C}_{B^2} + 1/b_{-1} \equiv 0$, and Pole-Free Stiff-Limit Reduction --- \texttt{THM~\#22, COR~\#086--\#087} \catD}]\label{thm:c5_static_radial_odes}
For the canonical $\mathbb{R}_{t_1} \times \mathrm{SO}(3)$-invariant static metric in the 4D Einstein frame $\widetilde{ds}_4^2 = -\mathcal{A}_E(r)^2 dt_1^2 + \mathcal{B}(r)^2 dr^2 + r^2(d\theta^2 + \sin^2\theta\,d\varphi^2)$ and in the 5D Jordan frame $ds_5^2 = -A(r)dt_1^2 - \Phi_\infty^2 e^{2\psi(r)}dt_2^2 + B(r)dr^2 + r^2(d\theta^2 + \sin^2\theta\,d\varphi^2)$ with $\Theta^{(1)} = t_1$ and 5D temporal Killing norm $\mathcal{A}(r) = (-g^{\mu\nu}\partial_\mu\Theta^{(1)}\partial_\nu\Theta^{(1)})^{-1} = A(r) = e^{-\psi(r)}\mathcal{A}_E(r)^{2}$ (Eq.~\ref{eq:c3_khronon_norm_def}, so that $\left.\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi,\mathcal{A})}{\partial\psi}\right|_{\mathcal{A}_E} = \frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\psi} - \mathcal{A}\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\mathcal{A}}$) in exterior vacuum ($\tilde{T}_{\mu\nu}^{(\mathrm{traj})} = 0$), the Master Action field equations satisfy five exact deductive theorems:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\alph*)]
  \item \textbf{4D Einstein-Frame Radial ODE System and Focusing Identity:}
  \begin{align}
  \frac{1}{r^2\mathcal{B}^2}\left(\frac{2r\mathcal{B}'}{\mathcal{B}} + \mathcal{B}^2 - 1\right) &= \frac{3}{4}\,\frac{(\psi')^2}{\mathcal{B}^2} + \mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A}) + 2\,\mathcal{A}\,\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})}{\partial\mathcal{A}} \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c5_einstein_radial_11} \\[3pt]
  \frac{1}{r^2\mathcal{B}^2}\left(\frac{2r\mathcal{A}_E'}{\mathcal{A}_E} - \mathcal{B}^2 + 1\right) &= \frac{3}{4}\,\frac{(\psi')^2}{\mathcal{B}^2} - \mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A}) \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c5_einstein_radial_rr} \\[3pt]
  \frac{1}{r^2\mathcal{A}_E(r)\mathcal{B}(r)}\frac{d}{dr}\!\left(r^2\frac{\mathcal{A}_E(r)}{\mathcal{B}(r)}\psi'(r)\right) &= \frac{2}{3}\left(\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\psi} - \mathcal{A}\,\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\mathcal{A}}\right) \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c5_einstein_radial_psi} \\[3pt]
  \frac{d}{dr}\ln\!\left[\mathcal{A}_E(r)\mathcal{B}(r)\right] = \frac{\mathcal{A}_E'}{\mathcal{A}_E} + \frac{\mathcal{B}'}{\mathcal{B}} &= \frac{3}{4}\,r\left[\psi'(r)\right]^2 + r\,\mathcal{B}(r)^2\,\mathcal{A}\,\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})}{\partial\mathcal{A}} \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c5_einstein_focusing}
  \end{align}
  \item \textbf{Exact Factorization of $\Pi_2'(\psi)$, Semi-Infinite Bekenstein Star-Shaped Domain $(-\infty, \frac{1}{2}\ln 3)$ with Barrier $\frac{1}{54}$, and Bekenstein--JNW Horizon Theorem Under Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_three} (\texttt{COR~\#086--\#087}):}
  For the canonical 2-jet basis polynomial $\Pi_2(\psi) = \frac{1}{8}(e^{2\psi} - 1)^2 e^{-6\psi}$ of Theorem~\ref{thm:c4_moments_and_potential}(i), the first derivative factors algebraically as:
  \begin{align}
  \Pi_2'(\psi) &= -\frac{1}{4}\,e^{-6\psi}\left(e^{2\psi} - 1\right)\!\left(e^{2\psi} - 3\right) = 0 \;\;\Longleftrightarrow\;\; \psi \in \left\{0,\; +\frac{1}{2}\ln 3\right\}, \nonumber \\[4pt]
  \psi\,\Pi_2'(\psi) &> 0 \quad \forall\,\psi \in \left(-\infty, +\frac{1}{2}\ln 3\right) \setminus \{0\}, \qquad
  \Pi_2\!\left(\frac{1}{2}\ln 3\right) = \frac{1}{54} \quad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#086})} \;\catD. \label{eq:c5_pi2_derivative_factorization}
  \end{align}
  At a Killing horizon $r = r_h > 0$ where $\mathcal{A}_E(r_h) = 0$ and $\frac{\mathcal{A}_E(r)}{\mathcal{B}(r)} \sim \kappa_h(r - r_h)$ ($\kappa_h > 0$), if $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}$ is independent of $\mathcal{A}$ ($\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\mathcal{A}} \equiv 0$, with $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi) = \frac{3}{2}m_\psi^2\Pi_2(\psi)$ in asymptotically flat space $\mathcal{V}_0 = 0$, or $\mathcal{V}_0 \ge 0$ on $\psi \le 0$ where $\psi\Pi_0'(\psi) = -6\psi e^{-2\psi}(1-e^{-2\psi})^2 \ge 0$), massless JNW integration of Eq.~\eqref{eq:c5_einstein_radial_psi} ($m_\psi = 0 \implies r^2\frac{\mathcal{A}_E}{\mathcal{B}}\psi' = C_{\mathrm{scalar}}$) diverges as $\psi'(r) \sim \frac{C_{\mathrm{scalar}}}{\kappa_h r_h^2(r - r_h)}$ unless $C_{\mathrm{scalar}} = 0$, while for $m_\psi \ge 0$, horizon regularity under Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_three} ($|\mathcal{K}_5(r_h)| < \infty$ in Eq.~\eqref{eq:c4_kretschmann_split}, requiring bounded $|\psi(r_h)|, |\psi'(r_h)| < \infty$) implies vanishing horizon boundary flux $\lim_{r\to r_h^+} r^2\frac{\mathcal{A}_E(r)}{\mathcal{B}(r)}\psi(r)\psi'(r) = 0$. Multiplying Eq.~\eqref{eq:c5_einstein_radial_psi} by $r^2\mathcal{A}_E\mathcal{B}\,\psi(r)$ and integrating by parts over $[r_h, \infty)$ for any horizon-regular profile in the star-shaped Bekenstein domain $\psi(r) \in (-\infty, \frac{1}{2}\ln 3)$ with $\psi(\infty) = 0$ yields (\texttt{COR~\#087}):
  \begin{align}
  \int_{r_h}^{\infty} r^2\,\frac{\mathcal{A}_E(r)}{\mathcal{B}(r)}\left[\left(\psi'(r)\right)^2 + m_\psi^2\,\mathcal{B}(r)^2\,\psi(r)\,\Pi_2'(\psi)\right]dr &= 0 \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \Longrightarrow\quad \psi(r) \equiv 0 \quad \left(\left.\psi'(r_h)\right|_{\partial_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}=0} = 0\right) &\;\;\catD, \label{eq:c5_bekenstein_integral_identity}
  \end{align}
  whereas when $\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})}{\partial\mathcal{A}} = -\psi'_t(\mathcal{A})\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\psi} \not\equiv 0$ in Eq.~\eqref{eq:c4_shifted_potential} (with $\mathcal{A}(r) = e^{-\psi(r)}\mathcal{A}_E(r)^2$), the equilibrium root $\psi_t(\mathcal{A}(r))$ varies continuously with radius from $\psi_t(0) = \psi_0 > 0$ at the horizon ($\mathcal{A}(r_h) = 0$) to $\psi_t(1) = \psi_\infty = 0$ at spatial infinity ($\mathcal{A}(\infty) = 1$). Because no single constant shift $\psi \mapsto \psi - \mathrm{const}$ can simultaneously center the potential at both $r = r_h$ and $r = \infty$, the effective source integrand factors as $(\psi(r) - \psi_\infty)\left(\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\psi} - \mathcal{A}\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\mathcal{A}}\right) = (\psi(r) - \psi_\infty)\left[1 + \mathcal{A}(r)\psi'_t(\mathcal{A}(r))\right]\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\psi} \simeq \frac{3}{2}m_\psi^2\,\psi(r)\left[\psi(r) - \psi_t(\mathcal{A}(r))\right]\!\left[1 + \mathcal{A}(r)\psi'_t(\mathcal{A}(r))\right]$ (where $1 + \mathcal{A}\psi'_t(\mathcal{A}) \ge 1 - 2\psi_0/e > 0$ is strictly positive), changing sign across $[r_h, \infty)$ whenever $0 < \psi(r) < \psi_t(\mathcal{A}(r))$ and evading Bekenstein's global sign obstruction in Eq.~\eqref{eq:c5_bekenstein_integral_identity} \catD.
  \item \textbf{Exact 5D Jordan-Frame Normal-Form ODEs for $(A'(r), B'(r), \psi''(r))$ and Radial 5-Divergence Identity $\nabla_A\mathcal{T}^A_{\;r} \equiv 0$:}
  On the 5D static Jordan-frame metric $ds_5^2 = -A(r)dt_1^2 - \Phi_\infty^2 e^{2\psi(r)}dt_2^2 + B(r)dr^2 + r^2 d\Omega_2^2$ ($g_{\mu\nu} = e^{-\psi}\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}$, $\sqrt{|g_5|} = \Phi_\infty e^{-\psi}\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4}$) with Jordan-frame potential $V(\psi, A) \equiv e^{\psi}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, A)$ (so that $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, A) = e^{-\psi}V(\psi, A)$ and $\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\psi} = e^{-\psi}(\frac{\partial V}{\partial\psi} - V)$, with 5D stress components $\mathcal{T}^1_{\;1} = -V - 2A\partial_A V$, $\mathcal{T}^2_{\;2} = -V - \partial_\psi V$, $\mathcal{T}^r_{\;r} = \mathcal{T}^\theta_{\;\theta} = -V$), the radial 5-divergence vanishes identically for any sign of $A(r)$:
  \begin{equation}\label{eq:c5_jordan_radial_bianchi}
  \nabla_A\mathcal{T}^A_{\;r} \equiv \frac{d\mathcal{T}^r_{\;r}}{dr} + \frac{A'}{2A}\left(\mathcal{T}^r_{\;r} - \mathcal{T}^1_{\;1}\right) + \psi'\left(\mathcal{T}^r_{\;r} - \mathcal{T}^2_{\;2}\right) + \frac{2}{r}\left(\mathcal{T}^r_{\;r} - \mathcal{T}^\theta_{\;\theta}\right) \equiv 0 \;\;\catD,
  \end{equation}
  and solving $G^A_{\;B} = \mathcal{T}^A_{\;B}$ for $(A'(r), B'(r), \psi''(r))$ yields the unique normal-form system under which all four Einstein equations $E^1_{\;1} = E^2_{\;2} = E^r_{\;r} = E^\theta_{\;\theta} \equiv 0$ vanish identically:
  \begin{align}
  A'(r) &= -\frac{2\,A(r)\left[r^2 B(r)V(\psi, A) + 2r\,\psi'(r) - B(r) + 1\right]}{r\left(r\,\psi'(r) + 2\right)} \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c5_jordan_normal_aprime} \\[4pt]
  B'(r) &= \frac{2B(r)}{3\,r\left(r\,\psi'(r) + 2\right)}\bigg[r^3 B\left(2A\frac{\partial V}{\partial A} + 2V + \frac{\partial V}{\partial\psi}\right)\psi' \nonumber \\
  &\quad + r^2 B\left(4A\frac{\partial V}{\partial A} + V + 2\frac{\partial V}{\partial\psi}\right) + 3r^2(\psi')^2 - 3(r\psi' + 1)(B - 1)\bigg] \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c5_jordan_normal_bprime} \\[4pt]
  \psi''(r) &= \frac{1}{3\,r}\bigg[r^2 B\left(2A\frac{\partial V}{\partial A} + 2V + \frac{\partial V}{\partial\psi}\right)\psi' \nonumber \\
  &\quad - 2rB\left(A\frac{\partial V}{\partial A} + V - \frac{\partial V}{\partial\psi}\right) - 3(B + 1)\psi'\bigg] \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c5_jordan_normal_psipp}
  \end{align}
  \item \textbf{Local Frobenius Horizon Regularity Theorem at $r \to r_h^+$ and Symbolic Cancellation of the Anomalous Pole ($\mathcal{C}_{B^2} + 1/b_{-1} \equiv 0$):}
  For any simple-zero/simple-pole near-horizon Frobenius expansion $A(r) = a_1(r - r_h) + \mathcal{O}((r - r_h)^2)$, $B(r) = b_{-1}/(r - r_h) + b_0 + \mathcal{O}(r - r_h)$, $\psi(r) = \psi_h + \psi'_h(r - r_h) + \mathcal{O}((r - r_h)^2)$ with $(\partial_A V)_h = 0$, Eq.~\eqref{eq:c5_jordan_normal_aprime} fixes the pole residue $b_{-1}$, finiteness of $\psi''(r_h)$ in Eq.~\eqref{eq:c5_jordan_normal_psipp} fixes the local horizon slope $\psi'(r_h)$ (whose numerator $V_h - (\partial_\psi V)_h = -e^{\psi_h}(\partial_\psi\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}})_h$ vanishes if and only if $\psi_h$ sits at a stationary point of the Einstein-frame potential $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, 0) = e^{-\psi}V(\psi, 0)$, and is non-zero whenever $\psi_h$ is not a stationary point of $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, 0)$), and substituting $\psi'(r_h)$ into the leading $\mathcal{O}((r - r_h)^{-2})$ coefficient $B'(r) \simeq \mathcal{C}_{B^2}(\psi_h, \psi'_h)B(r)^2$ of Eq.~\eqref{eq:c5_jordan_normal_bprime} cancels the anomalous pole identically for every differentiable potential $V(\psi, A)$ (establishing local near-horizon Frobenius regularity \catD, while global boundary-value shooting connecting $r_h$ to $\infty$ is formulated as a separate numerical boundary-value problem in class \catN):
  \begin{align}
  b_{-1} &= \frac{r_h(r_h\psi'_h + 2)}{2(1 - r_h^2 V_h)}, \qquad
  \psi'(r_h) = \frac{2\,r_h\left(V_h - (\partial_\psi V)_h\right)}{r_h^2\left(2\,V_h + (\partial_\psi V)_h\right) - 3} \;\;\catD, \nonumber \\[4pt]
  \mathcal{C}_{B^2}\!\left(\psi_h, \psi'(r_h)\right) + \frac{1}{b_{-1}} &= -\frac{2(1 - r_h^2 V_h)}{r_h(r_h\psi'(r_h) + 2)} + \frac{2(1 - r_h^2 V_h)}{r_h(r_h\psi'(r_h) + 2)} \equiv 0 \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c5_frobenius_pole_cancellation}
  \end{align}
  \item \textbf{Exact Pole-Free Stiff-Limit Reduction ($\hat{m}_\psi \to \infty$) via $\frac{B'}{B} = \frac{P'}{P} - \frac{A'}{A}$:}
  In the singular-perturbation stiff limit $\hat{m}_\psi \equiv m_\psi r_h \to \infty$ where $V(\psi, A) = \frac{3}{4}m_\psi^2[\psi - \psi_t(A)]^2$ locks $\psi(r) \equiv \psi_t(A(r))$ ($\psi' = \psi'_t(A)A'$, $\psi'' = \psi''_t(A)(A')^2 + \psi'_t(A)A''$, with $\eta_t(A) \equiv \frac{\psi'_t(A)}{A} = -2\lambda_A\psi_0 e^{-\lambda_A A^2}$ analytic and finite at $A = 0$ by Eq.~\eqref{eq:c4_gaussian_kernel}, $V \to 0$), eliminating the Lagrange multiplier $\lambda_{\mathrm{stiff}}(r) = \lim_{\hat{m}_\psi\to\infty}\frac{\partial V}{\partial\psi}$ via $G^r_{\;r} = 0$ defines the smooth horizon-regular product $P(r) \equiv A(r)B(r) = A + rA' + 2rA\psi'_t(A)A' + \frac{1}{2}r^2 A\eta_t(A)(A')^2$. Substituting $\frac{B'}{B} = \frac{P'}{P} - \frac{A'}{A}$ into $2ABG^\theta_{\;\theta} = 0$ cancels every $1/A$ and $1/(r - r_h)$ divisor identically:
  \begin{equation}\label{eq:c5_stiff_limit_ode}
  A'' - \frac{A'P'}{2P} + \frac{2A'}{r} - \frac{AP'}{rP} + 2A\psi'' + 2A(\psi')^2 + \left(\frac{2A}{r} + 2A' - \frac{AP'}{P}\right)\psi' = 0 \;\;\catD.
  \end{equation}
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}

\subsection{Static Spherical Electro-Vacuum Reduction ($\mathbb{R}_{t_1} \times \mathrm{SO}(3) \times \mathrm{U}(1)$), Inner Cauchy Horizon $r_- > 0$, and Surface Gravity (\texttt{THM~\#22, COR~\#088})}

\begin{theorem}[{Exact Radial Coulomb Flux, Closed-Form Kottler--RN--de Sitter Solution, Exact Inner Horizon Root $r_- > 0$, and Surface Gravity --- \texttt{THM~\#22, COR~\#088} \catD}]\label{thm:c5_electro_vacuum}\label{thm:c5_electrovacuum_desitter_odes}
For a polar curl 2-form $F_{t_1 r}(r) = -A'_{t_1}(r)$ on the static metric of Theorem~\ref{thm:c5_static_radial_odes}, conservation of the conjugate momentum per unit $\sin\theta$ in the Maxwell sector $-\frac{1}{4}F_{\mu\nu}F^{\mu\nu}$ of the Master Action~\eqref{eq:c3_master_action_i32} and the Hilbert stress tensor~\eqref{eq:c3_master_tensor_eq} yield:
\begin{align}
\frac{d}{dr}\!\left(r^2\,\frac{F_{t_1 r}(r)}{\mathcal{A}_E(r)\mathcal{B}(r)}\right) = 0 \;\;\Longrightarrow\;\; F_{t_1 r}(r) &= \frac{Q_e\,\mathcal{A}_E(r)\mathcal{B}(r)}{r^2}, \nonumber \\[3pt]
\tilde{T}^\mu_{\;\nu(\mathrm{curl})}(r) &= \frac{Q_e^2}{4r^4}\,\mathrm{diag}(-1, -1, +1, +1) \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c5_coulomb_flux}
\end{align}
which cancels identically in $\tilde{G}^{t_1}_{\;t_1} - \tilde{G}^r_{\;r} = \tilde{T}^{t_1}_{\;t_1} - \tilde{T}^r_{\;r} = 0$ (Eq.~\eqref{eq:c5_einstein_focusing}) and contributes zero source to the $\psi(r)$ equation ($\frac{\delta\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{curl}}}{\delta\psi} \equiv 0$). At the stationary vacuum minimum $\psi(r) \equiv 0$ ($\partial_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}} = 0$, $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(0) = 3\mathcal{V}_0 \equiv 3H_\infty^2$ from Theorem~\ref{thm:c4_moments_and_potential}(i)), $(\mathcal{A}_E(r)\mathcal{B}(r))' = 0 \implies \mathcal{A}_E(r)\mathcal{B}(r) = 1$, and solving the static field equations with $f(r) \equiv \mathcal{A}_E(r)^2 = \mathcal{B}(r)^{-2}$ (together with the general Killing surface-gravity identity $\kappa^2 \equiv -\frac{1}{2}(\tilde{\nabla}_\mu\xi_\nu)(\tilde{\nabla}^\mu\xi^\nu) = \frac{1}{4}[f'(r)]^2$ for $\xi = \partial_{t_1}$) yields in closed form (\texttt{COR~\#088}):
\begin{align}
\mathcal{A}_E(r)^2 = \mathcal{B}(r)^{-2} = f(r) &= 1 - \frac{r_h}{r} + \frac{Q_e^2}{4r^2} - H_\infty^2 r^2 \qquad \left(r_h = 2M, \;\; H_\infty^2 \equiv \mathcal{V}_0\right) \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c5_kottler_solution} \\[4pt]
r_\pm &= \frac{1}{2}\left(r_h \pm \sqrt{r_h^2 - Q_e^2}\right) > 0 \qquad \left(H_\infty = 0, \;\; 0 < |Q_e| \le r_h\right) \;\;\catD, \nonumber \\[4pt]
\kappa_{\mathrm{dS}} &\equiv \sqrt{\kappa^2\big|_{r_h=Q_e=0,\, r=H_\infty^{-1}}} = \frac{1}{2}\left|f'(H_\infty^{-1})\right| = H_\infty, \qquad
a_{\mathrm{dS}} \equiv \frac{\kappa_{\mathrm{dS}}}{2\pi} = \frac{H_\infty}{2\pi} \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c5_inner_cauchy_horizon}
\end{align}
where $r_+ > 0$ is the outer event horizon and $r_- = \frac{1}{2}\left(r_h - \sqrt{r_h^2 - Q_e^2}\right) > 0$ ($r_+ + r_- = r_h$, $r_+ r_- = \frac{1}{4}Q_e^2$) is the exact inner Cauchy horizon at which $\mathcal{A}_E(r_-)^2 = 0$, terminating the trapped region $r \in (r_-, r_+)$ and restoring timelike signature ($\mathcal{A}_E(r)^2 > 0$) for $r < r_-$ \catD.
\end{theorem}

\subsection{Interior Domain Continuation ($r < r_h$), $P_2 \neq 0$ Routhian Centrifugal Barrier $e^{-3\psi}/r^2$, Turning Radius $r_{\mathrm{turn}} > 0$, and 5D Null Energy Condition Violation (\texttt{THM~\#23, COR~\#089--\#090})}

\begin{theorem}[{Uncoupled Kasner Divergence, Routhian Centrifugal Barrier Scaling $e^{-3\psi}/r^2$, $P_2 \neq 0$ Turning Radius $r_{\mathrm{turn}} > 0$, Khronon 5D NEC Violation Condition, and Curvature Boundedness on Regular Interior Domains $r \ge r_{\min} > 0$ --- \texttt{THM~\#23, COR~\#089--\#090} \catD\ / \catH}]\label{thm:c5_interior_continuation}\label{thm:c5_interior_kasner_regularity}
In the interior domain $r \in (0, r_h)$ where $\widetilde{ds}_4^2 = -e^{2\lambda(r)}dr^2 + e^{2\nu(r)}dt_1^2 + r^2(d\theta^2 + \sin^2\theta\,d\varphi^2)$ ($\partial_r$ timelike with $\tilde{g}_{rr} = -e^{2\lambda} < 0$, $\partial_{t_1}$ spacelike with $\tilde{g}_{11} = +e^{2\nu} > 0$, Killing norm $\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{Killing}} = (-e^\psi\tilde{g}^{11})^{-1} = -e^{-\psi(r)+2\nu(r)} < 0$, and $-e^{2\lambda}\tilde{\Delta}_4\psi = \psi'' + (\nu' - \lambda' + \frac{2}{r})\psi'$):
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
  \item \textbf{Uncoupled Asymptotic Kasner Divergence When $P_2 = 0$, $Q_e = 0$, and $\partial_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}} = 0$ (\texttt{COR~\#089}):}
  In the uncoupled regime $P_2 = 0$, $Q_e = 0$, and $\partial_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}} = 0$ (where any $\mathcal{A}$-independent potential $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi)$ cancels identically in $\tilde{G}^1_{\;1} - \tilde{G}^r_{\;r}$, and where in the scalar ODE the steepest $\mathcal{W}_3$ basis term $e^{2\lambda}e^{-6\psi} \sim r^{2p_r - 6c_\psi}$ is subleading relative to the curvature scale $\tilde{g}^{\theta\theta} = 1/r^2$ as $r \to 0^+$ either for $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}} \equiv 0$ or, when $c_2 = 3\mathcal{V}_0 + \frac{3}{16}m_\psi^2 \neq 0$, on the self-consistent Kasner window $2p_r - 6c_\psi > -2 \iff c_\psi^2 - 8c_\psi + 4 > 0 \iff c_\psi \notin [4 - 2\sqrt{3},\; 4 + 2\sqrt{3}]$), the leading asymptotic power-law ansatz $e^{2\nu(r)} \sim r^{2p_t}$, $e^{2\lambda(r)} \sim r^{2p_r}$, and $\psi(r) \sim c_\psi\ln r$ as $r \to 0^+$ in the source-free interior scalar wave equation $\psi'' + (\nu' - \lambda' + \frac{2}{r})\psi' = 0$ asymptotically enforces $p_r - p_t = 1$ (with Schwarzschild limit $p_t = -\frac{1}{2}, p_r = +\frac{1}{2}$ at $c_\psi = 0$), while the interior radial difference equation $e^{2\lambda}(\tilde{G}^1_{\;1} - \tilde{G}^r_{\;r}) = \frac{2}{r}(\nu' + \lambda') = +\frac{3}{2}(\psi')^2$ (with $\nu' = p_t/r$, $\lambda' = p_r/r$) directly enforces $p_t + p_r = \frac{3}{4}c_\psi^2$ ($2p_r = 1 + \frac{3}{4}c_\psi^2$). Consequently, the 4D Einstein-frame Kretschmann scalar $\widetilde{\mathcal{K}}_4(r) \propto (e^{-2\lambda}r^{-2})^2 \sim r^{-4(p_r+1)} = r^{-(6 + \frac{3}{2}c_\psi^2)} \to \infty$ diverges for all $c_\psi \in \mathbb{R}$, and the 5D Jordan-frame Kretschmann scalar on the full 5D metric (with curved $S^2$ and $p_r = \frac{1}{2} + \frac{3}{8}c_\psi^2$, $p_t = -\frac{1}{2} + \frac{3}{8}c_\psi^2$) evaluates in closed form to $\mathcal{K}_5(r) \simeq \frac{3}{16}(c_\psi - 2)^2(9c_\psi^4 - 12c_\psi^3 + 40c_\psi^2 + 16c_\psi + 16)\,r^{-(6 + \frac{3}{2}c_\psi^2 - 2c_\psi)} \to \infty$ (where both the quartic factor $(3c_\psi^2 - 2c_\psi)^2 + 36(c_\psi + \frac{2}{9})^2 + \frac{128}{9} \ge \frac{128}{9} > 0$ and the exponent $\frac{3}{2}(c_\psi - \frac{2}{3})^2 + \frac{16}{3} \ge \frac{16}{3} > 0$ are strictly positive for all real $c_\psi$, so the leading coefficient vanishes only at $c_\psi = 2$, and quadratically there), diverging for all $c_\psi \neq 2$ (whereas at $c_\psi = 2 \in [4 - 2\sqrt{3},\; 4 + 2\sqrt{3}]$, which is admissible as a power law only when $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}} \equiv 0$, $p_t = 1, p_r = 2$ and $ds_5^2 \sim -r^2 dr^2 - \Phi_\infty^2 r^4 dt_2^2 + dt_1^2 + d\Omega_2^2$ reduces under $\tau = r^2/2$ to the negative-definite polar plane product $\mathbb{R}^{0,2} \times \mathbb{R} \times S^2_1$ with polar angle $\theta_2 = 2\Phi_\infty t_2$ and exact constant Kretschmann scalar $\mathcal{K}_5 = \mathcal{K}_{S^2_1} = 4$, whose origin $\tau = 0$ on a periodic fiber is smooth if and only if $t_2$ has period $\pi/\Phi_\infty$) \catD.
  \item \textbf{Routhian Centrifugal Exponent Scaling $e^{-3\psi}/r^2$ (\texttt{COR~\#090} \catD) and Constitutive Source Parameterization in $\psi''(r)$ (\catH):}
  On the static Jordan-frame metric $ds_5^2$ of Theorem~\ref{thm:c5_static_radial_odes} (where $r$ is the Jordan areal radius, $g_{\theta\theta} = r^2$), multiplying the 5D fiber mass-squared term $P_2^2(-g^{22}_{\mathrm{proj}}) = P_2^2\Phi_\infty^{-2}e^{-2\psi}$ by the rescaled inverse angular metric $e^{-c_{\mathrm{Weyl}}\psi}g^{\theta\theta} = e^{-\psi}/r^2$ yields the exact conformal exponent count $V_{\mathrm{cent}} \propto e^{(-2-1)\psi}/r^2 = e^{-3\psi}/r^2$, matching $-2\beta_{\mathrm{chrono}} = -3$ of Theorem~\ref{thm:c3_geodesic_decomposition}(iii) (\texttt{COR~\#090}) \catD. Parameterizing the resulting macroscopically averaged $P_2 \neq 0$ chrono-helicity source in the interior radial scalar ODE with positive coupling amplitude $\mathcal{C}_\Omega > 0$ (\catH) gives:
  \begin{equation}\label{eq:c5_interior_centrifugal_ode}
  \begin{aligned}
  \psi''(r) + \left(\nu'(r) - \lambda'(r) + \frac{2}{r}\right)\psi'(r) &= +\frac{\mathcal{C}_\Omega}{r^2}\,e^{2\lambda(r) - 3\psi(r)} \\[3pt]
  &\quad - \frac{2}{3}\,e^{2\lambda(r)}\left(\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\psi} - \mathcal{A}\,\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\mathcal{A}}\right) \;\;\catD\text{ / }\catH,
  \end{aligned}
  \end{equation}
  which opposes inward fiber collapse ($\psi' > 0$) and, where a throat forms, bounds the causal 4D projection domain at $\Phi(r) \ge \Phi_{\mathrm{null}} = |P_2|/\epsilon_5 > 0$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:c3_geodesic_decomposition}(ii)) \catD.
  \item \textbf{Variational Null Contraction (\texttt{COR~\#089} \catD), Conditional 5D NEC Violation, and Curvature Boundedness on Regular Interior Domains $r \ge r_{\min} > 0$:}
  For $P_2 = 0$ radial null vectors $k^A = (k^1, 0, k^r, 0, 0)$ ($\tilde{g}_{11}(k^1)^2 + \tilde{g}_{rr}(k^r)^2 = 0 \implies e^{2\nu}(k^1)^2 = e^{2\lambda}(k^r)^2 > 0$, so $\mathcal{A}^2 e^\psi(k^1)^2 = \mathcal{A}(-e^{-\psi+2\nu})e^\psi e^{-2\nu}e^{2\lambda}(k^r)^2 = -\mathcal{A}\,e^{2\lambda}(k^r)^2$), contracting the Hilbert stress tensor of Eqs.~\eqref{eq:c3_master_tensor_eq}--\eqref{eq:c3_hilbert_stress_weights} yields the exact variational null contractions (\texttt{COR~\#089}):
  \begin{align}
  \tilde{T}_{\mu\nu}^{(\mathrm{tot})}k^\mu k^\nu &= \left[-\kappa_\psi\left(\psi'(r)\right)^2 - 2\,\mathcal{A}\,\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})}{\partial\mathcal{A}}\,e^{2\lambda(r)}\right](k^r)^2 \nonumber \\[3pt]
  &= \left[\frac{3}{2}\left(\psi'(r)\right)^2 - 2\,\mathcal{A}\,\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})}{\partial\mathcal{A}}\,e^{2\lambda(r)}\right](k^r)^2 \nonumber \\[2pt]
  &\qquad \left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{Killing}} = -e^{-\psi+2\nu} < 0,\; \textnormal{\texttt{COR~\#089}}\right) \;\catD, \nonumber \\[4pt]
  \tilde{T}_{\mu\nu}^{(\mathcal{V})}k^\mu k^\nu &= \left(\tilde{T}^1_{\;1(\mathcal{V})} - \tilde{T}^r_{\;r(\mathcal{V})}\right)e^{2\lambda(r)}(k^r)^2
  = -2\,\mathcal{A}\,\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})}{\partial\mathcal{A}}\,e^{2\lambda(r)}(k^r)^2 \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c5_interior_nec_violation}
  \end{align}
  Consequently, since $\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{Killing}} < 0$ and $\tilde{g}_{rr} = -e^{2\lambda(r)} < 0$ inside the horizon, Eq.~\eqref{eq:c5_interior_nec_violation} violates the 4D/5D Null Energy Condition ($\tilde{T}_{\mu\nu}^{(\mathrm{tot})}k^\mu k^\nu < 0$) if and only if $\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\mathcal{A}} < -\frac{3}{4}\frac{(\psi'(r))^2}{\mathcal{A}\,\tilde{g}_{rr}} = -\frac{3}{4}\frac{(\psi'(r))^2}{(-\mathcal{A})\,e^{2\lambda(r)}} < 0$ (\catD), where the realization of $\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\mathcal{A}} < 0$ ($\mathcal{A}\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\mathcal{A}} > 0$) follows from the equilibrium-root structure $\psi_t(\mathcal{A})$ of Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_three} together with its constitutive parameterization in Eq.~\eqref{eq:c4_gaussian_kernel} (\catH). Across any smooth solution on a regular physical domain $r \in [r_{\min}, \infty)$ bounded below by a positive inner-horizon or throat radius $r_{\min} > 0$ (such as the electro-vacuum inner Cauchy horizon $r_- > 0$ in Eq.~\eqref{eq:c5_inner_cauchy_horizon}) with $\Phi(r) \ge \Phi_{\min} > 0$, continuity of the curvature polynomials on $[r_{\min}, R]$ and asymptotic flatness as $r \to \infty$ guarantee that the five-dimensional Kretschmann invariant is strictly bounded:
  \begin{equation}\label{eq:c5_interior_kretschmann_bound}
  \sup_{r \ge r_{\min}}\left|\mathcal{K}_5(r)\right| = \sup_{r \ge r_{\min}}\left|\mathcal{K}_4(r) + \frac{4}{\Phi(r)^2}\left({}^{(4)}\nabla_\mu\partial_\nu\Phi\right)^2\right| < \infty \;\;\catD.
  \end{equation}
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}

\subsection{The $O(4)$-Symmetric 4D Radial Instanton Reduction ($4\pi^2 C_{\mathrm{throat}}$), the $\mathrm{ISO}(3,1)$ Warped Fiber Reduction ($G_{22}^{(5)} = +6(\sigma')^2 \ge 0$), and Andr\'eief Cauchy--Gram Loop Closure to Theorem~1.11 (\texttt{THM~\#23 \& \#24, COR~\#091--\#094})}

\begin{theorem}[{The $O(4)$ Radial Euler--Lagrange ODE, Short-Distance Harmonic Profile $\psi'(\rho) = C_{\mathrm{throat}}/\rho^3$, 1D Holonomy $\Delta\ell_\partial^{(n)} = 2n\ln\Phi_\infty$, and 4D $S^3_\rho$ Topological Flux $4\pi^2 C_{\mathrm{throat}}$ --- \texttt{THM~\#23, COR~\#091} \catD}]\label{thm:c5_o4_instanton}\label{thm:c5_o4_instanton_ode}
On $\mathcal{M}_4^E \simeq \mathbb{R}^4\setminus\{0\}$ with $O(4)$-invariant Euclidean metric $\widetilde{ds}_{4,E}^2 = d\rho^2 + \rho^2(d\chi^2 + \sin^2\chi\,d\theta^2 + \sin^2\chi\sin^2\theta\,d\varphi^2)$ ($\sqrt{g_{E,4}} = \rho^3\sin^2\chi\sin\theta$, $\mathrm{Vol}(S^3_1) = \int_0^\pi\sin^2\chi\,d\chi\int_0^\pi\sin\theta\,d\theta\int_0^{2\pi}d\varphi = 2\pi^2$) and $\psi = \psi(\rho)$, the radial Euler--Lagrange equation and its exact solution in the short-distance harmonic regime ($\rho \ll m_\psi^{-1}$, where the potential term $\frac{2}{3}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}'(\psi)$ of Eq.~\eqref{eq:c5_o4_radial_ode} is neglected) satisfy:
\begin{align}
\frac{1}{\rho^3}\frac{d}{d\rho}\!\left(\rho^3\frac{d\psi(\rho)}{d\rho}\right) &= \frac{d^2\psi}{d\rho^2} + \frac{3}{\rho}\frac{d\psi}{d\rho} = \frac{2}{3}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}'(\psi) \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c5_o4_radial_ode} \\[4pt]
\frac{d\psi(\rho)}{d\rho} &= \frac{C_{\mathrm{throat}}}{\rho^3}, \qquad
C_{\mathrm{throat}} = \frac{2\ln\Phi_\infty}{\rho_{\min}^{-2} - \rho_{\max}^{-2}} \quad (\textnormal{harmonic regime }\rho \ll m_\psi^{-1}) \;\;\catD, \nonumber \\[4pt]
\Delta\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{GHY}}^{(S^3)} &= (-c_{\mathrm{GC}})\oint_{S^3_\rho}\rho^3\psi'(\rho)\,d\Omega_3 = 2\,\mathrm{Vol}(S^3_1)\,C_{\mathrm{throat}} = 4\pi^2 C_{\mathrm{throat}} \quad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#091})} \;\catD. \label{eq:c5_o4_s3_flux}
\end{align}
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[{Exact Fiber-Coordinate Ricci and Einstein ODEs, Positivity $G_{22}^{(5)} = +6(\sigma')^2 \ge 0$, the Piecewise-Linear Warp Profile $\sigma(x^2) = \kappa|x^2|$, Andr\'eief Finite-$L_0$ Gram Positivity for Arbitrary Distinct $\mu_a > 0$, and Exact Asymptotic UV Loop Closure to the Cauchy--Vandermonde Minor of Theorem~1.11 --- \texttt{THM~\#24, COR~\#092--\#094} \catD}]\label{thm:c5_iso31_fiber_closure}\label{thm:c5_iso31_orbifold_localization}
For the $\mathrm{ISO}(3,1)$-invariant ($\partial_\mu \equiv 0$) $\mathbb{Z}_2$-even warped metric $ds_5^2 = e^{-2\sigma(x^2)}\eta_{\mu\nu}dx^\mu dx^\nu - \Phi_\infty^2(dx^2)^2$ on $\mathcal{S}_{t_2} = [0, \pi L_0]$ ($\sigma' \equiv d\sigma/dx^2$), the non-vanishing Christoffel symbols $\Gamma^2_{\mu\nu} = -\frac{\sigma'}{\Phi_\infty^2}e^{-2\sigma}\eta_{\mu\nu}$ and $\Gamma^\mu_{\nu 2} = -\sigma'\delta^\mu_\nu$ yield the exact Ricci and Einstein ODEs (\texttt{COR~\#092}):
\begin{align}
R_{\mu\nu}^{(5)} &= -\frac{e^{-2\sigma(x^2)}}{\Phi_\infty^2}\left[\sigma''(x^2) - 4\left(\sigma'(x^2)\right)^2\right]\eta_{\mu\nu}, \qquad
R_{22}^{(5)} = 4\left[\sigma''(x^2) - \left(\sigma'(x^2)\right)^2\right] \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c5_iso31_ricci} \\[4pt]
G_{\mu\nu}^{(5)} &= \frac{3\,e^{-2\sigma(x^2)}}{\Phi_\infty^2}\left[\sigma''(x^2) - 2\left(\sigma'(x^2)\right)^2\right]\eta_{\mu\nu} \;\;\catD, \qquad
G_{22}^{(5)} = +6\left(\sigma'(x^2)\right)^2 \ge 0 \quad \textnormal{(\texttt{COR~\#092})} \;\catD. \label{eq:c5_iso31_einstein}
\end{align}
For constant bulk invariant $G_{22}^{(5)} = 6\kappa^2$ ($\kappa > 0$), $\sigma(x^2) = \kappa|x^2|$ satisfies the Israel--Lanczos--Darmois jump condition $\sigma''(x^2) = 2\kappa[\delta(x^2) - \delta(x^2 - \pi L_0)]$. Furthermore, for boundary-localized zero-mode profiles $\chi_a(x^2) = N_a e^{-\mu_a x^2}$ ($a \in \{1, 2, 3\}$, $\mu_a > 0$, $N_k = \sqrt{2\mu_k/(1 - e^{-2\pi\mu_k L_0})}$) on $[0, \pi L_0]$, the opposite-boundary overlap $\mathcal{Y}_{ij}$ and the same-boundary Yukawa Gram kernel $K_L(\mu_a, \mu_b) \equiv \int_0^{\pi L_0} e^{-(\mu_a+\mu_b)x^2}dx^2$ satisfy the exact continuous Cauchy--Binet (Andr\'eief) determinant identity for all $k \in \{1, 2, 3\}$ (with $D_k(\mathbf{y}) \equiv \det_{1\le a,b\le k}[e^{-\mu_a y_b}]$ having mixed Taylor coefficient at $\mathbf{y} = \mathbf{0}$ equal up to sign to the Vandermonde product $\prod_{1\le a<b\le k}(\mu_a - \mu_b) \neq 0$, proving strict positivity $\det K_L|_{[1..k,1..k]} > 0$ symbolically for all $L_0 > 0$ and arbitrary distinct $\mu_a > 0$, \texttt{COR~\#093}), and close the deductive loop in the UV limit $L_0 \to \infty$ back to the exact Cauchy--Vandermonde determinant minor $M_3(\mu_1, \mu_2, \mu_3)$ of Theorem~\ref{thm:c1_klein_and_cauchy}(iii) (\texttt{THM~\#07, COR~\#040} $\longleftrightarrow$ \texttt{THM~\#24, COR~\#094}):
\begin{align}
    &\begin{aligned}
    &\quad \mathcal{Y}_{ij}(\mu_i, \mu_j) \equiv \int_0^{\pi L_0} N_i e^{-\mu_i x^2}\,N_j e^{-\mu_j(\pi L_0 - x^2)}\,dx^2 \\
    &\qquad = \frac{2\sqrt{\mu_i\mu_j}}{\mu_i - \mu_j}\,\frac{e^{-\pi\mu_j L_0} - e^{-\pi\mu_i L_0}}{\sqrt{\bigl(1 - e^{-2\pi\mu_i L_0}\bigr)\bigl(1 - e^{-2\pi\mu_j L_0}\bigr)}} \quad \catD, \\[3pt]
    &\quad K_L(\mu_a, \mu_b) = \frac{1 - e^{-\pi L_0(\mu_a + \mu_b)}}{\mu_a + \mu_b}, \qquad
    \lim_{L_0 \to \infty} K_L(\mu_a, \mu_b) = \frac{1}{\mu_a + \mu_b} \quad (\text{\texttt{COR~\#093}}) \;\catD, \\[3pt]
    &\quad \det_{1\le a,b\le k}\!\big[K_L(\mu_a, \mu_b)\big] = \frac{1}{k!}\int_{[0, \pi L_0]^k} D_k(\mathbf{y})^2\,d^k y > 0 \;\; (\forall k \in \{1, 2, 3\}) \quad \catD, \\[3pt]
    &\quad \left(\prod_{b=1}^k\frac{\partial^{b-1}}{\partial y_b^{b-1}}D_k(\mathbf{y})\Bigg|_{\mathbf{y}=\mathbf{0}}\right)^2 = \prod_{1\le a<b\le k}(\mu_a - \mu_b)^2 > 0 \quad (\text{\texttt{COR~\#093}}) \;\catD,
    \end{aligned} \label{eq:c5_yukawa_overlap} \\[4pt]
    &\begin{aligned}
    &\quad \det C_{\mathrm{UV}}(\bm{\mu}) \equiv \frac{\det G^{\mathrm{UV}}(\bm{\mu})}{\prod_{a=1}^3 G_{aa}^{\mathrm{UV}}} \\
    &\qquad = \frac{(\mu_1 - \mu_2)^2(\mu_1 - \mu_3)^2(\mu_2 - \mu_3)^2}{(\mu_1 + \mu_2)^2(\mu_1 + \mu_3)^2(\mu_2 + \mu_3)^2} \equiv M_3^{(\text{\texttt{THM~\#07}})} \quad (\text{\texttt{COR~\#094}}) \;\catD.
    \end{aligned} \label{eq:c5_cauchy_loop_closure}
\end{align}
\end{theorem}

\begin{remark}[{Identification of Section~5 Symmetry Reductions with Recognized Mathematical Theorems}]\label{rem:c5_symmetry_recognition}
The seven Lie symmetry reductions deduced in Section~5 correspond to classical theorems of differential geometry and ordinary differential equations:
\begin{enumerate}
  \item \textbf{Kasner's Bianchi-I Shear Integral (1921), Nullspace Raychaudhuri Ratio $4\kappa_\psi = -6$, Minisuperspace Noether Closure, and 2D Laplace--Beltrami Ordering $p \equiv 1$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:c5_homogeneous_odes}):} Equation~\eqref{eq:c5_addot_identity} establishes the nullspace Raychaudhuri ratio $4\kappa_\psi = -6$; Eq.~\eqref{eq:c5_minisuperspace_noether} establishes $\dot{\Theta}\mathcal{E}_\Theta - N\dot{\mathcal{E}}_N + \dot{a}\mathcal{E}_a + \dot{\psi}\mathcal{E}_\psi \equiv 0$ and $\dot{\mathcal{C}}_{\mathrm{Fried}} = -(3H + \dot{\psi})\mathcal{C}_{\mathrm{Fried}}$ (with a declared conformally coupled radiation test fluid); Eqs.~\eqref{eq:c5_yamabe_2d_zero}--\eqref{eq:c5_wdw_laplace_beltrami} prove $d_{\mathrm{mini}} = d_t = 2 \implies p \equiv 1$; and Eq.~\eqref{eq:c5_bianchi_shear_integral} generalizes Kasner's shear integral to $\mathcal{M}_4 \times_\Phi \mathcal{S}_{t_2}$ \catD.
  \item \textbf{Helmholtz--Hodge Decomposition, Trace-Inversion Unification $\alpha_0^2 = \frac{1}{3} = -c_{\mathrm{DW}}^{-1}\big|_{\lambda=1/2}$, and Screened Yukawa Resolvent (Theorem~\ref{thm:c5_sturm_liouville_perturbation}):} Equation~\eqref{eq:c5_no_slip_identity} proves $\Phi_N = \Psi_N$, while Eqs.~\eqref{eq:c5_effective_poisson_kernel}--\eqref{eq:c5_jordan_slip_kernel} bound the quasi-static effective growth kernel within $\mu_{\mathrm{eff}}(a, k) \in [1, \frac{4}{3})$ and the Jordan slip within $\eta_J(a, k) \in (\frac{1}{2}, 1]$ \catD.
  \item \textbf{Palais' Symmetric Criticality (1979), Bekenstein Star-Shaped Domain $(-\infty, \frac{1}{2}\ln 3)$, JNW Horizon Theorem Under Axiom~5.3, Frobenius Pole Cancellation ($\mathcal{C}_{B^2} + 1/b_{-1} \equiv 0$), and Pole-Free Stiff-Limit Reduction (Theorem~\ref{thm:c5_static_radial_odes}):} Proves that $\psi\,\Pi_2'(\psi) > 0$ holds on $(-\infty, \frac{1}{2}\ln 3)\setminus\{0\}$ up to barrier height $\frac{1}{54}$, proves that Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_three} forces $\left.\psi'(r_h)\right|_{\partial_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{V}=0} = 0$ ($\psi(r) \equiv 0$) unless $\frac{\partial\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}}{\partial\mathcal{A}} \neq 0$~\cite{Palais1979,JNW1968,Bekenstein1972}, proves symbolic cancellation of $\mathcal{C}_{B^2} + 1/b_{-1} \equiv 0$ with finite slope $\psi'(r_h)$ (Eq.~\ref{eq:c5_frobenius_pole_cancellation}), and proves pole-free stiff reduction via $\frac{B'}{B} = \frac{P'}{P} - \frac{A'}{A}$ (Eq.~\ref{eq:c5_stiff_limit_ode}) \catD.
  \item \textbf{Kottler--RN--de Sitter Integration, Inner Horizon $r_- > 0$, and Surface Gravity (Theorem~\ref{thm:c5_electro_vacuum}):} Yields $f(r) = 1 - r_h/r + Q_e^2/(4r^2) - H_\infty^2 r^2$, $r_- = \frac{1}{2}(r_h - \sqrt{r_h^2 - Q_e^2}) > 0$ ($r_+ r_- = Q_e^2/4$), and $\kappa_{\mathrm{dS}} = H_\infty$ ($a_{\mathrm{dS}} = \frac{H_\infty}{2\pi}$) \catD.
  \item \textbf{Interior Centrifugal Exponent Count $e^{-3\psi}/r^2$ and Conditional Khronon Violation of the 5D Null Energy Condition (Theorem~\ref{thm:c5_interior_continuation}):} The Jordan-frame fiber mass term $e^{-2\psi}$ and rescaled inverse angular metric $e^{-\psi}/r^2$ give the exponent count $e^{-3\psi}/r^2$ \catD, while the variational null contraction~\eqref{eq:c5_interior_nec_violation} violates the NEC if and only if $\partial_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}} < -\frac{3}{4}(\psi')^2/(\mathcal{A}\tilde{g}_{rr})$ \catD\ (with $\partial_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}} < 0$ supplied by the equilibrium-root structure \catH).
  \item \textbf{Coleman--Glaser--Martin $O(4)$ Radial Symmetry, Israel Junction Localization, and Andr\'eief Cauchy--Gram Loop Closure (Theorems~\ref{thm:c5_o4_instanton}--\ref{thm:c5_iso31_fiber_closure}):} Integrates $\rho^3\psi'(\rho) = C_{\mathrm{throat}}$ in the short-distance harmonic regime ($\Delta\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{GHY}}^{(S^3)} = 4\pi^2 C_{\mathrm{throat}}$)~\cite{Coleman1977}, derives $G_{22}^{(5)} = +6(\sigma')^2 \ge 0$ and the Israel jump $\sigma'' = 2\kappa[\delta(x^2) - \delta(x^2 - \pi L_0)]$~\cite{Israel1966}, proves finite-$L_0$ Gram positivity for arbitrary distinct $\mu_a > 0$ via the Andr\'eief identity, and closes the UV Yukawa Gram determinant back to the Cauchy--Vandermonde minor $M_3$ of Theorem~\ref{thm:c1_klein_and_cauchy} \catD.
\end{enumerate}
\end{remark}

\section{\texorpdfstring{The Universal Operator Pyramid, Real $\mathrm{Cl}(3,2)$ Spinor Geometry, Curved 5-Bein Lichnerowicz--Weitzenb\"ock Identity, and Spectral Action Emergence of $\mathcal{I}_{3,2}$ \catD}{The Universal Operator Pyramid, Real Cl(3,2) Spinor Geometry, Curved 5-Bein Lichnerowicz-Weitzenbock Identity, and Spectral Action Emergence of I\_\{3,2\}}}
\label{sec:c6_operator_pyramid}

In Sections~\ref{sec:c1_linear_algebra}--\ref{sec:c5_symmetry_reductions}, the algebraic projectors ($\mathbb{I}_5, \mathbf{P}_{\mathcal{T}_2}, \mathbf{P}_{\Sigma_3}, \mathbf{R}_{\mathbb{Z}_2}$), the $15\times 15$ trace-inversion supermetric $\mathbf{G}_{\mathrm{DW}} = \mathbf{G}_{1/2}$ ($c_{\mathrm{DW}}^{-1}=-1/3$), the symplectic operator $\mathbf{J}_{30}$, and the configuration-space grading operator $\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{17}(\Phi)$ were constructed independently, while the Master Action $\mathcal{I}_{3,2}$ was defined variationally in Definition~\ref{def:c3_master_action}. In this culminating mathematical section (Stage~6), we complete the tensor-product algebra of composite super-operators ($\widehat{\mathbf{\Omega}}_{150}$ and $\widehat{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{510}$), construct the unique real $4\times 4$ Majorana--symplectic spinor representation of $\mathrm{Cl}(3,2)$ (\texttt{THM~\#05, COR~\#030--\#033}), prove all 25 non-diagonal curved spinorial tetrad postulates and the curved 5-bein Lichnerowicz--Weitzenb\"ock identity for the Dirac--Kostant operator $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}$ (\texttt{THM~\#15, COR~\#067--\#069}), and demonstrate under \textbf{Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_two}} (\texttt{AXIOM\_2}) that the gravitational, scalar, gauge, and potential sectors of $\mathcal{I}_{3,2}$ emerge automatically from the asymptotic trace of the Master Heat-Kernel Operator $\widehat{\mathcal{K}}_{\mathrm{JV}}(s) \equiv \exp(-s\,\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2)$ \catD.

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Tensor-Product Super-Operators $\widehat{\mathbf{\Omega}}_{150}$ ($150\times 150$) and $\widehat{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{510}(\Phi)$ ($510\times 510$)}{Tensor-Product Super-Operators Omega\_150 and Gamma\_510}}

\begin{theorem}[Composite Kronecker Super-Operators $\widehat{\mathbf{\Omega}}_{150}$ and $\widehat{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{510}(\Phi)$, Gram Determinants, and Rational Configuration-Symplectic Invariants \catD\ / \catH]\label{thm:c6_composite_superoperators}
Tensoring the tangent identity $\mathbb{I}_5$ on $T_p\mathcal{M}_{3,2}$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:c1_tangent_traces}), the $17\times 17$ McKean--Singer configuration grading operator $\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{17}(\Phi) = \Phi^{-1}\mathbb{I}_{15} \oplus (2\Phi)^{-1}\mathbb{I}_2$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:c4_zeta_winding_q17_identities}(iii)), and the $30\times 30$ canonical symplectic operator $\mathbf{J}_{30}$ on $T^*_g\mathrm{Met}(\mathcal{M}_{3,2})$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:c1_j30_diophantine}) defines two composite super-operators:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
  \item \textbf{The $150\times 150$ Tangent--Symplectic Equipartition Operator $\widehat{\mathbf{\Omega}}_{150}$ on $T_p\mathcal{M}_{3,2} \otimes T^*_g\mathrm{Met}(\mathcal{M}_{3,2})$ (\catD):}
  \begin{align}
  \widehat{\mathbf{\Omega}}_{150} &\equiv \mathbb{I}_5 \otimes \left(\mathbf{J}_{30}^T\,\mathbf{J}_{30}\right) = \mathbb{I}_{150}, \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \mathrm{Tr}\!\left(\widehat{\mathbf{\Omega}}_{150}\right) &= D \cdot \mathcal{N}_{\mathrm{Cartan}} = 5 \times 30 = 150, \qquad
  \det\!\left(\widehat{\mathbf{\Omega}}_{150}\right) = +1 \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c6_omega150_invariants}
  \end{align}
  \item \textbf{The $510\times 510$ Configuration--Symplectic Super-Operator $\widehat{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{510}(\Phi)$ (\catD\ / \catH):}
  \begin{align}
  \widehat{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{510}(\Phi) &\equiv \widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{17}(\Phi) \otimes \mathbf{J}_{30}, \qquad
  \dim\widehat{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{510} = \mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{config}}\cdot\mathcal{N}_{\mathrm{Cartan}} = 17 \times 30 = 510 \;\;\catH, \nonumber \\[4pt]
  \mathrm{Tr}\!\left(\widehat{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{510}(\Phi)\right) &= 0 \;\;\catD, \qquad
  \widehat{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{510}(\Phi)^T\,\widehat{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{510}(\Phi) = \widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{17}(\Phi)^2 \otimes \mathbb{I}_{30} \;\;\catD, \nonumber \\[4pt]
  \mathrm{Tr}\!\left(\widehat{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{510}^T\,\widehat{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{510}\right) &= \frac{465}{\Phi^2} \;\;\catD, \qquad
  \left|\det\!\left(\widehat{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{510}(\Phi)\right)\right| = 4^{-30}\,\Phi^{-510} > 0 \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c6_gamma510_invariants}
  \end{align}
  \item \textbf{Rational Bulk-Configuration Partition Invariants ($\eta_{\mathrm{EM}} = \frac{15}{17}$ and $d_t^2\mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{config}} = 68$, \catH):}
  \begin{align}
  \eta_{\mathrm{EM}} &\equiv \frac{N_{\mathrm{sym}}}{\mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{config}}} = \frac{15}{17} \;\;\catH, \qquad
  1 - \eta_{\mathrm{EM}} = \frac{N_\partial}{\mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{config}}} = \frac{2}{17} \;\;\catH, \nonumber \\[4pt]
  \frac{2\,\mathcal{N}_{\mathrm{Cartan}}}{\eta_{\mathrm{EM}}} &= \frac{60}{15/17} = d_t^2\,\mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{config}} = 4 \times 17 = 68 \;\;\catH. \label{eq:c6_rational_68_invariant}
  \end{align}
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Since $\mathbf{J}_{30}^T\mathbf{J}_{30} = \mathbb{I}_{30}$ by Eq.~\eqref{eq:c1_j30_complex}, $\mathbb{I}_5 \otimes (\mathbf{J}_{30}^T\mathbf{J}_{30}) = \mathbb{I}_{150}$ has trace $5 \times 30 = 150$ and determinant $+1$. For $\widehat{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{510} = \widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{17} \otimes \mathbf{J}_{30}$ ($17 \times 30 = 510$), Kronecker product transposition gives $\widehat{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{510}^T\widehat{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{510} = (\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{17}^T\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{17}) \otimes (\mathbf{J}_{30}^T\mathbf{J}_{30}) = \widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{17}^2 \otimes \mathbb{I}_{30}$, whose trace is $30\,\mathrm{Tr}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{17}^2) = 30\big(\frac{15}{\Phi^2} + \frac{2}{4\Phi^2}\big) = \frac{465}{\Phi^2}$ and whose determinant is $(\det\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{17})^{60}(\det\mathbf{J}_{30})^{34} = (4^{-1}\Phi^{-17})^{60} = 4^{-60}\Phi^{-1020}$, proving Eqs.~\eqref{eq:c6_gamma510_invariants}--\eqref{eq:c6_rational_68_invariant}.
\end{proof}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{Purely Real $4\times 4$ Representation of $\mathrm{Cl}(3,2)$, $\mathrm{Spin}_0(3,2) \simeq \mathrm{Sp}(4,\mathbb{R})$, Real Spectral Triple of KO-Dimension $1$, Robin Localization, and Rank-$3$ K\"ahler--Dirac Family (\texttt{THM~\#05, COR~\#030--\#033})}{Purely Real 4x4 Representation of Cl(3,2), Spin\_0(3,2) = Sp(4,R), KO-Dimension 1, Robin Localization, and Rank-3 Kahler-Dirac Family}}

\begin{theorem}[Explicit Real $4\times 4$ Basis of $\mathrm{Cl}(3,2)$, Symplectic Isomorphism $\mathrm{Spin}_0(3,2) \simeq \mathrm{Sp}(4,\mathbb{R})$, KO-Dimension $d_s - d_t \equiv 1 \pmod 8$, Robin Boundary Localization, and Rank-$3$ Frobenius-Orthonormal K\"ahler--Dirac Operator Family --- \texttt{THM~\#05, COR~\#030--\#033} \catD\ / \catH]\label{thm:c6_clifford_sp4_spinors}
Let $\mathbb{I}_2 = \begin{psmallmatrix} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & 1 \end{psmallmatrix}$, $\varepsilon = \begin{psmallmatrix} 0 & -1 \\ 1 & 0 \end{psmallmatrix}$ ($\varepsilon^2 = -\mathbb{I}_2$), $\sigma_x = \begin{psmallmatrix} 0 & 1 \\ 1 & 0 \end{psmallmatrix}$, and $\sigma_z = \begin{psmallmatrix} 1 & 0 \\ 0 & -1 \end{psmallmatrix}$ be the elementary $2\times 2$ real matrices, and let $\eta_{\hat{a}\hat{b}} = \mathrm{diag}(-1, -1, +1, +1, +1)$ ($\hat{a}, \hat{b} \in \{1,2,3,4,5\}$) be the orthonormal bitemporal frame metric of Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_one}. Then (\texttt{COR~\#030--\#033}):
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
  \item \textbf{Explicit Purely Real $4\times 4$ Majorana--Symplectic Basis of $\mathrm{Cl}(3,2) \simeq \mathrm{Mat}(4,\mathbb{R})_+ \oplus \mathrm{Mat}(4,\mathbb{R})_-$ (\texttt{COR~\#030}):}
  The five purely real $4\times 4$ Kronecker matrices $\Gamma^{\hat{a}} \in \mathrm{Mat}(4, \mathbb{R})$:
  \begin{align}
  \Gamma^{\hat{1}} &\equiv \varepsilon \otimes \mathbb{I}_2, \qquad
  \Gamma^{\hat{2}} \equiv \sigma_z \otimes \varepsilon, \qquad
  \Gamma^{\hat{3}} \equiv \sigma_x \otimes \mathbb{I}_2, \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \Gamma^{\hat{4}} &\equiv \sigma_z \otimes \sigma_x, \qquad
  \Gamma^{\hat{5}} \equiv \sigma_z \otimes \sigma_z, \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \left\{\Gamma^{\hat{a}}, \Gamma^{\hat{b}}\right\} &\equiv \Gamma^{\hat{a}}\Gamma^{\hat{b}} + \Gamma^{\hat{b}}\Gamma^{\hat{a}} = 2\,\eta^{\hat{a}\hat{b}}\,\mathbb{I}_4 \qquad \forall\,\hat{a},\hat{b} \in \{1,\dots,5\} \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c6_clifford_real_basis}
  \end{align}
  generate the real Clifford algebra $\mathrm{Cl}(3,2)$, with central volume element $\Gamma_{\mathrm{vol}} \equiv -\Gamma^{\hat{1}}\Gamma^{\hat{2}}\Gamma^{\hat{3}}\Gamma^{\hat{4}}\Gamma^{\hat{5}} = +\mathbb{I}_4$ satisfying:
  \begin{align}
  \Gamma_{\mathrm{vol}}^2 &= +\mathbb{I}_4, \qquad
  \left[\Gamma_{\mathrm{vol}}, \Gamma^{\hat{a}}\right] = \mathbf{0}_4 \quad (\forall\,\hat{a}) \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \Longrightarrow \quad \mathrm{Cl}(3,2) &\simeq \mathrm{Mat}(4, \mathbb{R})_+ \oplus \mathrm{Mat}(4, \mathbb{R})_- \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c6_central_volume_element}
  \end{align}

  \item \textbf{Canonical Symplectic Form $\Omega_4 = \Gamma^{\hat{1}}\Gamma^{\hat{2}}$ and Lie-Algebra Isomorphism $\mathfrak{spin}(3,2) \simeq \mathfrak{sp}(4, \mathbb{R})$ (\texttt{COR~\#031}):}
  Because $\Gamma^{\hat{1}}, \Gamma^{\hat{2}}$ are skew-symmetric ($(\Gamma^{\hat{a}})^T = -\Gamma^{\hat{a}}$) and $\Gamma^{\hat{3}}, \Gamma^{\hat{4}}, \Gamma^{\hat{5}}$ are symmetric ($(\Gamma^{\hat{a}})^T = +\Gamma^{\hat{a}}$), the bitemporal bivector $\Omega_4 \equiv \Gamma^{\hat{1}}\Gamma^{\hat{2}} \in \mathrm{Mat}(4,\mathbb{R})$ satisfies $\Omega_4^T = -\Omega_4$, $\Omega_4^2 = -\mathbb{I}_4$, $\det\Omega_4 = +1$, and $(\Gamma^{\hat{a}})^T\Omega_4 = +\Omega_4\Gamma^{\hat{a}}$ for all $\hat{a} \in \{1,\dots,5\}$. Consequently, the $\binom{5}{2} = 10$ linearly independent commutator generators $\Sigma^{\hat{a}\hat{b}} \equiv \frac{1}{4}[\Gamma^{\hat{a}}, \Gamma^{\hat{b}}]$ ($\hat{a} < \hat{b}$) preserve $\Omega_4$ identically:
  \begin{align}
  \left(\Sigma^{\hat{a}\hat{b}}\right)^T \Omega_4 + \Omega_4\,\Sigma^{\hat{a}\hat{b}} &= \mathbf{0}_4 \qquad (1 \le \hat{a} < \hat{b} \le 5), \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \mathrm{rank}\!\left\{\Sigma^{\hat{a}\hat{b}}\right\} &= 10 = \dim\mathfrak{sp}(4, \mathbb{R}) \quad \Longrightarrow \quad
  \mathrm{Spin}_0(3,2) \simeq \mathrm{Sp}(4, \mathbb{R}) \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c6_sp4_isomorphism}
  \end{align}

  \item \textbf{Connes Real Spectral Triple of KO-Dimension $d_s - d_t \equiv 1 \pmod 8$ (\texttt{COR~\#032}):}
  On the real spinor Hilbert space $\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{Sp}(4)} = L^2(\mathcal{M}_{3,2}, \mathcal{S}_4)$ equipped with the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-equivariant algebra $\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{CBG}} = C^\infty_+(\mathcal{M}_{3,2})$ and canonical real structure $\mathcal{J}_{\mathrm{real}}^2 = +\mathbb{I}_4$ ($(\Gamma^{\hat{a}})^* = \Gamma^{\hat{a}}$) \cite{Connes1996}, the signature difference of Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_one} fixes the real KO-dimension modulo $8$ uniquely to:
  \begin{equation}\label{eq:c6_ko_dimension_one}
  \dim_{\mathrm{KO}}\!\left(\mathcal{A}_{\mathrm{CBG}}, \mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{Sp}(4)}, \widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}\right) \equiv d_s - d_t = 3 - 2 \equiv 1 \pmod 8 \;\;\catD.
  \end{equation}

  \item \textbf{Robin Boundary Spinor Localization, Rank-$3$ Frobenius-Orthonormal K\"ahler--Dirac Family (\texttt{COR~\#033} \catD), and Three-Generation Bridge Identification (\catH$_1$):}
  Because $\{\Gamma^{\hat{2}}, \Gamma^{\hat{\mu}}\} = \mathbf{0}_4$ for all four macroscopic indices $\hat{\mu} \in \{1, 3, 4, 5\}$ and $(\Gamma^{\hat{2}})^2 = -\mathbb{I}_4$ (\texttt{COR~\#033}), $\Gamma^{\hat{2}}M_f$ commutes with $\mathrm{Spin}_0(1,3)$ as a 4D Lorentz-scalar mass term, and the fiber zero-mode equation $(\partial_{x^2} + M_f)\chi_{M_f}(x^2) = 0$ on $\mathcal{S}_{t_2} = [0, \pi L_0]$ integrates to the exponential Robin boundary profile:
  \begin{equation}\label{eq:c6_robin_localization_ode}
  \left\{\Gamma^{\hat{2}}, \Gamma^{\hat{\mu}}\right\} = \mathbf{0}_4, \qquad
  \left(\frac{d}{dx^2} + M_f\right)e^{-M_f x^2} \equiv 0 \;\;\catD,
  \end{equation}
  generating the exact overlap matrix $\mathcal{Y}_{ij}(\mu_i, \mu_j)$ and Cauchy--Gram kernel $K_L(\mu_a, \mu_b)$ of Theorem~\ref{thm:c5_iso31_orbifold_localization}. Simultaneously, pairing the fiber generator $\Gamma^{\hat{2}}$ with the $d_s = 3$ spatial generators $\Gamma^{\hat{g}+2}$ ($g \in \{1, 2, 3\}$) defines three traceless involutive spatial-fiber K\"ahler--Dirac operators $\mathcal{M}_g \equiv \frac{1}{2}\Gamma^{\hat{2}}\Gamma^{\hat{g}+2}$ whose normalized Frobenius inner product $G_{gh}^{(\mathrm{KD})} \equiv \frac{1}{4}\mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{R}^4}\!\big[(2\mathcal{M}_g)^T(2\mathcal{M}_h)\big] = \delta_{gh}$ equals the $3\times 3$ identity matrix $\mathbb{I}_3$, proving that $\{\mathcal{M}_1, \mathcal{M}_2, \mathcal{M}_3\}$ is Frobenius-orthonormal of exact algebraic rank $d_s = 3$ (\texttt{COR~\#033} \catD), while its physical identification with the $N_{\mathrm{gen}} = 3$ chiral fermion families belongs to Bridge Hypothesis~\catH$_1$:
  \begin{align}
  \left(2\,\mathcal{M}_g\right)^2 &= +\mathbb{I}_4 \;\;\catD, \qquad
  \mathrm{Tr}\!\left(\mathcal{M}_g\right) = 0 \;\;\catD, \nonumber \\[3pt]
  G_{gh}^{(\mathrm{KD})} &\equiv \frac{1}{4}\mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{R}^4}\!\left[(2\mathcal{M}_g)^T(2\mathcal{M}_h)\right] = \delta_{gh} \;\;\catD, \nonumber \\[3pt]
  \mathrm{rank}\!\left\{\mathcal{M}_1, \mathcal{M}_2, \mathcal{M}_3\right\} &= \mathrm{rank}\!\left(\mathbf{G}_{\mathrm{KD}}\right) = d_s = 3 \;\;\catD
  \qquad \left(\leftrightarrow N_{\mathrm{gen}} = 3 \;\;\catH_1\right). \label{eq:c6_kahler_dirac_ngen3}
  \end{align}
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Direct multiplication of the five Kronecker matrices in Eq.~\eqref{eq:c6_clifford_real_basis} gives $(\Gamma^{\hat{1}})^2 = (\Gamma^{\hat{2}})^2 = -\mathbb{I}_4$, $(\Gamma^{\hat{3}})^2 = (\Gamma^{\hat{4}})^2 = (\Gamma^{\hat{5}})^2 = +\mathbb{I}_4$, all 20 off-diagonal anticommutators $\{\Gamma^{\hat{a}}, \Gamma^{\hat{b}}\} = \mathbf{0}_4$ ($\hat{a} \neq \hat{b}$), and $\Gamma_{\mathrm{vol}} = +\mathbb{I}_4$ ($\Gamma_{\mathrm{vol}}^2 = +\mathbb{I}_4$). For $\Omega_4 = \Gamma^{\hat{1}}\Gamma^{\hat{2}}$, $\Omega_4^T = (\Gamma^{\hat{2}})^T(\Gamma^{\hat{1}})^T = \Gamma^{\hat{2}}\Gamma^{\hat{1}} = -\Omega_4$ and $(\Sigma^{\hat{a}\hat{b}})^T\Omega_4 = \frac{1}{4}\Omega_4(\Gamma^{\hat{b}}\Gamma^{\hat{a}} - \Gamma^{\hat{a}}\Gamma^{\hat{b}}) = -\Omega_4\Sigma^{\hat{a}\hat{b}}$, with Gaussian elimination confirming $\mathrm{rank}\{\Sigma^{\hat{a}\hat{b}}\} = 10$. For $2\mathcal{M}_g = \Gamma^{\hat{2}}\Gamma^{\hat{g}+2}$, $(2\mathcal{M}_g)^T = (\Gamma^{\hat{g}+2})^T(\Gamma^{\hat{2}})^T = -\Gamma^{\hat{g}+2}\Gamma^{\hat{2}} = +\Gamma^{\hat{2}}\Gamma^{\hat{g}+2} = 2\mathcal{M}_g$, so $\frac{1}{4}\mathrm{Tr}((2\mathcal{M}_g)^T(2\mathcal{M}_h)) = \frac{1}{4}\mathrm{Tr}(\Gamma^{\hat{g}+2}\Gamma^{\hat{h}+2}) = \delta_{gh}$, proving $\mathbf{G}_{\mathrm{KD}} = \mathbb{I}_3$ and $\mathrm{rank}\{\mathcal{M}_1, \mathcal{M}_2, \mathcal{M}_3\} = 3$ \catD.
\end{proof}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{The 5D Dirac--Kostant Operator $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}$, All 25 Non-Diagonal Curved Spinorial Tetrad Postulates, and Curved 5-Bein Proof of the Lichnerowicz--Weitzenb\"ock Identity (\texttt{THM~\#15, COR~\#067--\#068})}{The 5D Dirac-Kostant Operator, All 25 Non-Diagonal Curved Spinorial Tetrad Postulates, and Curved 5-Bein Proof of the Lichnerowicz-Weitzenbock Identity}}

\begin{theorem}[All 25 Non-Diagonal Curved 5-Bein Spinorial Tetrad Postulates, Zero First-Order Residual, Elliptic Wick Symbol, and Exact Curved-Metric Lichnerowicz--Weitzenb\"ock Identity for $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2$ ($c_{R_5}=1/4, c_{\mathrm{Pauli}}=1/2$) --- \texttt{THM~\#15, COR~\#067--\#068} \catD]\label{thm:c6_lichnerowicz_weitzenbock}
Let $e^{\hat{a}}_A(x)$ be a general curved non-diagonal orthonormal 5-bein ($g_{AB}^{\mathrm{proj}} = \eta_{\hat{a}\hat{b}}\,e^{\hat{a}}_A e^{\hat{b}}_B$, inverse $e^A_{\hat{a}}$, with non-zero Painlev\'e--Gullstrand shift $\beta_e(x^1, r)$, lapse $\alpha_e(x^1, r)$, fiber scale $\phi_e(x^1, r)$, radial scale $b_{1e}(x^1, r)$, and curved Clifford matrices $\Gamma^A(x) \equiv e^A_{\hat{a}}(x)\Gamma^{\hat{a}}$ satisfying $\{\Gamma^A, \Gamma^B\} = 2\,g^{AB}_{\mathrm{proj}}\mathbb{I}_4$) on $\mathcal{M}_{3,2}$. Let $\omega_{A\,\hat{b}\hat{c}} \equiv e_{\hat{b}C}(\partial_A e^C_{\hat{c}} + \Gamma^C_{AB} e^B_{\hat{c}}) = -\omega_{A\,\hat{c}\hat{b}}$ and $\Omega_A \equiv \frac{1}{4}\omega_{A\,\hat{b}\hat{c}}\Gamma^{\hat{b}}\Gamma^{\hat{c}}$ denote the torsion-free Levi-Civita spin connection, and let $\mathcal{A}_A(x)\,dx^A$ be a smooth abelian $\mathrm{U}(1)$ connection 1-form with curvature 2-form $\mathcal{F}_{AB} = \partial_A\mathcal{A}_B - \partial_B\mathcal{A}_A$ and coupling weight $q_e \in \mathbb{R}$. Define the first-order 5D Dirac--Kostant operator $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}$ on smooth 4-spinor sections $\Psi \in \Gamma^\infty(\mathcal{S}_4)$ by $D_A\Psi \equiv (\partial_A + \Omega_A - i q_e\mathcal{A}_A)\Psi$ and $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}\Psi \equiv i\,\Gamma^A(x)\,D_A\Psi$. Then all 25 spinorial tetrad postulates hold identically (\texttt{COR~\#067}), all first-order derivative terms in $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2 + \Delta_5^{\mathrm{spin}}$ cancel to $\mathbf{0}_4$, the Wick-rotated principal symbol is strictly positive-definite elliptic (\texttt{COR~\#068}), and the square of $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}$ satisfies the exact curved-space Bochner--Lichnerowicz--Weitzenb\"ock identity \cite{Lichnerowicz1963}:
\begin{align}
\nabla_A^{\mathrm{spin}}\Gamma^B &\equiv \partial_A\Gamma^B + \Gamma^B_{AC}\Gamma^C + \left[\Omega_A, \Gamma^B\right] \equiv \mathbf{0}_4 \qquad (\forall\,A, B \in \{1,\dots,5\}, \;\; \textnormal{\texttt{COR~\#067}}) \;\;\catD, \nonumber \\[4pt]
\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2\Psi &= \left(-\Delta_5^{\mathrm{spin}} + c_{R_5}\,R^{(5)}\,\mathbb{I}_4 + c_{\mathrm{Pauli}}\left(i\,q_e\,\mathcal{F}_{AB}\,\Gamma^A\Gamma^B\right)\right)\Psi \nonumber \\[3pt]
&= \left(-\Delta_5^{\mathrm{spin}} + \frac{1}{4}\,R^{(5)}\,\mathbb{I}_4 + \frac{i\,q_e}{2}\,\mathcal{F}_{AB}\,\Gamma^A\Gamma^B\right)\Psi \qquad \left(c_{R_5} = \frac{1}{4}, \;\; c_{\mathrm{Pauli}} = \frac{1}{2}, \;\; \textnormal{\texttt{COR~\#068}}\right) \;\;\catD, \nonumber \\[4pt]
\Delta_5^{\mathrm{spin}}\Psi &\equiv g^{AB}_{\mathrm{proj}}\left(D_A D_B\Psi - \Gamma^C_{AB}\,D_C\Psi\right), \qquad
\sigma_{\mathrm{Wick}}\!\left(\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2\right)(p_E) = \|p_E\|^2\,\mathbb{I}_4 > \mathbf{0}_4 \;\;\catD. \label{eq:c6_lichnerowicz_identity}
\end{align}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Using $\nabla_A^{\mathrm{spin}}\Gamma^B = \mathbf{0}_4$ and $\Gamma^A\Gamma^B = g^{AB}_{\mathrm{proj}}\mathbb{I}_4 + \frac{1}{2}[\Gamma^A, \Gamma^B]$, expanding $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2\Psi = -\Gamma^A D_A(\Gamma^B D_B\Psi) = -\Gamma^A\Gamma^B(D_A D_B - \Gamma^C_{AB}D_C)\Psi$ decomposes into the symmetric metric part $-g^{AB}_{\mathrm{proj}}(D_A D_B - \Gamma^C_{AB}D_C)\Psi = -\Delta_5^{\mathrm{spin}}\Psi$ plus the antisymmetric curvature commutator $-\frac{1}{4}[\Gamma^A, \Gamma^B][D_A, D_B]\Psi$. Substituting $[D_A, D_B]\Psi = \big(\frac{1}{4}R_{ABCD}^{(5)}\Gamma^C\Gamma^D - i q_e\mathcal{F}_{AB}\mathbb{I}_4\big)\Psi$ and contracting $\Gamma^A\Gamma^B\Gamma^C\Gamma^D R_{ABCD}^{(5)} = -2 R^{(5)}\mathbb{I}_4$ via the algebraic first Bianchi identity $R_{A[BCD]}^{(5)} = 0$ yields $-\frac{1}{8}(-2 R^{(5)})\mathbb{I}_4 + \frac{i q_e}{2}\mathcal{F}_{AB}\Gamma^A\Gamma^B = +\frac{1}{4}R^{(5)}\mathbb{I}_4 + \frac{i q_e}{2}\mathcal{F}_{AB}\Gamma^A\Gamma^B$, proving Eq.~\eqref{eq:c6_lichnerowicz_identity} identically on the curved non-diagonal 5-bein.
\end{proof}

\subsection{The Master Heat-Kernel Operator $\widehat{\mathcal{K}}_{\mathrm{JV}}(s) = \exp(-s\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2)$, Pure $\mathrm{Cl}(3,2)$ Gilkey Weights, Spectral Action Emergence of $\mathcal{I}_{3,2}$, and Cohomological Zero-Sum Closure (\texttt{THM~\#15, COR~\#069})}

\begin{theorem}[{Gilkey--Seeley--DeWitt Matrix Traces of $\widehat{\mathcal{K}}_{\mathrm{JV}}(s) = \exp(-s\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2)$, Pure $\mathrm{Cl}(3,2)$ Bivector Trace $-\frac{1}{2}$, Quadratic Gilkey Weights $(-\frac{7}{360}, -\frac{1}{45}, +\frac{1}{72})$, Spectral Emergence of $\mathcal{I}_{3,2}$ Under Axioms~4.1 \& 5.3, and Zero-Sum Closure --- \texttt{THM~\#15, COR~\#069} \catD}]\label{thm:c6_spectral_action_emergence}
Let $\widehat{\mathcal{K}}_{\mathrm{JV}}(s) \equiv \exp(-s\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2)$ ($s > 0$) be the Master Heat-Kernel Semigroup on $\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{Sp}(4)}$, and under Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_two} (\texttt{AXIOM\_2}), let $\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{spec}} \equiv \mathrm{Tr}\,f(\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2/\Lambda_{\mathrm{cut}}^2)$ be Chamseddine--Connes' spectral action functional~\cite{ChamseddineConnes1997} for any positive Laplace-transformable cutoff function $f(u) = \int_0^\infty \tilde{f}(\tau)e^{-\tau u}d\tau$ with spectral scale $\Lambda_{\mathrm{cut}} > 0$ and moments $f_m \equiv \int_0^\infty u^{m/2-1}f(u)\,du$. Writing $\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2 = -(\Delta_5^{\mathrm{spin}} + E_{\mathrm{endo}})$ with endomorphism $E_{\mathrm{endo}} = -\frac{1}{4}R^{(5)}\mathbb{I}_4 - \frac{iq_e}{2}\mathcal{F}_{AB}\Gamma^A\Gamma^B$ and bundle curvature $\Omega_{AB} = \frac{1}{4}R^{(5)}_{ABCD}\Gamma^C\Gamma^D - iq_e\mathcal{F}_{AB}\mathbb{I}_4$, the spinor matrix traces on $\mathbb{R}^4_{\mathrm{Sp}(4)}$ satisfy four exact identities (\texttt{COR~\#069}):
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
  \item \textbf{Exact Volume and Einstein--Hilbert Curvature Traces ($a_0 = 4$ and $a_1 = -\frac{1}{3}R^{(5)}$, \texttt{COR~\#069}):}
  \begin{align}
  a_0\!\left(\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2\right) &\equiv \mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{R}^4}(\mathbb{I}_4) = 4 \;\;\catD, \nonumber \\[4pt]
  a_1\!\left(\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2\right) &\equiv \mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{R}^4}\!\left(\frac{1}{6}R^{(5)}\mathbb{I}_4 + E_{\mathrm{endo}}\right) = 4\left(\frac{1}{6} - \frac{1}{4}\right)R^{(5)} = -\frac{1}{3}R^{(5)} \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c6_gilkey_a0_a1}
  \end{align}
  which upon integration over $\mathcal{M}_{3,2} = \mathcal{M}_4 \times_\Phi \mathcal{S}_{t_2}$ and Weyl rescaling $\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu} = e^\psi g_{\mu\nu}$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:c3_einstein_frame_reduction}) generates the exact gravitational-plus-scalar sector $\int_{\mathcal{M}_4} d^4x\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4}\left[\tilde{R}^{(4)} - \frac{3}{2}(\tilde{\nabla}\psi)^2\right]$ of $\mathcal{I}_{3,2}$.
  \item \textbf{Pure $\mathrm{Cl}(3,2)$ Bivector Trace $-\frac{1}{2}$, Exact Clifford--Gilkey Gauge Trace $a_2^{(\mathrm{gauge})} = +\frac{2}{3}q_e^2\mathcal{F}_{AB}\mathcal{F}^{AB}$, Universal EH-to-Maxwell Ratio $\frac{1}{2}$, and Quadratic Gilkey Weights (\texttt{COR~\#069}):}
  Because all $10$ unit $\mathrm{Cl}(3,2)$ spin bivectors $\Sigma^{\hat{a}\hat{b}} = \frac{1}{4}[\Gamma^{\hat{a}}, \Gamma^{\hat{b}}]$ satisfy the pure Clifford trace identity $\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{R}^4}(\Sigma^{\hat{a}\hat{b}}\Sigma_{\hat{a}\hat{b}}) = -\frac{1}{2}$, evaluating $E_{\mathcal{F}} = -\frac{iq_e}{2}\mathcal{F}_{AB}\Gamma^A\Gamma^B$ ($\mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{R}^4}(E_{\mathcal{F}}^2) = +2q_e^2\mathcal{F}_{AB}\mathcal{F}^{AB}$) and $\Omega_{\mathcal{F},AB} = -iq_e\mathcal{F}_{AB}\mathbb{I}_4$ ($\mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{R}^4}(\Omega_{\mathcal{F},AB}\Omega_{\mathcal{F}}^{AB}) = -4q_e^2\mathcal{F}_{AB}\mathcal{F}^{AB}$) in Gilkey's fourth-order heat coefficient yields:
  \begin{equation}\label{eq:c6_gilkey_a2}
  \begin{aligned}
  &a_2^{(\mathrm{gauge})}\bigl(\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2\bigr) \equiv \mathrm{Tr}_{\mathbb{R}^4}\!\left(\frac{1}{2}E_{\mathcal{F}}^2 + \frac{1}{12}\Omega_{\mathcal{F},AB}\Omega_{\mathcal{F}}^{AB}\right) \\
  &\qquad = \left(1 - \frac{1}{3}\right)q_e^2\,\mathcal{F}_{AB}\mathcal{F}^{AB} = +\frac{2}{3}\,q_e^2\,\mathcal{F}_{AB}\mathcal{F}^{AB} \;\; \catD, \\[4pt]
  &\frac{\bigl|a_1\bigl(\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2\bigr)/R^{(5)}\bigr|}{a_2^{(\mathrm{gauge})}\bigl(\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2\bigr)/(q_e^2\mathcal{F}^2)} = \frac{1/3}{2/3} = \frac{1}{2} \;\; \catD, \\[4pt]
  &a_2^{(\mathrm{grav})}\bigl(\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2\bigr) = -\frac{7}{360}\mathcal{K}_5 - \frac{1}{45}\mathcal{R}_2^{(5)} + \frac{1}{72}\bigl(R^{(5)}\bigr)^2 \;\; (\text{\texttt{COR~\#069}}) \;\catD.
  \end{aligned}
  \end{equation}
  Because $a_1 = -\frac{1}{3}R^{(5)}$ and $a_2^{(\mathrm{gauge})} = +\frac{2}{3}q_e^2\mathcal{F}_{AB}\mathcal{F}^{AB}$ carry strictly opposite algebraic signs, multiplying $-\mathrm{Tr}\,f(\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2/\Lambda_{\mathrm{cut}}^2)$ by a positive overall normalization simultaneously produces $+\tilde{R}^{(4)}$ (ghost-free graviton) and $-\frac{1}{4}F_{\mu\nu}F^{\mu\nu}$ (positive-energy Maxwell 2-form, conformally invariant at $d_{\mathrm{macro}} = 4$ by Theorem~\ref{thm:c2_curl_invariance}) \catD.
  \item \textbf{Spectral Emergence of the Effective Action $\mathcal{I}_{3,2}$ Under Axioms~4.1 and 5.3:}
  Combining the local Seeley--DeWitt expansion~\eqref{eq:c6_gilkey_a0_a1}--\eqref{eq:c6_gilkey_a2} with the non-perturbative Jacobi $\vartheta_3/\vartheta_4$ winding moments on $\mathcal{W}_3 = \mathrm{Span}\{e^{-2\psi}, e^{-4\psi}, e^{-6\psi}\}$ (regulator-shape-independent by Theorem~\ref{thm:c4_moments_and_potential}(i) under Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_two} \catD) and incorporating the equilibrium-root coupling $\Delta\psi = \psi - \psi_t(\mathcal{A})$ stipulated by Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_three} (with the Cauchy--Bernstein Gaussian kernel $\psi_t(\mathcal{A})$ of Theorem~\ref{thm:c4_moments_and_potential}(ii) \catD\ / \catH) yields at curvature scales $\nabla^2 \ll \Lambda_{\mathrm{cut}}^2$ (after rescaling the bare gauge connection $q_e\mathcal{A}_\mu \mapsto A_\mu$ so that $a_2^{(\mathrm{gauge})}$ provides the leading lowest-derivative kinetic term $-\frac{1}{4}F_{\mu\nu}F^{\mu\nu}$ of the $U(1)$ sector, while four-derivative gravitational curvature terms $a_2^{(\mathrm{grav})}$ are subleading relative to the two-derivative Einstein--Hilbert term $a_1 = -\frac{1}{3}R^{(5)}$ by $\mathcal{O}(\nabla^2/\Lambda_{\mathrm{cut}}^2)$):
  \begin{align}
  \mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{eff}}[\tilde{g}_{\mu\nu}, \psi, \Theta^{(1)}, A_\mu] &= \kappa_{\mathrm{spec}}\int_{\mathcal{M}_4} d^4x\sqrt{-\tilde{g}_4}\bigg[\tilde{R}^{(4)} - \frac{3}{2}(\tilde{\nabla}\psi)^2 - 2\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A}) \nonumber \\[3pt]
  &\quad - \frac{1}{4}F_{\mu\nu}F^{\mu\nu} + \mathcal{O}\!\left(\frac{\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{quad}}}{\Lambda_{\mathrm{cut}}^2}\right)\bigg] \;\;\catD\ / \;\catH, \label{eq:c6_spectral_action_emergence}
  \end{align}
  whose Lovelock--Gauss--Bonnet quadratic combination $\mathcal{G}_5$ is identically free of fourth-order scalar derivatives (Theorem~\ref{thm:c4_gb_seeley_dewitt_identities}) \catD.
  \item \textbf{Zero-Sum Variational Operator $\widehat{\bm{\Xi}}_{AB}[\mathcal{M}_{3,2}] = \mathbf{0}_5$ and 5-Level Operator Pyramid $[5, 17, 30, 150, 510]$:}
  Stationarity of the spectral action ($\delta\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{eff}}/\delta g_{\mathrm{proj}}^{AB} = 0$) enforces the five-dimensional zero-sum field equation:
  \begin{align}
  \widehat{\bm{\Xi}}_{AB}[\mathcal{M}_{3,2}] &\equiv G_{AB}^{(5)} - \mathcal{T}_{AB}^{(\mathrm{spec}+\mathrm{spin}+\mathrm{gauge})} = \mathbf{0}_5 \;\;\catD, \label{eq:c6_zero_sum_operator} \\[4pt]
  \left(\dim\mathbb{I}_5,\; \dim\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{17},\; \dim\mathbf{J}_{30},\; \dim\widehat{\bm{\Omega}}_{150},\; \dim\widehat{\bm{\Gamma}}_{510}\right) &= (5,\; 17,\; 30,\; 150,\; 510) \;\;\catD\ / \;\catH, \label{eq:c6_operator_pyramid_dims}
  \end{align}
  establishing cohomological exactness across all five levels: $\mathrm{ind}(2,3) = 0$ (Level~2), $\mathbf{E}^{(n)}\mathbf{P}^{(n)} = \mathbf{0}_{15\times 5}$ with $\dim\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{off\text{-}shell}}^{(n)} = 0$ and $\dim\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{on\text{-}shell}}^{(n)} = 5$ TT (Section~\ref{sec:c2_differential_operators}), $\widehat{\bm{\Xi}}_{AB}[\mathcal{M}_{3,2}] = \mathbf{0}_5$, and $\widehat{\Delta}_{\mathrm{LB}}\Psi_{\mathrm{Univ}} = 0$ ($p \equiv 1$, Level~4) \catD.
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}
