\section{\texorpdfstring{Axiomatic Architecture of CBG, Four-Tier Epistemological Taxonomy (\catA\ / \catD\ / \catH\ / \catN), Complete 24-Theorem DAG Audit, and Summary of Deductive Pillars}{Axiomatic Architecture of CBG, Four-Tier Epistemological Taxonomy ([A] / [D] / [H] / [N]), Complete 24-Theorem DAG Audit, and Summary of Deductive Pillars}}
\label{sec:c7_axiomatic_conclusions}

\subsection{Exact Definition of the Four-Tier Epistemological Taxonomy (\catA\ / \catD\ / \catH\ / \catN)}

To maintain absolute logical transparency between pure differential-geometric deductions and physical applications, every assertion in Covariant Bitemporal Geometrodynamics is classified into one of four mutually exclusive epistemological strata:
\begin{enumerate}[label=\arabic*.]
  \item \textbf{Foundational Mathematical Axioms (\catA\ --- 3 Explicit Postulates Injected into the DAG):}
  Within this foundational monograph (\textit{Principia Geometrica}), the entire deductive Directed Acyclic Graph rests on \emph{three explicit foundational axioms} (\catA):
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=1.1cm]
    \item \textbf{Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_one} (\texttt{AXIOM\_1} --- The 5D Bitemporal Orbifold Arena):} The smooth five-dimensional orbifold bundle $\mathcal{M}_{3,2} = (\mathbb{R}_{t_1} \times_\Phi S^1_{t_2}/\mathbb{Z}_2) \times \Sigma_3$ with metric signature $(-,-,+,+,+)$, secondary temporal fiber at index $2$, and $\mathbb{Z}_2$ reflection parity $-1$ (whose integer dimensions $(d_t, d_s, D) = (2, 3, 5)$ are uniquely selected among all positive integer pairs $(d_t, d_s)$ by the dimension-count equality $\mathrm{ind}(d_t, d_s) = 0$ and spatial Hodge--Lie self-duality $\dim\Lambda^2(\mathbb{R}^{d_s}) = d_s$ in Theorem~\ref{thm:c1_j30_diophantine} \catD).
    \item \textbf{Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_two} (\texttt{AXIOM\_2} --- Spectral Action Principle \& Orbifold Winding Sector Stipulation):} Generation of the effective action by the Wick-rotated spectral trace $\mathrm{Tr}\,f(\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2/\Lambda_{\mathrm{cut}}^2)$ for a cutoff function with non-vanishing spectral moments $f_{4-2k} \neq 0$ ($k \in \{0, 1, 2\}$), evaluating the $k \in \{0, 1\}$ power-law winding moments on the untwisted periodic $\mathbb{Z}_2$-even subbundle $\mathrm{Sym}^2_+$ and discarding the divergent untwisted $k = 2$ harmonic series so that at $k = 2$ ($\Phi^0$) only the antiperiodic Scherk--Schwarz twisted subbundle $\mathrm{Sym}^2_-$ contributes, yielding $0 < |\mathcal{M}_k| < \infty$ for $k \in \{0, 1, 2\}$ (which deductively gives $\mathcal{M}_2^{(-)} = \frac{\ln 2}{16\pi^2}\Phi^0$ under the sign convention of $[-\widetilde{\Theta}_{\mathrm{wind}}^{(-)}]$ in Theorem~\ref{thm:c4_moments_and_potential} \catD).
    \item \textbf{Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_three} (\texttt{AXIOM\_3} --- Killing Horizon Curvature Regularity \& Equilibrium-Root Coupling):} Any static geometry supporting a non-constant scalar profile $\psi(r) \neq \mathrm{const}$ across a Killing horizon $r_h > 0$ must maintain bounded 5D Kretschmann curvature $|\mathcal{K}_5(r_h)| < \infty$, with the primary temporal foliation norm $\mathcal{A}$ entering $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})$ through its equilibrium root $\psi_t(\mathcal{A})$ (which deductively forces $\left.\psi'(r_h)\right|_{\partial_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{V}=0} = 0 \implies \psi(r) \equiv 0$ when $\partial_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}} \equiv 0$, while for $\partial_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}} \neq 0$ it evades Bekenstein's global sign obstruction and admits the local near-horizon Frobenius expansion with finite non-zero slope $\psi'(r_h)$ and $\mathcal{C}_{B^2} + 1/b_{-1} \equiv 0$ in Theorem~\ref{thm:c5_static_radial_odes} \catD).
  \end{itemize}

  \item \textbf{Pure Mathematical Deductions (\catD\ --- 24 Theorems \& 94 Corollaries, 118 DAG Nodes Verified in \texttt{verify\_cbg\_paper0\_v7.py}):}
  Every matrix identity, trace-inversion supermetric inversion at $\lambda = 1/2$ ($c_{\mathrm{DW}}^{-1} = -1/3$), Clifford/Pfaffian isomorphism, Frobenius-orthonormal K\"ahler--Dirac family ($\mathbf{G}_{\mathrm{KD}} = \mathbb{I}_3$, $\mathrm{rank} = 3$), Cauchy--Vandermonde minor, connection component, linearised 5D Einstein KK cohomology (0 off-shell states and 5 TT on-shell states per level $n \ge 1$, and $7 - 4 = 3$ zero-mode states at $n = 0$), O'Neill curvature reduction ($c_{\mathrm{GC}}=-2$), throat indicial equation ($\alpha^2 + 2\alpha + \gamma_n^2 = 0$, $\beta(\beta-1) = \frac{3}{4} - \gamma_n^2$, limit-circle square-integrability for all $n \ge 1$), Euler--Maclaurin and inductive partial-sum proof of $\zeta_R(0) = -\frac{1}{2}$ and $\zeta'_R(0) = -\frac{1}{2}\ln(2\pi)$ (under analytic continuation to $\mathrm{Re}(s) > -1$), De Rham supertrace ($\mathrm{Str}=+1$), $\mathcal{W}_3$ Vandermonde jet basis ($\det\mathbf{M}_{\mathrm{jet}}^{\mathrm{raw}}=-16$, scaled $\det\mathbf{M}_{\mathrm{jet}} = -\frac{32}{3}$, uniquely determining $(c_0, c_1, c_2)$ from the input scales $(\mathcal{V}_0, m_\psi^2)$ under $\mathcal{V}'_{\mathrm{spec}}(0)=0$ with zero free structural parameters), Cauchy--Bernstein semigroup theorem ($F(u+v)=F(u)F(v) \implies \psi_0 e^{-\lambda_A\mathcal{A}^2}$), Radon--Plancherel $\mathcal{C}^3$ ball overlap ($\Delta f_{\mathrm{mut}}^{(4)}(1)=-30$), ODE boundary-value theorem, Bekenstein star-shaped domain $(-\infty, \frac{1}{2}\ln 3)$, Kottler--RN--dS electrovacuum solution ($f(r) = 1 - r_h/r + Q_e^2/(4r^2) - V_0 r^2$, $r_+ r_- = Q_e^2/4$), Andr\'eief finite-$L_0$ Gram positivity, and spectral trace deduced from Axioms~\ref{ax:axiom_one}--\ref{ax:axiom_three} (under the explicit regime conditions stated in each theorem: quasi-static limit in Theorem~\ref{thm:c5_sturm_liouville_perturbation}, radiation test fluid in Eq.~\eqref{eq:c5_minisuperspace_noether}, and short-distance harmonic throat regime $\rho \ll m_\psi^{-1}$ in Theorem~\ref{thm:c5_o4_instanton}) belongs strictly to class~\catD.

  \item \textbf{Coherent Physical Bridge and Constitutive Hypotheses (\catH\ --- Connecting Pure Geometry to Physical Observables):}
  Transitioning from the pure mathematics (\catA\ + \catD) of this monograph to physical predictions introduces \emph{zero free fitting parameters}, requiring instead three declared physical bridge/constitutive hypotheses (\catH):
  \begin{itemize}[leftmargin=1.1cm]
    \item \textbf{Bridge Hypothesis \catH$_1$ (Spectral Stationarity \& Observable Correspondence):} Physical states correspond to stationary configurations of the spectral action $\delta\,\mathrm{Tr}\,f(\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2/\Lambda_{\mathrm{cut}}^2) = 0 \Longleftrightarrow \widehat{\mathbf{\Xi}}_{AB}[\mathcal{M}_{3,2}] = \mathbf{0}_5$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:c6_spectral_action_emergence}), identifying $x^1 = c t_1$ with macroscopic causal time, the compact orbifold fiber $\mathcal{S}_{t_2}$ with internal quantum/chrono-informational phase (with $\mathbf{R}_{\mathbb{Z}_2}$ acting as discrete charge-parity conjugation), the conformally invariant 2-form $F_{\mu\nu}$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:c2_curl_invariance}) with the electromagnetic gauge field, and the rank-$3$ Frobenius-orthonormal K\"ahler--Dirac spatial-fiber operators / Robin boundary zero-modes of $\mathrm{Sp}(4,\mathbb{R})$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:c6_clifford_sp4_spinors}(iv)) with the $N_{\mathrm{gen}} = 3$ chiral fermion families.
    \item \textbf{Bridge Hypothesis \catH$_2$ (Metrological Unit Anchoring, Constitutive Thresholds \& 1-Loop Symplectic Vacuum Dressing $\Phi_\infty \leftrightarrow \alpha_{\mathrm{EM}}$):} Fixing human SI reference units via the universal conversion constants $(c, G_4, \hbar)$ of Remark~\ref{rem:c3_human_units_dictionary} ($G_4 \equiv \frac{G_5}{\pi L_0\Phi_\infty}$), imposing memoryless semigroup composition in $u = \mathcal{A}^2$ and identifying the constitutive threshold scales $(\psi_0, \lambda_A^{-1/2})$ of the deduced Cauchy--Bernstein Gaussian law $\psi_t(\mathcal{A})$ with the cross-order endomorphism ratios $(\psi_{\max} = \frac{2}{25}, A_{\mathrm{crit}} = \frac{1}{4})$ (Eq.~\ref{eq:c1_trace_ratios}) and bulk-configuration partition $\eta_{\mathrm{EM}} = \frac{15}{17}$ (Eq.~\ref{eq:c6_rational_68_invariant}), and identifying the dimensionless asymptotic vacuum aperture $\Phi_\infty$ of $\mathcal{S}_{t_2}$ with the collective electromagnetic coupling $\alpha_{\mathrm{EM}}$ partitioned equally across the $\mathcal{N}_{\mathrm{Cartan}} = \mathrm{Tr}(\mathbf{J}_{30}^T\mathbf{J}_{30}) = 30$ symplectic phase-space channels (Theorem~\ref{thm:c1_j30_diophantine}) and screened at one loop on $\mathcal{S}_{t_2}$ by the bulk-to-slice projector trace ratio $\gamma_{\mathrm{bulk/slice}} = \mathrm{Tr}(\mathbb{I}_5)/\mathrm{Tr}(\mathbf{P}_{\Sigma_3}) = 5/3$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:c1_tangent_traces}(iv)):
    \begin{equation}\label{eq:c7_symplectic_dressing_bridge}
    \Phi_\infty = \frac{\alpha_{\mathrm{EM}}}{\mathcal{N}_{\mathrm{Cartan}}}\left(1 - \gamma_{\mathrm{bulk/slice}}\,\alpha_{\mathrm{EM}}\right) = \frac{\alpha_{\mathrm{EM}}}{30}\left(1 - \frac{5}{3}\,\alpha_{\mathrm{EM}}\right) \qquad \textnormal{(Bridge Hypothesis \catH$_2$)}.
    \end{equation}
    While the rational coefficients $30$ and $5/3$, exact cosmic constancy $\Delta\alpha_{\mathrm{EM}}/\alpha_{\mathrm{EM}} \equiv 0$ ($\frac{\delta\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{curl}}}{\delta\psi} \equiv 0$, Theorem~\ref{thm:c2_curl_invariance}), and the dihedral GHY holonomy scaling $\exp(\Delta\ell_\partial^{(n)}) = \Phi_\infty^{2n}$ (Theorem~\ref{thm:c4_kretschmann_ghy_identities}(iii)) are proved theorems (\catD) of this monograph, anchoring $\Phi_\infty$ to the empirical fine-structure constant via Eq.~\eqref{eq:c7_symplectic_dressing_bridge} is transparently classified as~\catH$_2$.
    \item \textbf{Domain Constitutive Hypotheses \catH$_3$ (Macroscopic Matter Equations of State):} Whenever a specific macroscopic medium is modeled—such as pressureless baryonic dust ($\tilde{p}=0$) in cosmological structure growth, relativistic potential flow or macroscopically averaged $\mathcal{C}_\Omega$ chrono-helicity sources inside black-hole horizons, or thermal plasma distributions—that specific matter stress tensor $\tilde{T}_{\mu\nu}^{(\mathrm{matter})}$ is explicitly labeled~\catH\ so that phenomenological fluid models are never conflated with universal geometric theorems (\catD).
  \end{itemize}

  \item \textbf{Numerical Integration and Empirical Confrontation (\catN):}
  Substitution of measured CODATA constants ($\alpha_{\mathrm{EM}}, c, G_4, \hbar$) and direct shooting/collocation boundary-value integration (\texttt{DOP853}/\texttt{solve\_bvp}) of the parameter-free ODE systems of Section~\ref{sec:c5_symmetry_reductions} against observational datasets are classified as~\catN.
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{Summary of the Six Deductive Pillars}

With all six deductive stages completed, the mathematical architecture deduced from Axioms~\ref{ax:axiom_one}--\ref{ax:axiom_three} (\catA) is summarized in six machine-verified pillars (\catD):
\begin{enumerate}[label=\arabic*.]
  \item \textbf{Dimension-Count Equality ($30 = 30$), the $15\times 15$ Trace-Inversion Supermetric ($c_{\mathrm{DW}}^{-1}=-1/3$ at $\lambda = 1/2$), and Uniqueness of $(2, 3, 5)$ (Section~\ref{sec:c1_linear_algebra}, \texttt{THM~\#01--\#04, \#06--\#07}):} Single-time 4D geometry $(1,3,4)$ has dimension-count difference $\mathrm{ind}(1,3) = -8$, whereas $(2,3,5)$ is the unique positive integer root of $\mathrm{ind}(d_t, d_s) = 0$ and $\dim\Lambda^2(\mathbb{R}^{d_s}) = d_s$ (consistent with Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_one}), admitting an explicit invertible $30\times 30$ frame-dependent linear bijection $\bm{\Phi}_{30}$ ($|\det\bm{\Phi}_{30}|=1/64$), an inverse trace weight $c_{\mathrm{DW}}^{-1}=\frac{\lambda}{1-\lambda D}\big|_{\lambda=1/2}=-\frac{1}{D-2}=-\frac{1}{3}$, a 6D Klein Pfaffian quadric inducing $(3,2)$ on $\Lambda^2_0(\mathbb{R}^4)$, and a self-dual $S_3$ polar conic $Q = 2/3$ \catD.
  \item \textbf{Linearised 5D Einstein KK Cohomology, All-Orders Equivariant Ricci Parity, O'Neill Submersions, Throat Indicial Equation, and Weyl Invariance (Section~\ref{sec:c2_differential_operators}, \texttt{THM~\#08--\#11}):} Momentum-independent Stueckelberg determinant $|\det\mathbf{M}_{\mathrm{St\ddot{u}ck}}^{(n)}|=2\Phi_0^2(n/L_0)^5$ guarantees 0 off-shell states and 5 transverse-traceless on-shell states ($\omega^2 = |\mathbf{k}|^2 - n^2/(L_0\Phi_0)^2$) at each level $n \ge 1$, and $7 - 4 = 3$ physical zero-mode degrees of freedom at $n = 0$; $\mathrm{Ric}(\mathcal{P}_{t_2}^* g) = \mathcal{P}_{t_2}^*\mathrm{Ric}(g)$ enforces $R_{\mu 2}^{(5)} \equiv 0$ at all non-linear orders; O'Neill's identities yield $R^{(5)} = R^{(4)} - \frac{2}{\Phi}\Delta_4\Phi$ ($c_{\mathrm{GC}}=-2$) and $G^2_2 \equiv -\frac{1}{2}R^{(4)}$; $\Delta_5\Psi = 0$ on a linear throat yields the indicial equation $\alpha^2 + 2\alpha + \gamma_n^2 = 0$ and Liouville normal form $\beta(\beta - 1) = \frac{3}{4} - \gamma_n^2$ ($\beta_- + \beta_+ = 1$, Weyl limit-circle case for all $n \ge 1$); and $\Omega_c^{d_{\mathrm{macro}}-4}\big|_4 \equiv 1$ locks $\frac{\delta\mathcal{I}_{\mathrm{curl}}}{\delta\psi} \equiv 0$ \catD.
  \item \textbf{Constructive Curved Weyl Rescaling ($\kappa_\psi=-3/2$), Quasi-Static Yukawa Coupling ($\alpha_0^2 = 1/3 = -c_{\mathrm{DW}}^{-1}$ at $D = 5$), and General-$\mathcal{V}$ Bivariate Khronon Noether Identity (Section~\ref{sec:c3_master_action}, \texttt{THM~\#12--\#14 \& \#20}):} Non-diagonal curved Weyl rescaling yields $(c_{\mathrm{Weyl}}, c_{\mathrm{div}}, \kappa_\psi) = (1, 1, -3/2)$, 5D geodesics enforce the null-projection causal threshold $\Phi_{\mathrm{null}} = |P_2|/\epsilon_5$ and Routhian slopes $(+1/2, +3/2)$, quasi-static perturbation theory yields $\alpha_0^2(D) = \frac{2}{(D-2)(D-3)}\big|_{D=5} = \frac{1}{3} = -c_{\mathrm{DW}}^{-1}\big|_{\lambda=1/2, D=5}$, and Hilbert variation on the full-rank 6D 2-jet space $J^2(\mathbb{R}^2)$ yields $(c_V, c_{\mathrm{kh}}) = (-2, -2)$ and $\tilde{\nabla}_\mu\tilde{T}^\mu_{\;\nu} \equiv \frac{3}{2}\,\mathcal{E}_\psi\,\partial_\nu\psi + 2\,\mathcal{E}_\Theta\,\partial_\nu\Theta^{(1)}$ for any differentiable potential $\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}}(\psi, \mathcal{A})$ \catD.
  \item \textbf{McKean--Singer De Rham Supertrace ($\mathrm{Str}=+1$), Euler--Maclaurin Ray--Singer Torsion, Twisted Jacobi $\vartheta_2 \leftrightarrow \vartheta_4$ Duality, and Regulator-Independent $\mathcal{W}_3$ Potential (Section~\ref{sec:c4_spectral_geometry}, \texttt{THM~\#16--\#19}):} $\mathrm{Str}_{\Omega^\bullet}(e^{-s\Delta}) = b_0 - b_1 = +1$ deductively fixes $a_{1/2}^{(N,D)} = \pm\frac{1}{2}$ and $|\partial\mathcal{S}_{t_2}|=2$; the Euler--Maclaurin representation and inductive partial sums of $J(0)$ prove $-\zeta'_{\mathrm{orb}}(0) = \ln(2\pi L_0\Phi)$; \textbf{Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_two}} selects $\mathcal{M}_2^{(-)} = \frac{\ln 2}{16\pi^2}$ (under the sign convention of Eq.~\eqref{eq:c4_mellin_moments_values}); $\det\mathbf{M}_{\mathrm{jet}}^{\mathrm{raw}} = -16 \neq 0$ ($\det\mathbf{M}_{\mathrm{jet}} = -\frac{32}{3}$) guarantees regulator-shape independence ($\partial_{\mu_k}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{reg}} \equiv 0$) and uniquely fixes $(c_0, c_1, c_2)$ in terms of $(\mathcal{V}_0, m_\psi^2)$ under $\mathcal{V}'_{\mathrm{spec}}(0)=0$, the Cauchy--Bernstein Gaussian law $\psi_t(\mathcal{A})$ under the semigroup hypothesis \catH, and the $\mathcal{C}^3$ ball overlap $f(\lambda)$ ($\Delta f_{\mathrm{mut}}^{(4)}(1) = -30$) \catD.
  \item \textbf{Bekenstein Domain $(-\infty, \frac{1}{2}\ln 3)$, Horizon Regularity Under Axiom~5.3, Frobenius Pole Cancellation, Kottler--RN--dS Solution, 2D WDW Ordering $p \equiv 1$, and Andr\'eief Cauchy--Gram Loop Closure (Section~\ref{sec:c5_symmetry_reductions}, \texttt{THM~\#21--\#24}):} Exact factorization of $\Pi'_2(\psi)$ proves $\psi\,\Pi'_2(\psi)>0$ on $(-\infty, \frac{1}{2}\ln 3)\setminus\{0\}$ with barrier $\frac{1}{54}$; \textbf{Axiom~\ref{ax:axiom_three}} forces $\left.\psi'(r_h)\right|_{\partial_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{V}=0}=0$ and $\partial_{\mathcal{A}}\mathcal{V}_{\mathrm{spec}} \neq 0$; Eq.~\eqref{eq:c5_frobenius_pole_cancellation} cancels $\mathcal{C}_{B^2} + 1/b_{-1} \equiv 0$ symbolically with finite slope $\psi'(r_h)$; Eq.~\eqref{eq:c5_stiff_limit_ode} eliminates all stiff-limit horizon poles; Eq.~\eqref{eq:c5_kottler_solution} yields $f(r) = 1 - r_h/r + Q_e^2/(4r^2) - H_\infty^2 r^2$ ($r_+ r_- = Q_e^2/4 > 0$); $d_{\mathrm{mini}} = 2$ fixes $p \equiv 1$ with Raychaudhuri ratio $4\kappa_\psi=-6$; and Eqs.~\eqref{eq:c5_yukawa_overlap}--\eqref{eq:c5_cauchy_loop_closure} prove symbolic finite-$L_0$ Gram positivity via the Andr\'eief identity for all $L_0 > 0$ and distinct $\mu_a > 0$ and close the UV Gram determinant back to the Cauchy--Vandermonde minor $M_3$ of \texttt{THM~\#07} \catD.
  \item \textbf{Real $\mathrm{Cl}(3,2)$ Spinors, Curved 5-Bein Lichnerowicz--Weitzenb\"ock Identity, Spectral Emergence of $\mathcal{I}_{3,2}$, and the Operator Pyramid $[5, 17, 30, 150, 510]$ (Section~\ref{sec:c6_operator_pyramid}, \texttt{THM~\#05 \& \#15}):} The Master Heat Kernel $\widehat{\mathcal{K}}_{\mathrm{JV}}(s) = \exp(-s\,\widehat{\mathcal{D}}_{\mathrm{JV}}^2)$ unifies the gravitational ($a_1 = -\frac{1}{3}R^{(5)}$), gauge ($a_2^{(\mathrm{gauge})} = +\frac{2}{3}q_e^2\mathcal{F}^2$), quadratic Gilkey ($-\frac{7}{360}\mathcal{K}_5 - \frac{1}{45}\mathcal{R}_2^{(5)} + \frac{1}{72}(R^{(5)})^2$), spinor ($\mathrm{Sp}(4,\mathbb{R})$, KO-dimension $1$, rank-3 Frobenius-orthonormal K\"ahler--Dirac family $\mathbf{G}_{\mathrm{KD}} = \mathbb{I}_3$), and composite phase-space ($\widehat{\mathbf{\Omega}}_{150}, \widehat{\mathbf{\Gamma}}_{510}$) structures into a single cohomologically closed edifice \catD.
\end{enumerate}

The self-contained Python verification suite (\texttt{verify\_cbg\_paper0\_v7.py}) executing all 3 Axioms, 24 Theorems, and 94 Corollaries (118 composite DAG verification nodes, with explicit declaration of the regime conditions inside the corresponding nodes: quasi-static limit in \texttt{THM~\#14}, sign convention of Eq.~\eqref{eq:c4_mellin_moments_values} and uniqueness of analytic continuation for $\zeta_R$ in \texttt{THM~\#17}, semigroup hypothesis in \texttt{THM~\#18}, radiation test fluid in \texttt{THM~\#21}, and short-distance harmonic throat regime in \texttt{THM~\#23}) across Sections 1--6 with zero errors is archived alongside this manuscript.
